# QA issues — independent visual quality review (18 Nov 2026)

Independent art-director + QA review of every game at every level, done from screenshots of autoplayed levels. Nothing was fixed; this is the defect list for the builders. `candy` was skipped (still being built).

## How this was reviewed

- **Captures.** `tools/qa-shots.sh` (with the TELEPORT monitor) was run for all 22 games × 3 levels, 16 shots per level. Levels that did not finish at a 3.5 s interval were captured again at 7.5 s; four that still stopped early (myday L1/L3, snack L3, story L3) got a third run at 11 s. About 1,700 frames in total.
- **Review.** Every in-game frame was opened full size, and character–prop contact points (hands, feet, bellies, containers, edges, held items) were cropped and enlarged. Each defect was traced to the responsible code or model script so the fix is specific.
- **Audit cross-references.** Part 2 is grouped by world to sit beside `docs/storyboards/AUDIT-<world>.md`. An item marked `↔ AUDIT <ID>` is the same issue as that audit item; "Fix: as AUDIT <ID>" means the audit's fix covers it; "conflicts with AUDIT" means the two fixes disagree and one must be chosen. Most visual items (clipping, models, framing) have no audit counterpart.
- **Models.** All 392 files in `app/assets/models/index.json` were rendered six at a time in the dev viewer, normalised to one size, and judged one by one (Part 3).
- **Teleports.** Every TELEPORT log line was clustered into events (SNAP = one-frame jump, ZIP = several frames at 20+ units/s) and traced to the tween or assignment that causes it.

## Read this before acting on an item

- **The code moved while this ran.** Builders were editing games, the engine and models during the six hours of capture. Items whose capture pre-dates a later edit are marked "re-verify" in place, and each game's "Captures" line says which folders are affected. myday and snack L3 were re-checked against a 20:20–20:26 capture; those items say whether they are still present.
- **Fix numbers are starting points.** Positions, scales and camera values in the "Fix" text were derived from reading the code, not tested.
- **Items marked "from code, not seen in shots"** were proven by reading the code path, not caught in a frame. The autoplayer plays cleanly, so it does not reproduce stuck states caused by repeated or early taps.
- **The teleport monitor only tracks creatures.** Props that snap (blocks, cards, train cargo) are reported from the shots instead. In `where`, the regular SNAP pair each round is the invisible mini friend inside the thought bubble, not the real character.
- **Stray clicks.** A real mouse sometimes clicked in the capture window after a level ended, so some folders end in the Home map or another game. Those frames and their teleport lines were ignored.
- **Views not covered.** Model backs, undersides and animation pose sheets were not reviewed; the catalogue shows one three-quarter view at rest.

## Severity

- **P1** — must fix, visible to a parent: obvious clipping, teleports and zips, floating or senseless poses, placeholder-looking models in the play area, hidden or cut-off targets, broken deformation, effects that cover the action.
- **P2** — noticeable: minor intersections, weak framing, mediocre models, clutter, awkward spacing, confusing but playable.
- **P3** — polish.

## Counts

One item is one distinct defect. A defect seen at several levels is a single item tagged with those levels and takes its highest severity. Items that appear fixed in a later capture are listed under "Appears fixed — confirm" and not counted.

# Part 1 — What matters most

## Top 15 P1s

1. **myday, all levels — paws never go into the basin.** At wet and rinse the paws hang at rim height with pads to the camera, the spout is in front of them and the water stream collapses to a sliver; the wash pose reads as a head with two stretched tubes (`myday-L0` `shot03`, `shot15`). `myday.js` `PAWS.in`, `waterOnPaws()`, arm `reach: 1.2`.
2. **myday, all levels — tummy cut by the basin rim, and the "stuck then jumps behind the sink" path.** Bunny reads as waist-deep in the sink (`myday-L0` `shot01`, `shot13`); the walk to the stool squeezes through a gap narrower than its body, pauses and hops sideways behind the basin (`myday-L1-short` `shot09`, plus code). `myday-house.js` `SPOTS.sink`, `PATH_NODES.sinkR`.
3. **myday, all levels — the towels.** The hand towel is a rigid flat slab cut by the bottom of the frame; the towel wrap is a coloured box standing in the basin or over Bunny's mouth; the bath towel is a slab stuck to Bunny's front (`myday-L0-short` `shot07`, `shot08`; `myday-L0` `shot12`). `prop_myday.py` `towel()`, `towelwrap()`.
4. **myday, all levels — a second tap on the tissue freezes the game for good** (from code). `pick()` cancels the tween that `atchoo()` is awaiting and `tween.js` `stop()` never resolves it (`myday.js` l.917, l.632).
5. **myday L2/L3 — Bunny in bed.** The upright model is tipped 90° on top of the bedding, the duvet is a slab with star pegs, and Teddy stands in a T-pose on top of Bunny (`myday-L1` `shot01`; `myday-L2` `shot14`–`shot16`). `myday.js` `LIE`, `DUVET`, `cuddle()`.
6. **band, all levels — spoons float and instruments swap inside each other.** The wooden spoons hang in mid-air above the pots, held by nobody (`band-L1` `shot01`, `shot04`); at round change the old and new instruments and two beat boards sit inside each other (`band-L0` `shot10`); the front audience friend is cut in half by a hay bale or board post. `band-props.js` `pot()`, `band.js` `setInstruments()`.
7. **snack, all levels — hand-washing pose.** The frog's and pig's heads lie on the basin with the tap spout inside the cheek and the hands hidden (`snack-L1` `shot05`; `snack-L0` `shot09`); the bunny's paws stop at the back rim and never enter the sink (`snack-L0` `shot05`). After the 19:17–19:25 edits the paws still miss the basin (`qalong/snack-L2` `shot02`, `shot09`); the pig's head now clears it, frog and bunny are unconfirmed. `snack.js` `solveReach()` never tests the head.
8. **snack L3 — the towel.** A flat orange slab mostly below the frame whose chrome rail lifts off the cabinet with it; from code it hangs through the basin floor when drying (`snack-L2` `shot13`). `prop_kitchen.py` `towel()`, `snack.js` `doDry()`.
9. **cook, all levels — the knife chops in mid-air, held by nobody,** while Bear's arms stay behind the counter; chopped pieces interpenetrate and a broccoli slab stands through the blade (`cook-L0` `shot01`, `shot05`; `cook-L1-short` `shot10`). The pot handle also covers Bear's eye and nose in every pot view.
10. **share L2 — Pip is embedded in the barn wall** in every frame: head, wing, belly and a foot poke through the planks as separate blobs (`share-L1` `shot01`). `share-props.js` `makeSet` level 1.
11. **where — broken containers.** The pail deforms into a sealed lumpy dome with the crab invisible under "In!" (`where-L0` `shot01`); the umbrella's panels cut through the duck's neck and chest (`where-L1` `shot01`); the box's flap floats detached (`where-L1` `shot03`). Bucket and parasol were rebuilt after capture — re-verify.
12. **patterns L2/L3 — items spawn in a heap at the world origin, then snap.** Every L3 round starts with the row piled at the origin for about 1.6 s before snapping 6 units; the first item ends up inside its podium in rounds 3, 5 and 6 (`patterns-L2` `shot02`, `shot08`); the L2 train spawns at the origin each round (8–17 unit snaps). `patterns.js` `arrivePodiums()`, `landAt()`.
13. **"Look what you made!" plays over an empty scene** in story (all levels), bricks (all levels), blocks (all levels) and patterns L2/L3, because the creation is cleared before the finale photo (`story-L0` `shot11`; `bricks-L0` `shot07`; `blocks-L2` `shot11`; `patterns-L1` `shot09`). No `creationView()` / `creationObjects()` in those lessons.
14. **pots — Pip stands inside the pen fence** with pickets through his tummy all through Level 2 (`pots-L1` `shot01`), and at every level each found friend zips 6–11 units to Pip in 0.9 s and then covers Pip's face. `pots-props.js` `makeFence(15)`, `pots.js` `jumpToPip()`.
15. **feelings, all levels — the chosen friend flies about 3 units in 0.1 s up to Pip's stage** every round (11 logged ZIPs), and Pip's story props (gift, ice cream, cupcake) hang in the air beside him (`feelings-L0` `shot01`, `shot02`, `shot06`). `feelings.js` `tapFriend`, `feelings-stories.js` prop anchors.

Close behind: the sink game's key and marble landing on the sand outside the tubs; body's swim ring going through the towel when the bear bows; dress garments unreadable on the rail and the monkey's body poking through its shorts; colours L3 buckets passing through each other during the shuffle.

## Problems that repeat across games

These are worth fixing once, in the engine or pipeline, instead of game by game.

- **Hand-at-a-station poses do not work** (myday, snack, cook). Arms are too short and heads too big for the reach, so paws stop short of the basin, heads land on it, and tools are not held. This is the parent's complaint and it is present in all three kitchen and bathroom games.
- **Finale photo of an empty scene** (story, bricks, blocks, patterns): the lesson clears the creation before the engine's "Look what you made!".
- **Characters zip instead of walking**: Pip's `celebrate()` cartwheel in `lesson.js` (about 5 units in 0.15 s), `feelings.js` `tapFriend`, `pots.js` `jumpToPip()`, `where.js` `gagUnder`, `sizes.js` drop arrivals, `story.js` `flyTo()`, myday's leap into the bath.
- **Things spawn at the world origin before being placed** (patterns rows and train, blocks' new pieces): visible heap, then a snap.
- **Held items are not held**: knife, ladle, cup, spade, lolly, apple, flag pole, wooden spoons, Teddy.
- **Flat slabs standing in for cloth**: every towel, the duvet, blankets, rugs, curtains.
- **Ragged paint from `lib.py`**: sawtooth shards where stripes cross coarse triangles, scribbled ink loops around stickers and highlights, dotted outlines on thin white parts. This accounts for most of the 101 touch-up models.
- **Ear springs collapse** on cat and bunny (`app/src/engine/creature.js`), and the bunny's outline breaks up on white fur.
- **Leftover code primitives** in the play area: discs for puddles, ponds and rugs, cylinder-and-cone lamps, box walls and floors, sphere foam and bubbles (listed in Part 3 A).
- **UI over the action**: the "Go!" badge, the emoji hint hand, the caption bubble and the sticker badge regularly cover the target or a face; clouds drift across the sun's face; scenery (snowman, pumpkins, trees) is cut by the frame edge or sits behind Pip.
- **Friends and visitors parked in front of the target** (colours, sizes, share, band, pots).

# Part 2 — Defects by world and game

Within each game: P1 first, then P2, then P3. Level tags are the app's L1–L3 (capture folders `-L0` to `-L2`). Shot paths are relative to the capture folder; `-short` is the 3.5 s capture and `qalong/` the 11 s one.

## Meadow world

### colours — Sort colours
Captures: `colours-L0`, `colours-L1`, `colours-L2`. `colours-L1` and `colours-L2` pre-date the 16:53:31 `lesson.js` change (old loud success: confetti, praise word, `celebrate()` dance; current code runs it only with `cheer: true`) — items marked re-verify. `colours-L0` contact sheet is stale; shots were used.

1. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `colours-L1/shot08`, `colours-L2/shot08`, `colours-L0/shot05` — the visitor stands in front of the right-most container: elephant covers half the green basket (one eye) during "Green! Put it with green."; pig covers half the red bucket in the red round; frog hides the yellow tube's lower-right third; also hedgehog over yellow basket (`colours-L1/shot04`), cat over green bucket (`colours-L2/shot04`). **Fix:** as AUDIT C5 (friend watches from the side, x=6.8 z=1.5); raw alternative `colours.js` `FRIEND_AT` (3.6, 0, 3.6) → about (6.2, 0, 4.2) or enter on the left (right-most container is x=4 / 4.2 in `LAYOUT[3]` / `LAYOUT[4]`); also scale the friend 0.6 in the 4-container layout. ↔ AUDIT C5
2. **[P1]** L2 · between `colours-L1/shot01`/`shot02` and `shot07`/`shot08`, `colours-L1/shot09` — engine: Pip zips twice (4.83 units in 152 ms, 5.18 in 166 ms); shot09 catches him mid-spin, sideways. (re-verify: pre-16:53 capture) **Fix:** `app/src/engine/lesson.js` `celebrate()` cartwheel branch (`m === 3`) — remove it, or run it over 1.2 s with radius `c = 0.6`.
3. **[P1]** L3 · `colours-L2/shot06` — during the shuffle the buckets pass through each other (red wall cuts into blue, blue handle disappears into red, green and yellow touch) while the prompt is already showing. **Fix:** `colours.js` `startRound` `R.shuffle` / `placeTubes` — alternate buckets on a z arc (back z=-2.6, front z=+0.4) during the 0.8 s tween; prompt only after it finishes (as AUDIT C10). ↔ AUDIT C10
4. **[P2]** L2 · `colours-L1/shot01–10` — baskets are about 18% of screen height; the lower half of the frame is empty mat. **Fix:** `colours.js` `build` `frame(0, 3.2, 12.6, 0, 1.9, 0)` — for the basket style about `frame(0, 2.6, 10.2, 0, 1.2, 0.5)`.
5. **[P2]** L3 · `colours-L2/shot03–09` — sorted items vanish inside the buckets, so "So much green!" has nothing to show. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/colours-items.js` `MODEL.bucket.floor` (1.15) — raise to about 1.55.
6. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `colours-L1/shot02`, `colours-L2/shot02` — the "gulp" squashes the container so far that its eyes become slits; the basket rim flares like a hat, the bucket body squashes under an unsquashed handle. **Fix:** `colours.js` `payoff` 'gulp' (`b.scale.set(1 + k*0.14, 1 - k*0.16, …)`) — halve it for basket/bucket styles, or parent the face to an un-squashed group.
7. **[P2]** L2 · `colours-L1/shot02`, `colours-L1/shot10` — confetti, a floating heart and the falling leaves all run at once; in shot10 the sticker glow is added on top. (re-verify: pre-16:53 capture) **Fix:** `colours.js` `build` — pause the leaf weather inside the play box; one effect per success (heart float removed by AUDIT C6). ↔ AUDIT C6
8. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `colours-L0/shot01–08`, `colours-L1/shot05`, `colours-L2/shot06` — a thin straight hairline crosses the top of every container's smile: the open-mouth sphere squashed to y=0.001 but still drawn. **Fix:** `colours-items.js` `makeTube` (`mouth.scale.set(1, 0.001, 0.25)`) — `mouth.visible = false` at rest; toggle it where `colours.js` scales the mouth (`u.mouth.scale.set(...)`).
9. **[P2]** L2 · `colours-L1/shot01–10` — baskets have scratchy, broken black ink along every weave ridge and no outline at the base. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_meadow_pots.py` (`prop_m_cbasket`) — smooth the ridges, one clean outline hull.
10. **[P2]** L3 · `colours-L2/shot04` — the yellow "paintpot" is unrecognisable (reads as a pencil sharpener). **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_meadow_toys.py` `prop_m_paintpot` — remodel as a round pot with a paint-drip lip and a brush.
11. **[P2]** L3 · `colours-L2/shot05` — the toy car has flat black blob wheels with no hubs and a torn white smear for a windscreen. **Fix:** `prop_meadow_toys.py` `prop_m_car` — add hubcaps and a clean window layer.
12. **[P2]** L3 · `colours-L2/shot01`, `colours-L2/shot02` — the green block's white star and blob decals are ragged smears with saw-tooth ridges on the edges. **Fix:** `prop_meadow_toys.py` (`prop_m_block`) — clean `layer()` stickers.
13. **[P2]** L1 · `colours-L0/shot03` — the bunny's ink outline breaks up: missing along the left of the body, black fragments at armpits and legs, white body melting into the cream mat. **Fix:** bunny creature script in `tools/blender/creatures/` (outline hull thickness on torso); or tint `makeMat` in `colours.js` darker than white fur.
14. **[P2]** L3 · `colours-L2/shot01–09` — Pip stands in front of the snowman (hat pompom merges into its body) and the snowman is cut by the left frame edge. **Fix:** `colours.js` `calmBackdrop` `hide` — hide the snowman; `isTree` does not catch it.
15. **[P3]** L1–L2 · `colours-L0/shot04`, `colours-L0/shot05`, `colours-L1/shot03–10` — sorted items show above the rim as flat, outline-less domes in the container's own colour (red ball; apple and plums, no outline between the two plums), reading as a lump on the lid. **Fix:** `colours-items.js` `MODEL.tube.floor` (2.45) → about 2.2; darken the inside disc (`shade(c, 0.5)` → 0.35).
16. **[P3]** L1 · `colours-L0/shot01` — the 👆 hint hand sits on the red tube's face, covering its mouth. **Fix:** `colours.js` `showHint` — end the ghost hand at `mouthY + 0.2` (the rim) instead of `mouthY - 0.4`.
17. **[P3]** L1 · `colours-L0/shot01`, `colours-L0/shot02` — the white stripe on the red ball and the white panel on the beach ball have ragged, torn edges. **Fix:** `prop_meadow_toys.py` (`prop_m_toyball` band, `prop_m_beachball`) — repaint as clean `layer()` shapes.
18. **[P3]** L1–L3 · `colours-L0/shot02`, `colours-L1/shot03`, `colours-L2/shot04` — engine: a cloud drifts across the sun's face (also `colours-L0/shot03`, `colours-L1/shot04`, `colours-L2/shot03`, `shot05`). **Fix:** `app/src/engine/stage.js` cloud lanes — keep cloud y/x clear of the sun disc.
19. **[P3]** L1 · `colours-L0/shot01–08` — the tubes have no contact shadow or base outline on the mat, and a grass blade grows through Pip's feet. **Fix:** `colours.js` `build` — blob shadow under each tube; extend the `calmBackdrop` hide box to cover grass at Pip's spot (-7.9, -3.6).
20. **[P3]** L2 · `colours-L1/shot01–10` — a pumpkin is cut in half by the left frame edge and a mushroom pokes out from behind Pip's foot. **Fix:** `colours.js` `calmBackdrop` `hide` — also hide scenery with x < -8.5.
21. **[P3]** L3 · `colours-L2/shot07`, `colours-L2/shot09` — a black blob (the car's wheel) sits at the blue bucket's rim and reads as a blotch. **Fix:** goes away with #5 and #11.
22. **[P3]** L3 · `colours-L2/shot04`, `colours-L2/shot08` — dark crease lines at the cat's and pig's raised armpits. **Fix:** cat/pig creature scripts — shoulder weights; or limit the wave to 1.2 rad in `colours.js` `friendThrow`.
23. **[P3]** L3 · `colours-L2/shot10` — "Look! So much green!" is still on screen after the Home map has loaded. **Fix:** `colours.js` `onFinale` — guard the delayed `say` at 1.4 s with `if (this.dead) return`.

### counting — Apple count
Captures: `counting-L0`, `counting-L1`, `counting-L2`. `counting-L1` and `counting-L2` pre-date the 16:53:31 `lesson.js` change — items marked re-verify. `counting-L0` contact sheet is stale; shots were used. The white glove at the right edge of `counting-L2` shots 06–09 is the app's `#cursor`, not a defect.

1. **[P1]** L1 · `counting-L0/shot05` — in the picnic view the hedgehog is cut off flat at the belly; feet and lower body are below the grass. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/counting.js` `HOME` (3.9, 0, 2.7) with `CAMS.blanket` — cause not found in code: check ground height at HOME and whether the 'worm' payoff or `prop_m_blanket` in `counting-hosts.js` lowers the eater; snap `hog.position.y` to the ground.
2. **[P1]** L2–L3 · `counting-L1/shot03`, after `counting-L2/shot12` — engine: Pip is upside-down in mid-air in front of the autumn tree, mid-cartwheel (L2); Pip jumps 0.36 units in one frame (L3; cause not proven from code, most likely the `celebrate()` cartwheel or hop peak on a slow frame). (re-verify: pre-16:53 capture; confirm with a re-capture) **Fix:** `lesson.js` `celebrate()` — as colours #2.

### shapes — Find shapes
Captures: `shapes-L0`, `shapes-L1`, `shapes-L2`. Part of `shapes-L0` pre-dates the 16:53:31 `lesson.js` change — item marked re-verify.

1. **[P1]** L1 · `shapes-L0/shot01–12` — the cottage roof, the largest object on screen, has a V-shaped notch in the eave right of centre where sky shows through and is covered in irregular saw-tooth "tile" marks; the chimney bricks are the same noise. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_meadow_*.py` (`prop_m_cottage`, `prop_m_chimney`; loaded in `app/src/lessons/shapes-themes.js`) — straight eave, three or four clean scalloped tile rows as a `layer()`.
2. **[P1]** L2–L3 · `shapes-L1/shot05–13`, `shapes-L2/shot07–13` — in the 4-window layout the outer window hangs off the building: the circle window, its party hat and ledge hang off the left end of the carriage over open air (L2); after the shuffle the rectangle window overhangs the wall's left edge onto the tower (L3). **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/shapes.js` `LAYOUT[4]` [-3.6, -1.2, 1.2, 3.6] → train [-3.0, -1.0, 1.0, 3.0], window scale 0.85, drop the `theme === 'train'` -0.4 offset; castle [-3.1, -1.05, 1.05, 3.1], rect window 0.85.
3. **[P1]** L3 · `shapes-L2/shot10` — the "spin" payoff rotates only the rectangle window's frame 45° into a diamond while its yellow pane, bow and ledge stay put; reads as a broken mesh. **Fix:** as AUDIT S2 (delete `windowPayoff`); if kept, `shapes.js` line ~463 `hole.userData.rim.rotation.z = e * Math.PI * 2` — spin the whole `hole` group, or use 'bounce'/'flash' for non-circle kinds. ↔ AUDIT S2
4. **[P1]** L1, L3 · `shapes-L2/shot12`, `shapes-L2/shot13`, `shapes-L0/shot12` — in the finale a piece lands on the bunny's head: the pink rectangle (blue one behind it) hides the ears and part of the face (L3); the red circle hides the ears (L1). **Fix:** as AUDIT S4 (pieces stay seated, no spit-out); if the tumble is kept, `shapes.js` `onFinale` (`tx = clamp(w.x, -2.9, 3.4) + random*1.2`) — clamp `tx` to [-1.6, 2.6] or add a static collider above each side friend. ↔ AUDIT S4
5. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `shapes-L0/shot12`, `shapes-L1/shot12`, `shapes-L2/shot12` — the finale line-up is messy: pieces hide behind each other (purple circle behind green square, blue triangle behind red square), the orange triangle lies on its side intersecting the green square, the red square's face is upside-down (reads as sad), the orange circle's sideways (also `shapes-L1/shot13`, `shapes-L2/shot13`). **Fix:** as AUDIT S4; if kept, `onFinale` — after settling tween each piece upright (`inner.rotation.z → 0`) and spread to fixed slots 1.6 apart. ↔ AUDIT S4
6. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `shapes-L0/shot11`, `shapes-L1/shot12`, `shapes-L2/shot11–13` — the bear is cut off by the right frame edge (including inside the "photo" frame, L3); in L2 the bunny stands on top of a train wheel. **Fix:** as AUDIT S4 (Bunny and Bear pop-ins removed); if kept, `shapes-themes.js` `hooray` friend positions — |x| ≤ 5.6. ↔ AUDIT S4
7. **[P2]** L1–L2 · `shapes-L0/shot07`, `shapes-L1/shot01–13` — Pip is pressed against the left frame edge with his wing cut (15 px from the edge at rest in L1); his celebration cartwheel takes him half out of frame. ### feelings — How does Pip feel?
Captures: `feelings-L0`, `feelings-L1`, `feelings-L2` (all shots opened full-size) plus `feelings-L0-short`, `feelings-L2-short` (`feelings-*-short` frames reviewed as 4x4 montages plus crops). No stale-capture caveat.

1. **[P1]** L1–L3 · after `feelings-L0/shot01`, after `feelings-L1/shot07`, `feelings-L2/shot07` — the chosen friend flies about 3 units in 0.1 s from the grass up to Pip's stage: 4 ZIPs in L1 (after shot01, 02, 03, 06), 5 in L2, the sheep twice in L3 (shot07 shows it mid-air). **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/feelings.js` `tapFriend` (`s.anim(0.9, … lerpVectors(from, to, e) … += sin(p·π)·1.6 …, Ease.inOut)`) — walk to the foot of the stage at about 2.5 units/s, then one 0.6 s hop up; linear x, not inOut.
2. **[P1]** L1 · `feelings-L0/shot01`, `feelings-L0/shot02`, `feelings-L0/shot06` — Pip's story props hang in the air beside him: gift box and star (01), ice-cream cone (02), cupcake (06, also `shot07`). **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/feelings-stories.js` prop anchors — rest props on the blanket at `TOP`, or put them in Pip's wing.
3. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `feelings-L0/shot01`, `feelings-L1/shot06`, `feelings-L2/shot01` — Pip's face, the one the child must read, is the smallest on screen; the friends' heads are about three times larger; in L3 the middle friend also stands in front of the stump, hiding its base. **Fix:** `feelings.js` `pip.scale.setScalar(1.2)` → 1.6 with `HEAD_S` on Pip; friends' `ss` → 1.0.
4. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `feelings-L0/shot03`, `feelings-L1/shot02`, `feelings-L2/shot04` — for long stretches only Pip (about 26% of screen height) stands on the stage with the lower 40% of the frame empty and often no caption (also `feelings-L0/shot04–06`, `feelings-L1/shot03–05`, `feelings-L2/shot05`). **Fix:** `feelings.js` `frame(0, 4.7, 13.2, 0, 2.6, 0)` — during the story beat glide to about `frame(0, 4.2, 9.5, 0, 3.0, 0)`, back out when the friends enter. ↔ AUDIT F7
5. **[P2]** L1 · `feelings-L0-short/shot14`, `feelings-L0-short/shot05` — on the stage the bear stands in front of Pip and covers half of him; the hugging cat merges into Pip. **Fix:** `feelings.js` `to = (PIP_POS.x - 2.05, TOP, PIP_POS.z + 1.0)` — x -2.6, z +0.2; hug lean 0.25 → 0.15.
6. **[P2]** L2 · `feelings-L1/shot07` — the right-hand bunny arrives enormous (head about 300 px) and half off the right frame edge. **Fix:** `feelings.js` friend entrance (`x0 = x1 ± 2.5` with `popIn`) — start at x1 ± 1.2 and pop to scale S without overshoot.
7. **[P2]** L1 · `feelings-L0/shot01`, `feelings-L0/shot02` — side friends and props are cut by the frame: a second gift box is half off the right edge (01), the bear's ear and arm are cut (02). **Fix:** `feelings.js` `xs = two ? [-4.4, 4.4] …`, `ss` 1.22 — xs ±3.9, scale 1.1.

### sizes — Big and small
Captures: `sizes-L0` (shots 01–14), `sizes-L1` (01–13), `sizes-L2` (01–12, 14); later shots are the Home screen. Captures (17:21–17:33) are newer than every game/engine file involved, so nothing is stale. The white glove at a frame edge is the app cursor, ignored.

1. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `sizes-L2/shot06`, `sizes-L0/shot04`, `sizes-L0/shot12` — the visitor stops directly in front of Pip and covers his body in every level (also `sizes-L0/shot07`, `shot08`, `shot13`, `shot14`; `sizes-L1/shot03`, `shot04`, `shot09`; `sizes-L2/shot10`, `shot11`, `shot12`, `shot14`). L3: the frog's face is half hidden behind the big mushroom's cap. L1: the big kitty's paw overlaps the hedgehog visitor, three characters stacked in a 200 px column. L2: the cheer heart sits on the kitty's head against Pip's cheek. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/sizes.js` `spawnVisitor()` (ends x = -6.6, z = -2.6, on Pip's sight line) — Pip to about (-9.6, 0, -4.2), visitor stop x ≈ -7.3, z ≈ -3.4; three-thing `xs` [-3.8, 0.4, 4.6] → about [-3.0, 1.0, 5.0].
2. **[P1]** L1 · `sizes-L0/shot04`→`shot05` (bears), `sizes-L0/shot08`→`shot09` (frogs) — animals in "drop" rounds plummet from 13 units up in 0.8 s with an accelerating ease (peak ≈ 49 units/s), slamming out of the sky faster than the eye can follow, then squash; no shot catches them in the air. **Fix:** `sizes.js` `arrive()` `mode === 'drop'` — `t.position.y = 13` → about 5, duration 0.8 → 1.1, `Ease.in` → gentle ease with a small bounce; or walk/peek for animals and "drop" only for objects (pumpkin, melon, drum). AUDIT Z6 keeps only walk and peek arrivals. ↔ AUDIT Z6
3. **[P1]** L2 · `sizes-L1/shot05` — the small present lies on its side with lid and bow hanging off the left end; it toppled during the physics "sky" arrival and is then praised as "Small present!". **Fix:** `sizes.js` `arrive()` `'sky'` — box things: locked rotation in `physics.toss` (`rot: [0, 0, 0]` / `planar`), or the tweened `'drop'` for `gift`; snap `rotation` upright after settling, before enabling taps. AUDIT Z6 keeps only walk and peek arrivals. ↔ AUDIT Z6
4. **[P1]** L2 · `sizes-L1/shot10`, `sizes-L1/shot11`, `sizes-L1/shot12` — beach balls (`prop_m_beachball`), the whole round: ragged torn-paper panel edges, stray ink scratches along the seams, a white splatter "highlight" that looks like a stain (also `shot13`). **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_meadow_toys.py` `beachball()` — clean lune panels on the colour border (crisp `paint()`), no interior ink, one rounded shine.
5. **[P1]** L2 · `sizes-L1/shot01`, `sizes-L1/shot02` — toy ball (`prop_m_toyball`), the only object on screen in round 1: torn, wobbly white band with loose black ink scratches beside it, misshapen blob shine, star only half outlined. **Fix:** `prop_meadow_toys.py` `toyball()` — the `band` layer (voxel 0.008 remesh of a thin lathe) gives the ragged edge: paint the band with `paint_fn` or raise it as a clean ring with outline off; `shine` as a rounded capsule sticker; `star` outline all round or none.
6. **[P1]** L3 · `sizes-L2/shot12` (compare `sizes-L2/shot11`) — in the finale hop the big carrot's leaf tuft collapses into a flat propeller-shaped disc with a swirl artefact, and its soil mound flies up with it. **Fix:** `sizes.js` `jump()` / `onFinale()` / `smallPayoff()` (`k === 'carrot'`) — hop only `t.userData.carrot`, never the group owning mound and shadow; keep `hop()`'s squash off the tuft (`userData.noSquash`, or position-only hop).
7. **[P2]** L1 · `sizes-L0/shot01–14` — Pip's lower-left body is hidden behind the game's own stage bush for the whole level (x≈0–140 px, y≈400–515). **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/sizes-things.js` `makeStage()` level-0 list, first entry `[-9, -2.8, 1.1]` — move to about `[-10.8, -3.6, 1.0]` or delete it (Pip is at (-8.3, 0, -4.6) in `sizes.js` `build()`).
8. **[P2]** L3 · `sizes-L2/shot01–14` — the picket fence renders pure white on white snow, a band of thin ink scribbles, and stands in front of Pip (fence z = −3, Pip z = −4.6), caging his body in every frame; the tint `0xd9b07a` passed in code is not visible. **Fix:** `sizes-things.js` `makeStage()` `level === 2` — make the tint take effect (check `propModel('prop_fence', { tint })`) or use a wood colour that survives the winter light; move the fence to z ≈ -5.4 or stop it at x > -6.5.
9. **[P2]** L1 · `sizes-L0/shot11`, `sizes-L0/shot03` — the peekaboo bushes are so large (up to 1.28×) that the left one completely covers Pip and all three merge with the scenery bushes into one green wall; nothing says where to look. **Fix:** `sizes.js` `arrive()` `'peek'` — `makeBush(0.6 + size * 0.5)` → `makeBush(0.45 + size * 0.4)`; hide the stage bushes (`this.stage.visible = false`) while the peek bushes are up.
10. **[P2]** L2 · `sizes-L1/shot10`, `sizes-L1/shot11`, `sizes-L1/shot13` — the beach balls do not stay on their line: the small yellow one rolls off the rug to the toy box and touches it, the big red one overlaps Pip's wing; the three-on-one-ground-line comparison is lost. **Fix:** `sizes.js` `arrive()` `'sky'` ball opts — rolling friction/damping or a planar constraint, re-centre on `userData.slotX` after settling; three-thing `xs[0]` -3.8 → -3.0. AUDIT Z6 keeps only walk and peek arrivals. ↔ AUDIT Z6
11. **[P2]** L3 · `sizes-L2/shot10` — the white bunny visitor on white snow in front of the white fence and Pip is nearly invisible apart from ears and face. **Fix:** `sizes.js` `PLANS[2]` — a coloured visitor on the snow level (bear/cat), plus #1.
12. **[P2]** L3 · `sizes-L2/shot08` — after the swap the big melon covers half of Pip and the fence; it has a stray ink squiggle top-left and no outline along the bottom. **Fix:** `sizes.js` three-thing `xs[0]` -3.8 → -3.0; `tools/blender/creatures/prop_meadow_garden.py` `watermelon()` — remove the stray ink, close the outline.
13. **[P2]** L1, L3 · `sizes-L0/shot02`, `sizes-L2/shot04` — the stomp "dust" is six flat-white translucent spheres (`MeshBasicMaterial`, r 0.28 growing to 2.5×) sitting on the big elephant's feet like glass domes, hiding the toes; on snow they are invisible except over the big pumpkin, whose lower third looks see-through. **Fix:** as AUDIT Z3 (delete `puffs`); if kept, `sizes.js` `puffs()` — start outside the feet (`0.8 * s` → `1.15 * s`), radius 0.28 → 0.16, growth `1.5 * s` → 0.8, opacity 0.9 → 0.55, life 0.8 → 0.5 s, soft round sprite. ↔ AUDIT Z3
14. **[P2]** L2 · `sizes-L1/shot01–13` — the "toy room" is the autumn meadow with a rug of eight flat untextured box strips (`geo.box(2.4, 0.03, 10)`): hard rectangular edges, no border or fringe, back edge cutting straight across the grass; reads as a placeholder beside the sculpted toy box. **Fix:** `sizes-things.js` `makeStage()` `level === 1` — one rounded rug model with a border (e.g. the existing `makeMat` style or a Blender rug), ending behind the things.
15. **[P2]** L2 · `sizes-L1/shot03`, `sizes-L1/shot04`, `sizes-L1/shot08` — toy blocks (`prop_m_block`): furry-edged star stickers, side-face stickers cut off at the block edge, small dark triangles in the corners; on the small scatter blocks the stickers collapse into blobs (also `shot09`). **Fix:** `prop_meadow_toys.py` `block()` — crisp sticker layer with its own outline, inset to clear the bevel; hide stickers at scatter size (`makeScatter('blocks')` in `sizes-things.js`: `hide: ['star', ...]`).
16. **[P2]** L2 · `sizes-L1/shot05` — big present: the lid sits off-centre (a second box edge shows on the left) and the box ribbon is a thin wobbly line that does not line up with the wide lid ribbon. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_meadow_story.py` `giftbox()` / gift lid — same ribbon width on box and lid; `sizes-things.js` `gift()` — centre `lid` (`lid.position.x/z = 0`; check the `size: 1.6` lid vs `[1.6, 1.62, 1.6]` box).
17. **[P2]** L3 · `sizes-L2/shot01`, `sizes-L2/shot02`, `sizes-L2/shot03` — snowballs (`prop_m_snowball`) are white on white; only lumpy black outlines are visible (also `shot04`). **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_meadow_treats.py` `snowball()` — pale-blue shading/base, or a coloured scatter on the snow level (`PLANS[2]` `scatter`).
18. **[P2]** L3 · `sizes-L2/shot01`, `sizes-L2/shot02` — tree (`prop_m_tree`): bark is a scatter of dark triangles that looks like a shading error, the crown has zig-zag dark marks, the trunk base has no outline. **Fix:** `prop_meadow_garden.py` `tree()` — two or three clean bark stripes, clean crown shading, close the outline at the base.
19. **[P2]** L3 · `sizes-L2/shot05`, `sizes-L2/shot06` — mushroom (`prop_m_mushroom`): eyes are two tiny mismatched dots even at the big size; dark square blotch on the cap rim. **Fix:** `prop_meadow_garden.py` `mushroom()` — larger matching eyes with highlights, clean the cap shading.
20. **[P2]** L1 · `sizes-L0/shot07`, `sizes-L0/shot08` — hedgehog model: loose black ink strokes inside the quill mass (not on the silhouette) and a flat brown slab visible between the feet under the belly. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/hedgehog.py` — quill layer outlines the silhouette only; close the underside.
21. **[P3]** L1 · `sizes-L0/shot05`, `sizes-L0/shot13`, `sizes-L0/shot14` — loose scatter balls rest against the animals' feet (yellow ball overlapping the big bear's foot; red ball in front of the small kitty's legs through the finale). **Fix:** `sizes.js` `spawnProps()` — `z = 1.9 + rand * 1.3` → `2.6 + rand * 1.0`; `collide()` box 0.6 deeper toward the camera; conflicts with AUDIT Z6: audit cuts to 3 props placed behind the line, not in front.

23. **[P3]** L3 · `sizes-L2/shot01–14` — the "garden harvest" level is a snowfield with summer-green trees, sunflowers, melons and pumpkins; much barer than L1/L2. **Fix:** `sizes.js` — give Level 3 the harvest/autumn look, or dress the snow version (frosted beds `0xd5e2f2` on white are barely visible).

## Beach world

### patterns — Pattern parade
Captures: `patterns-L0`, `patterns-L1`, `patterns-L2` plus the three `-short` folders (16 frames each, read at half size); all captures (17:21–17:33) are newer than every game/engine file involved, nothing stale.

1. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `patterns-L1/shot09`, `patterns-L2/shot10`, `patterns-L0/shot07` — finale photo and "Look what you made!" show an empty track/beach and empty towel; the parade has already left (L3: walking out of frame, crab over the campfire); L1 also shows empty boardwalk with no caption after each exit. **Fix:** as AUDIT P7 (`patterns.js` `onFinale()` must not call `leave()` before the photo). ↔ AUDIT P7
2. **[P1]** L3 · `patterns-L2/shot02`, `patterns-L2/shot05`, `patterns-L2-short/shot01` — at every round start the whole new row lies in one heap at the world origin for about 1.6 s (crab inside a beach ball), then snaps 5.9–6.5 units to the leader. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/patterns.js` `arrivePodiums()` — hide items when created in `startRound()` (as `arriveWalk()` does), not after the 1.4 s leader tween + 0.2 s wait. ↔ AUDIT P5 (drops that walk-in)
3. **[P1]** L2 · teleport log (every round start) — the train is added at the world origin and placed only by the first anim frame: engine, driver, wagons and cargo sit in a heap for one frame, then jump 8.3–17.6 units left. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `arriveTrain()` — set positions for `e = 0` before `this.root.add(...)`, or create `visible = false` and show in the first anim callback.
4. **[P1]** L3 · `patterns-L2/shot08`, `patterns-L2/shot09`, `patterns-L2-short/shot09` — first pattern element ends inside its podium (ball embedded, starfish tips out both sides, shell missing in round 3); rounds 3, 5, 6 show slot 1 empty. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `landAt()` — `await` the final `tween(it.position, to, 0.15)` and `it.position.copy(to)`; start the tune loop after a 0.2 s wait.
5. **[P1]** L2 · `patterns-L1/shot01`, `patterns-L1/shot06`, `patterns-L1/shot08` — items sit so deep in the wagons they are half hidden (shell top arc, crab eyes, bucket rim); blue bucket rides in a blue wagon. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `arriveTrain()` rider height `cart.position.y + 0.29` → ≈ `+ 0.55`, or lower walls in `tools/blender/creatures/prop_traincart.py`; wagon colours never `th.bucket`.
6. **[P1]** L2 · `patterns-L1/shot03`, `patterns-L1/shot05`, `patterns-L1/shot07` — crab claws stick out through the wagon side walls and overlap the next wagon and the engine cab. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `makeItem()` crab scale `0.46` → ≈ `0.34` on the train stage (wagon inner width 1.3), or raise the crab onto the rim.
7. **[P1]** L2 · `patterns-L1/shot06`, `patterns-L1/shot05`, `patterns-L1-short/shot13` — the smiling sun sits exactly behind the third wagon: the orange crab/starfish there disappears and the sun's face reads as a passenger. **Fix:** `patterns.js` — move the row or sun for this stage (`ROW_Y` + camera below the sun disc, or no wagon stopping at x ≈ −0.9…0).
8. **[P1]** L2 · `patterns-L1-short/shot04` — the old train drives through the end buffer post and off the track into the air while the next train enters: two trains on screen. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `afterSuccess()` wait `0.7` → full leave time for the train stage; `prop_track.py`/`makeTrack` — run track past both frame edges or drop the buffers.
9. **[P1]** L3 · `patterns-L2/shot02`, `patterns-L2/shot05` — round change pile-up: new podiums grow where old ones are still being squashed flat ("pancakes" on the sand) while the old parade walks off and the new heap lies between. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `afterSuccess()` podium wait `0.7` → ≈ `2.4`; `leave()` sink podiums by `position.y`, not `scale.y → 0.001`.
10. **[P1]** L1, L3 · `patterns-L0/shot03`, `patterns-L0/shot02`, `patterns-L2/shot07` — the leader's flag pole goes straight through the duck's wing/body and crosses the gull's body when waved; nobody holds it. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `makeLeader()` `flag.position.set(-0.35, 0.2, 0.3)` — plant beside the bird (`z ≈ 0.65`) with wing raised, or parent to the wing bone; pivot the wave at the hand.
11. **[P1]** L1, L3 · `patterns-L0/shot03`, `patterns-L0-short/shot06`, `patterns-L2/shot04` — shells hover above planks/podium tops with sea visible under them; starfish tips do not reach the wood. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `makeItem()` — `shell` `position.y 0.45` → ≈ `0.30`, `star` `0.55` → ≈ `0.50`; safer: `Box3.setFromObject(m)` and subtract `box.min.y`.
12. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `patterns-L0/shot03`, `patterns-L1/shot02`, `patterns-L2/shot07` — Pip's hat brim is inside the boardwalk end, wing over its legs, Pip cut by the left edge; L2 his head covers the buffer lantern/track end and the train passes behind it; L3 leftmost podium touches his wing, lantern on his cap. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `build()` `pip.position.set(-6.6, 0, -0.5)` → about `(-7.6, 0, 1.2)`; nudge camera so Pip is whole.
13. **[P1]** L1 · `patterns-L0-short/shot03` — the finished parade marches off the right end of the boardwalk and continues in mid-air at plank height. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `leave()` (walk stage) — items travel `x0 + 13` at `ROW_Y` but the boardwalk ends at x = 6.5: hop down at the end, or extend `makeBoardwalk` past the frame edge. ↔ AUDIT P7 (reworks `leave()`)
14. **[P1]** L1 · `patterns-L0/shot06`, teleport log — crabs arrive by falling from 7 units above the planks: 5.8–6.2 units in 0.17 s (≈ 35 units/s); crab at the top of the frame half behind the caption. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `arriveWalk()` `ROW_Y + 7` + `landAt(time 0.55, arc 0.4)` — scuttle crabs in along the planks, or start at `ROW_Y + 2`, `time: 0.6`.
15. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `patterns-L0-short/shot03`, teleport logs — the parade/train leaves at zip speed: 13 units in 1.8 s on cubic ease-in ends at ≈ 21.7 units/s (0.35–0.40 units/frame for duck, gull, crabs). **Fix:** `patterns.js` `leave(dur, dist = 13)` — `Ease.in` → `Ease.inOut` or linear ≤ 3 units/s (dur ≈ 3.5 s); adjust the `afterSuccess()` wait. ↔ AUDIT P7 (reworks `leave()`)
16. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `patterns-L0-short/shot04`, `patterns-L1-short/shot14`, `patterns-L2-short/shot08` — the answer crab launches from the towel at ≈ 21 units/s (0.35–0.41 units/frame), up to caption height. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `pick()` `landAt(c, to, { time: 0.7, arc: 1.8 })` → `time: 1.0, arc: 1.2` for the crab branch. ↔ AUDIT P6 (walking-pace hop, no 1.8-high throw)
17. **[P2]** L1 · all shots — the pattern row is tiny: items ≈ 70 px tall (9 % of the 772 px frame) in a strip at y 280–360, empty sand below, towel larger than the row. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `build()` `setCamera((0, 3.3, 12.8), (0, 2.0, 0))` → about `(0, 3.0, 10.6)` looking at `(0, 1.9, 0)`, and/or row item scale 1 → 1.3.
18. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `patterns-L0/shot02`, `patterns-L1/shot04`, `patterns-L2/shot06` — unpicked choices stay full size on the towel while the answer flies, lands and the success caption shows. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `pick()` — move `for (const o of this.choices) popOut(...)` before `await this.landAt(...)`.
19. **[P2]** L1–L3 · all shots — caption glyphs do not match the objects: 🏐 grey volleyball for the striped beach ball, 🐚 spiral conch for the scallop, ⭐ for the starfish. **Fix:** as AUDIT P1 (`ICON` removed from captions; if any picture caption stays, use real item thumbnails). ↔ AUDIT P1
20. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `patterns-L1/shot01`, `patterns-L1/shot05`, `patterns-L2/shot06` — with three choices the outer two hang off the towel ends (starfish half on sand, crab claw over the fringe). **Fix:** `patterns.js` `startRound()` `xs = [-2.8, 0, 2.8]` → `[-2.2, 0, 2.2]`, or `makeChoiceTowel(8.4, 1.8)`.
21. **[P2]** L1 · `patterns-L0-short/shot13` — when the gap is the last slot the "?" badge touches the leader's flag and the white ground ring is invisible on the planks. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `arriveWalk()` `hx = slotX(n - 1, n) + 1.45` → `+ 2.1`; ring in `startRound()` gets a dark rim, radius 0.55–0.7.
22. **[P2]** L3 · `patterns-L2/shot01`, `patterns-L2/shot07`, `patterns-L2-short/shot11` — the leader stands in the campfire scenery (fire looks part of the gull); with seven podiums each thrown ball appears full size over the duck's head. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `arrivePodiums()` `z = ROW_Z + 0.4` → ≈ `ROW_Z + 1.6`, or hide campfire/log; spawn thrown items at scale 0 and grow.
23. **[P2]** L2 · `patterns-L1/shot06`, `patterns-L1/shot09`, `patterns-L1/shot01` — a background sailboat sits behind the empty wagon so its sail looks like cargo in the gap; in `shot09` a boat appears to ride the rails (track top on the horizon). **Fix:** move boats away from x ≈ 2.5–5 for this stage, or raise the track above the horizon.
24. **[P2]** L2 · `patterns-L1/shot01`, `patterns-L1/shot03` — engine: front wheel has broken black ink cracks, stray squiggle on the cab; driver jammed in the cab with the window pillar through it, gull driver an unreadable grey lump. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_engine.py` — clean wheel outline, open cab side; `patterns-stage.js` `makeEngine()` seat `(-0.48, 0.36, 0)` higher/forward, driver scale `0.8` → `0.65`.
25. **[P2]** L3 · `patterns-L2/shot03`, `patterns-L2/shot09` — the crab is wider than the podium top; legs and claws hang in the air both sides. **Fix:** `patterns.js` crab scale `0.46` → ≈ `0.36` on the podium stage, or widen the top in `tools/blender/creatures/prop_podium.py`.
26. **[P2]** L3 · `patterns-L2-short/shot05`, `patterns-L2-short/shot15` — a thrown beach ball lies on the sand right of the leader near the log before being snapped onto its podium. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `landAt()` — on the podium stage use a tweened arc for every item (as the crab does), not a physics toss with a 0.3 s cut-off.
27. **[P2]** L1 · all shots — a palm frond lies across the sun's face in the top-left corner for the whole level. **Fix:** hide that palm for this game or shift the camera (`setCamera` x ≈ +0.6).
28. **[P2]** L1–L2 · all shots — towel: white stripes have wobbly, ragged edges and the three stripes do not line up with the two choice positions (±1.9). **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_towel.py` — straight crisp stripes; `patterns.js` choice `xs`.
29. **[P2]** L3 · all shots — every podium carries the same stray black ink tick on the upper right of its body. **Fix:** `prop_podium.py` — remove the interior ink edge.
30. **[P3]** L1 · `patterns-L0-short/shot09` — round-4 payoff confetti bursts over the gull leader and flag while notes float. **Fix:** as AUDIT P8 (`payoff()` `g === 3` confetti removed). ↔ AUDIT P8
31. **[P3]** L1 · all shots — the background islet and small palm sit exactly behind the leader: it appears to stand on a sand heap and the flag merges with the palm trunk. **Fix:** move the leader's stop or the islet.
32. **[P3]** L1 · all shots — nothing on the boardwalk casts a contact shadow; items look pasted on. **Fix:** `patterns.js` `makeItem()` — add `blobShadow` per item.
33. **[P3]** L3 · `patterns-L2/shot01`, `patterns-L2/shot03`, `patterns-L2/shot09` — glowing jellyfish hover between the podiums just below item height and read as extra creatures in the row. **Fix:** hide the jellyfish near x −5…5 for this stage.

Teleports: L1 21 events — 6 crab ZIPs (`arriveWalk()`→`landAt()`), P1 #14; 14 SNAP/ZIPs at the end of `leave()`, P1 #15; 1 answer-crab leap (`pick()`→`landAt()`), P1 #16. ### where — Where's Crab?
Captures: `where-L0`, `where-L1`, `where-L2` plus `-short` folders. ALL where captures (17:34–17:43) pre-date edits to `where.js` (17:49) and `where-props.js` (17:50) and rebuilds of `prop_bucket.glb` (18:02), `prop_parasol.glb` (18:10), `prop_pole.glb` (18:06), `prop_boat.glb` (17:48); dependent items are tagged re-verify. Post-finale frames ignored.

1. **[P1]** L1 · `where-L0/shot01`, `where-L0-short/shot02`, `where-L0-short/shot03` — when the crab goes IN the bucket the pail becomes a sealed lumpy blue dome: handle vanishes, rim dragged into a lid, crab invisible under "In!"; still deformed next round. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_bucket.py` bone `handle` weights (handle layer only) + `app/src/lessons/where-props.js` `makeBucketProp` `userData.flop`; eyes and claws must show above the rim (raise `zones.in.rest.y` 0.55 if not). (re-verify: capture pre-dates code change; script now says handle "moves ONLY the handle layer")
2. **[P1]** L3 · `where-L2/shot01`, `where-L2/shot07`, `where-L2/shot08` — the upside-down green bucket is broken: wall bulges into a ragged bowl below the handle, rim torn/wavy, black gaps at the pivots, handle sticks out as a hoop. **Fix:** `where-props.js` `pail()` upside-down branch (`bones.handle.rotation.x = 1.85`) + `prop_bucket.py` — same weight bug as #1; hang the handle flat against the wall. (re-verify: capture pre-dates code change)
3. **[P1]** L2–L3 · `where-L1-short/shot01`, `where-L1/shot01`, `where-L2/shot02` — the umbrella looks broken: uneven torn-looking panels, a red panel hanging like a rag, blue one mostly hidden, lopsided canopy. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_parasol.py` — 8 equal scalloped panels, clean colour borders, symmetric, ~3.2 wide so a 1.6-scale duck fits under. (re-verify: rebuilt 18:10 after capture)
4. **[P1]** L2 · `where-L1/shot01`, `where-L1-short/shot03` — duck UNDER the umbrella: canopy drops onto it and panels cut through neck, chest and wing; the pole telescopes to a stub inside the duck's back. **Fix:** `where-props.js` `makeUmbrellaProp` — `zones.under.rest` z 0.35 → ≥ 1.0, `cover.drop` only to `POLE - 0.5` (not `- 1.55`), do not scale the pole; no `gagUnder` squash for `hides:false`. (re-verify: capture pre-dates code change)
5. **[P1]** L2 · `where-L1/shot03`, `where-L1/shot05`, `where-L1-short/shot06` — the open box's left flap is detached: a brown slab floats up-left with a clear gap; right flap is a thin plank (same in the bubble). **Fix:** `where-props.js` `makeBoxProp` `bones.flapL.rotation.z = 2.0` / `tools/blender/creatures/prop_box.py` bone `flapL` — bone head on the top-left hinge edge, mirror `flapR`, open ≈ ±2.2.
6. **[P1]** L1–L2 · `where-L0-short/shot15`, `where-L0-short/shot16`, `where-L1-short/shot09` — crab goes UNDER the chair at full size: leg and stretcher bar pass through claw and belly, eye-stalks through the slats; once squashed the front rail crosses its eyes. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/where.js` `gagUnder` — shrink to `base*fit` before/while the position tween; for `hides:false` covers `fwd` 0.5 → ≤ 0.15; same in `onFinale`.
7. **[P1]** L3 · `where-L2/shot08` — crab UNDER the bucket is wider than the pail: both claws come out through the pail wall. **Fix:** `where-props.js` `makeBucketProp` `zones.under.fit: 0.72` → ≤ 0.55 (pail r 1.2, crab 3.5 wide) and `clawsUp(ch, 1)` before `cover.drop`.
8. **[P1]** L3 · `where-L2-short/shot09`, `where-L2/shot01` — "Under!", the pal's hop-over and the rainbow fire 0.4 s after the drop while the cover is still in the air and the friend still on the sand. **Fix:** `where.js` `drop()` — replace `gag = this.gagUnder(…); await this.wait(0.4)` with awaiting the gag up to `cover.drop` (split `gagUnder` into `hide` + `peek`) before `success`, `palJoins`, `storyPayoff`.
9. **[P1]** L2–L3 · teleport ZIPs after `where-L1/shot01`, `where-L1/shot04`, `where-L2/shot01` — in every UNDER round the friend slides ~4.5 units from home to under the prop in 0.5 s (peak ≈ 27 units/s, 0.45 units/frame), no walk. **Fix:** `where.js` `gagUnder` `s.tween(ch.position, …, 0.5, Ease.inOut)` → duration `distance / 3` (≈ 1.5 s) so the gait layer walks it.
10. **[P1]** L3 · teleport ZIP after `where-L2/shot01` (gull, t=22967) — the gull is thrown from the sand to the umbrella top: ≈ 6 units in 0.6 s, starting ≈ 24 units/s. **Fix:** `where.js` `drop()` `this.arc(ch, rest, 0.9, 0.6)` → duration `max(0.6, distance / 5)` (≈ 1.2 s), or walk to the prop's foot first, then hop.
11. **[P1]** L1 · teleport between `where-L0/shot05` and `where-L0/shot06` (t=48179) — the watching baby crab dashes round the basket: ~8.5 units in 1.5 s, peak ≈ 17 units/s. **Fix:** `where.js` `palJoins` `s.anim(1.5, …, Ease.inOut)` → duration `pathLength / 3` (≈ 2.8 s), gentler ease, or spawn the pal at x 4.5 instead of 6.4.
12. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `where-L0/shot06`, `where-L1-short/shot10`, `where-L2-short/shot08` — friends leaving the previous round walk across Pip and the new prop while it lands, new friend entering, bubble up (five characters at once; L2 bubble shows a box while ring and chair remain). **Fix:** `where.js` `afterSuccess` — `await` the walk-off (at least to `x < -5`), walk off along z ≥ 3.2. ↔ AUDIT W4 (friends stay; applies to any remaining walk-off)
13. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `where-L0-short/shot03`, `where-L1-short/shot06`, `where-L2-short/shot08` — the thought bubble pops up 2.5–3 s before its owner arrives; it hangs alone with its tail pointing at the previous friend (L3: box still falling behind it). **Fix:** `where.js` `startRound` — call `this.showThought(spec)` after the walk-in `await`, or make the bubble follow `ch.position.x`.
14. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `where-L0/shot04`, `where-L1-short/shot02`, `where-L1-short/shot11` — the caption pill overlaps the top of the thought bubble; the bubble covers the far palm/island, half the sun's face and (L2 rain round) the angry cloud; mini duck's head pokes into the caption. **Fix:** `where.js` `showThought` `g.position.set(…, 3.82, 2.6)` → y ≈ 3.4 and scale 0.85, or anchor beside the caption.
15. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `where-L1-short/shot01`, `where-L1/shot04`, `where-L2/shot02` — the bubble's mini scene is too small to read: umbrella + duck fill ~15 % of the bubble, crab under the mini chair is a red speck. **Fix:** `where.js` `showThought` `k = Math.min(0.55, 1.75 / tall, …)` — crop tall props (canopy + friend only), min mini-friend height 0.5 bubble units.
16. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `where-L1/shot02`, `where-L1/shot06`, `where-L2-short/shot11` — the friend's home spot overlaps both props (claws over castle and boat, ring and chair leg; covers the left third of the target hat; claw over the bucket). **Fix:** `where.js` `SPAWN` z 3.6 → 4.6, or `L`/`R` −3.1/3.2 → ∓4.0 with Pip moved back.
17. **[P2]** L1 · `where-L0/shot04`, `where-L0-short/shot08` — the crab's home spot overlaps the only target: its claw covers the towel's right end. **Fix:** `where.js` `startRound` `this.home.x = … * 0.9` — use ±2.2 for a lone prop wider than 2.5, or derive from prop half-width + friend half-width.
18. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `where-L0/shot06`, `where-L1-short/shot09`, `where-L2-short/shot11` — story floats are system emoji, badly placed: ⛵ beside the sun, 🍉 alone in the sky, ☀️ half behind the caption, 😎 a chair-height above the crab, stray 💦; 💰 and photo-style ⚽. **Fix:** as AUDIT W7; also anchor to the friend (`rest + (0, 1.0·base, 0)`), keep y ≤ 4.6. ↔ AUDIT W7
19. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `where-L1-short/shot16`, `where-L2-short/shot16` — the finale photo is taken mid show-off: polaroid flying with the hat in mid-air above a full-size crab; crab in mid-air at the flash. **Fix:** as AUDIT W5 (photo after the play ends); else `where.js` `onFinale` returns a promise awaited in `showOff()`, or `admireTime` ≥ ≈ 7 s. ↔ AUDIT W5
20. **[P2]** L1 · `where-L0/shot07`, `where-L0-short/shot13`, `where-L0-short/shot14` — the crab shrinks to about half size to sit on the chair (≈ 140 px vs ≈ 310 px on the sand). **Fix:** `where-props.js` `makeChairProp` `zones.on.fit: 0.52` — widen the deck chair (W 2.1 → ~3.2, arm rests outside) so `fit` ≥ 0.8.
21. **[P2]** L2 · `where-L1-short/shot12`, `where-L1/shot05` — duck IN the box: only the top of its head shows and the tape tab above the front rim covers its bill. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_box.py` tape decal ends at the rim; `where-props.js` `makeBoxProp` `zones.in.rest` y `t` → 0.35.
22. **[P2]** L2 · `where-L1-short/shot14` — crab UNDER the hat is wider than the brim (both claws out) and its eyes show in front of the brim. **Fix:** `where-props.js` `makeHatProp` `zones.under.fit: 0.75` → 0.55 or brim radius 1.85 → 2.4; `liftCover(holder, 1.8, 0.35)` tip → 0.55.
23. **[P2]** L3 · `where-L2-short/shot03` — the crab is squashed pancake-flat (y 0.62) in the open well before the bucket comes down; reads as a collapsed mesh. **Fix:** `where.js` `gagUnder` — run the squash tween together with `cover.drop`.
24. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `where-L1/shot02`, `where-L2/shot07`, `where-L2-short/shot14` — the boat "floats" on a flat single-colour blue ellipse on the sand (brighter on the night beach). **Fix:** `where-props.js` `makeBoatProp` `pool` — shallow modelled tide/paddling pool with a sand lip and lighter inner ring, or dig it in. (re-verify: boat rebuilt 17:48, capture pre-dates code change)
25. **[P2]** L2 · `where-L1/shot01`, `where-L1-short/shot03` — the rain-payoff rainbow is ~600 px wide, saturated, right behind umbrella and duck; hides the sun; Pip covers its left foot. **Fix:** `where.js` `storyPayoff` `'rain'` — `popIn(s, bow, 1, …)` → scale 0.55 at `(0, 0.9, -6)` instead of `(rest.x, 0.9, -2.5)`.
26. **[P2]** L1 · `where-L0-short/shot02` — the splash crown is a ruler-straight row of 11 tiny outlined spiked balls plus a tall outlined ellipse in the sky; reads as little bombs. **Fix:** `where-props.js` `splashCrown` + `app/src/engine/beach.js` `makeWaterDrop` — outline 0.18 → ≤ 0.05, arc drops outward on a parabola from the rim, drop the centre spout or make it a teardrop.
27. **[P2]** L2 · `where-L1/shot01`, `where-L1-short/shot02` — rain drops are heavy-outlined spiked balls (same look as #26) and fall only around the bucket, not where the story is. **Fix:** `beach.js` `makeWaterDrop` outline; `where.js` `update` rain — spawn across x ±5.
28. **[P2]** L1 · `where-L0/shot01`, `where-L0-short/shot02` — a system-emoji 👆 hint hand stays parked on the bucket front, over the star sticker, during "In!". **Fix:** `where.js` `drop()` success branch — call `fx.clearHands()` (only `finale()` clears hands).
29. **[P2]** L3 · `where-L2/shot01`, `where-L2-short/shot02` — the "seeker" gull faces away with head tucked: a headless blob with a 🙈 emoji above. **Fix:** `where.js` `startStory` `'hide'` — turn to `y: 2.2` (three-quarter back view), cover its eyes with wings instead of the emoji. ↔ AUDIT W7 (🙈 float)
30. **[P2]** L2–L3 · teleport after the wrong answer (duck 0.89, t=13975; crab 1.16, t=13960) — `pulseThought()` sets `userData.t = 0`: the full-size mini friend vanishes and restarts in one frame, a visible cut in the bubble. **Fix:** `where.js` `pulseThought` — set `u.t = 2.65` (plays the 0.35 s shrink-out, then loops).
31. **[P2]** L1 · `where-L0-short/shot01`, `where-L0-short/shot10` — in the picture instruction the mini crab's claws stick out through the bucket and basket side walls. **Fix:** `where.js` `animThought` — raise the mini friend's claws once in (`clawsUp` on `u.fr`), or `u.sz` × 0.8 for `want === 'in'`.
32. **[P2]** L1 · `where-L0/shot07`, `where-L0-short/shot07` — the pal ends its hop-over standing on a loose beach ball (ball overlaps gull's foot/belly and crab's belly). **Fix:** `where.js` `palJoins` — before hopping push any bit within 1.2 units of `to` away (`physics.poke`), or pick `to` clear of `this.bits`.
33. **[P2]** L3 · `where-L2-short/shot14` — the pal duck ends almost entirely hidden behind the boat and the crab's raised claw. **Fix:** `where.js` `palJoins` — for `z.id === 'in'` on wide props put the pal in front (`rest.z + 2.2`).
34. **[P2]** L3 · `where-L2/shot03`, `where-L2/shot05`, `where-L2-short/shot06` — the pal waits exactly in front of the campfire/log: flames stick out behind its head, log from its side. **Fix:** `where.js` `startRound` pal spot `(6.4, 0, -1.9)` → `(5.6, 0, 0.6)` on this look, or hide the campfire.
35. **[P2]** L3 · `where-L2/shot04`, `where-L2-short/shot08` — the boat at `L` touches Pip (stern and flag over his wing) and the leaving crab covers both. **Fix:** `where.js` `L = -3.1` → −2.6 for the boat (3.4 long), or Pip to x −7.2.
36. **[P2]** L1 · `where-L0-short/shot08` — the 🥱 float sits on the crab's right eye. **Fix:** `where.js` `startStory` `'sleep'` offset `(0.5, 1.6, 0)` → `(1.6, 2.4, 0)`. ↔ AUDIT W7 (emoji floats)
37. **[P2]** L2 · `where-L1-short/shot01` — the level opens with "Let's play with Crab!" while a duck walks in. **Fix:** as AUDIT W9 (`app/src/lessons/meta.js` `where.intro`). ↔ AUDIT W9
38. **[P3]** L1 · `where-L0/shot06`, `where-L0-short/shot08` — the bubble's mini scene is pushed to the right half; the left half is empty while the mini friend rests on the prop. **Fix:** `where.js` `showThought` `scene.position.set(…)` — centre on prop + rest pose, not start-to-prop extent.
39. **[P3]** L1 · `where-L0/shot07`, `where-L0-short/shot07` — beach ball segments have a saw-tooth edge and a much thinner outline than everything else. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_beachball.py` — cut panels along mesh edges, outline width to match the creatures.
40. **[P3]** L2 · `where-L1/shot07` — hat: brim's lower outline breaks into dashes and the ribbon's top edge is ragged. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_hat.py` — clean paint border for the band, continuous brim outline.
41. **[P3]** L2 · `where-L1-short/shot07`, `where-L1/shot03` — chest lid sits offset with an overhang and a dark gap, like a rolled mat on the box. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_chest.py` lid — same footprint as the body, seated on it.
42. **[P3]** L3 · `where-L2/shot07`, `where-L2-short/shot14`, `where-L2-short/shot15` — red crab in/against a red boat: silhouette merges with the hull. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_boat.py` hull → blue/teal.

### more — Which has more?
Captures: `more-L0`, `more-L1`, `more-L2` plus `-short` folders; captures (17:44–17:53) post-date `more.js` (15:32) and `more-items.js`, nothing stale. Post-finale frames ignored.

1. **[P1]** L2 · all in-game shots, e.g. `more-L1/shot03` — the background shows through the jar glass exactly where the things to count are: lantern post, sailboats, island and sand shells appear "inside" both jars. **Fix:** `more-items.js` `makeHolder` jar glass (`opacity: 0.2`) — opaque pale back panel inside the jar (or back face ≥ 0.85); hide lantern posts/boats behind the holders.
2. **[P1]** L1 · `more-L0/shot01`, `more-L0-short/shot02`, `more-L0-short/shot07` — the lower matched row is laid across the gull and both awnings: a fish/star/ball on the gull's face and body, outer items over awning scallops and posts. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/more.js` `ROW_Y = [4.4, 3.3]`, `ROW_Z = 2.4` — eater down on the sand (`EATERS[0].at` y 0, no post), or rows above the awnings, e.g. `ROW_Y = [5.0, 4.2]`, `k = 0.7`, caption dropped for the match-up.
3. **[P1]** L2–L3 · `more-L1/shot03`, `more-L1-short/shot06`, `more-L2-short/shot09` — the two groups are drawn at different sizes with no size twist planned: 3 big fish vs 6 half-size; in the L3 "fewer" round 2 big fish vs 5 under half size, so the wagon with fewer looks like more. **Fix:** `more.js` `layoutGroup` `fit`/`scale` — one scale per round from the larger group, applied to both sides.
4. **[P1]** L3 · all in-game shots, e.g. `more-L2/shot01`, `more-L2/shot04` — the left wagon's black pull-handle and red knob lie across the crab: rod over its claw, knob on its cheek like a stick in its mouth. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_wagon.py` handle / `more.js` `build()` — handle to the outside (`this.racks[0].scale.x = -1`) or folded down; `EATERS[2].at` z −2.6 sits right behind it.
5. **[P1]** L1–L3 · teleports after `more-L0/shot06`, `more-L1/shot07`, `more-L2/shot07` — at the finale lap the eater jumps 1 unit toward the camera in one frame, loops ±3 units and 1.5 high at up to ≈ 10 units/s and never returns to its post (gull ends on the red awning; L3 crab covers Pip's face). **Fix:** as AUDIT M7 (lap replaced); if any lap stays, `more.js` `onFinale` ease z in (`h.z + Math.sin(e * Math.PI)`), halve amplitudes or 2.5 → 5 s, end at `h`. ↔ AUDIT M7
6. **[P1]** L2 · `more-L1-short/shot16` — during the finale lap the duck is behind/inside the right jar, seen through the glass with its head cut off by the red lid. **Fix:** as AUDIT M7 (lap replaced); else `more.js` `onFinale` keep the lap inside the gap (`h.x + Math.sin(…) * 0.6`) or in front (`z ≥ RACK_Z + 1.5`). ↔ AUDIT M7
7. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `more-L0/shot07`, `more-L1/shot08`, `more-L2-short/shot16` — "Look what you made!" and the photo show two empty holders; only the losing row's cookies/shells are left standing on their rims on the sand, one over a flower knob, one cut by the frame edge. **Fix:** `more.js` `onFinale` — refill both holders with the last round's groups (or line up all six snacks) before `showOff`; `gobble()` leftovers settle flat and pop away. ↔ AUDIT M7 (audit makes the fed eater the creation), M6
8. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `more-L0-short/shot03`, `more-L1/shot05`, `more-L2/shot05` — leftovers land badly: a fish on its nose leaning on Pip's wing; items in front of the jars reading as "still in the jar"; five fish on noses and tails over the crab's legs and a wheel, upside-down ice creams against the wagon. **Fix:** as AUDIT M6 (not-chosen group stays in its holder); else `more.js` `gobble()` toss to z ≥ 3.2, away from holders/Pip/post, lying flat, cleared before the next drop. ↔ AUDIT M6
9. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `more-L0/shot01`, `more-L0/shot02`, `more-L1-short/shot02` — the caption lags a step behind: "That one has fewer. Find more!" or the question is still up while the correct match-up plays; "More!" appears only at the gobble. **Fix:** `more.js` `pick()` — on a right pick clear the caption / show "More!" immediately, not queued behind the redirect line. ↔ AUDIT M5 ("More!" at the extras' wiggle)
10. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `more-L0/shot03`, `more-L0-short/shot05`, `more-L2-short/shot07` — the top row and the hop/gobble arcs run into the caption (stars behind its left end, shells behind "More!", cherries and "+2" touching it). **Fix:** `more.js` `pick()` hop `Math.sin(p * Math.PI) * 1.2` → 0.5; `gobble()` arc `* 1.0` → 0.3; `ROW_Y[0]` ≤ 4.2 or hide the caption during the match-up.
11. **[P2]** L2 · `more-L1/shot01`, `more-L1/shot07`, `more-L1-short/shot12` — the matched lower row is laid over the sun's face and the lids; with orange starfish the sun reads as one more star in the row. **Fix:** `more.js` `ROW_Y`/`ROW_Z` as #2; `PIP[1]`, `EATERS[1].at` — do not centre the gap on the sun, or dim/hide the sun during the match-up.

### body — Touch Bear's nose
Captures: `body-L0`, `body-L1`, `body-L2` (17:55–17:58); `body.js` (15:06) and `body-gear.js` are older than the shots, so current. `prop_parasol.glb`/`prop_pole.glb` were rebuilt 18:06–18:10, after the capture — umbrella item is re-verify.

1. **[P1]** L1, L3 · `body-L0/shot05`, `body-L0/shot09`, `body-L2/shot10` — "feet" self-touch pitches the whole bear forward about its feet: the root-parented swim ring goes through towel and sand, a ball is half swallowed, arms vanish, head fills ~45 % of the frame; in the L3 finale bow both flippers sink under the sand and the spade swings across the shin. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/body.js` `selfTouch()` `bend` (`f.rotation x: 0.35`) — bend the `body`/`head` bone (≤ 0.2 rad) or skip when `this.gear.tummy` exists; keep root-parented gear level; hide or re-seat the held spade during the reach.
2. **[P1]** L2–L3 · `body-L1/shot03`, `body-L1/shot04`, `body-L2/shot09` — "hands" gear: the spade hangs upside-down (blade up) from the side of the paw, gripped by nothing, open paw spread behind it; L3 handle end overlaps the rock. **Fix:** `body-gear.js` `spade()` `sp.rotation.z = 0.5`, `GEAR.hands.off [0.05, 0.05, 0.12]` — handle through the paw, blade down/out (rotation.z ≈ π + 0.4), curl the arm in.
3. **[P1]** L2–L3 · `body-L1/shot04`, `body-L2/shot03` — "mouth" gear: the ice lolly is pasted flat on the face over half the smile, stick hanging below the chin, held by nothing. **Fix:** `body-gear.js` `GEAR.mouth` — parent to the hand (`on: 'hand'`) and bring the paw up with `selfTouch('mouth')`, or angle it out of the mouth corner (rotation.z ≈ 0.9, off x 0.22).
4. **[P1]** L1 · `body-L0/shot01`–`shot10` — the beach umbrella reads as broken: two sagging white flaps, a red lump off the hub, a blue shard behind the pole, a green sliver at the left tip; canopy edge runs into the bear's head. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_parasol.py` (canopy panels/valance); `body-gear.js` `makeSet(0)` `FOOT` −3.3 → about −4.6. (re-verify: model rebuilt 18:10 after capture)
5. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `body-L0/shot05`, `body-L1/shot09`, `body-L2/shot07` — stray black ink ticks at both armpits and a hard black crease across each ear when Bear bends or swings arms in; Kitty with arms lowered shows jagged ink lines down the inside of both arms. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/bear.py`, `cat.py` — shoulder/ear weights and outline hull (folds when the arm bone passes ~1 rad); clamp the arm swing in `body.js` `selfTouch()` `reach`.
6. **[P2]** L1 · `body-L0/shot02`–`shot10`, `body-L0/shot05` — the nose "sunscreen" is a whipped-cream swirl as big as the muzzle top that hides the nose being taught; head-on it is a flat white disc with a ring. **Fix:** `body-gear.js` `sunscreen()` scale 0.85 → ~0.5, `GEAR.nose.off` y +0.01 → +0.07 (or a flat stripe decal).
7. **[P2]** L1 · `body-L0/shot07` — "Tummy!" is asked again while the swim ring fully covers the tummy; the tap lands on the ring. **Fix:** `body-gear.js` `GEAR.tummy.off` y −0.1 → about −0.45, or `body.js` `PLANS[0].rounds` — do not repeat a part that already wears gear.
8. **[P2]** L1 · `body-L0/shot04`–`shot10` — "Ears!" gives one pink blob pressed flat into the left ear only; reads as a heart/smear, not a hibiscus; right ear gets nothing. **Fix:** `body-gear.js` `GEAR.ears` — add `earR`, `flower(0.22)` → `flower(0.32)`, `off` z 0.15 → 0.24, tilt to face the camera.
9. **[P2]** L1 · `body-L0/shot06` — nose "boop": the paw stops at the right cheek/jaw, a head-width short of the nose; raised forearm shows dark stretch streaks. **Fix:** `body.js` `selfTouch()` — raise the `slide` clamp ±0.15 → ~0.35 for nose/mouth/eyes, or nod the head (`head.rotation.x ≈ 0.25`).
10. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `body-L0/shot06`, `body-L1/shot07`, `body-L2/shot09` — the flippers are green lumps with ragged, toothed front edges; read as monster/frog feet. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_flipper.py` — clean fan blade, 2–3 smooth ribs, foot pocket, no ragged edge.
11. **[P2]** L3 · `body-L2/shot01`–`shot11` — the rock pool is a flat blue disc with a paler flat ring, no depth or shore lip; the starfish lies on it like a sticker. **Fix:** `body-gear.js` `makeSet(2)` (`geo.circle(2.2)` + `geo.ring`) — replace with a Blender prop (sunken sandy rim, inset water).
12. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `body-L1/shot01`–`shot11`, `body-L2/shot01`–`shot11` — Pip is hidden: L2 behind the paddling pool from the chest down, lantern post behind his head, sailboat over his flower; L3 behind the left rock (feet hidden) with seaweed across his tummy. **Fix:** `body.js` `build()` `pip.position.set(-6.4, 0, -2.8)` → per level, e.g. L2 `(-6.6, 0, 1.6)`, L3 x −6.8, z 1.8; or move the pool / weed `[-4.7, 0.15]`.
13. **[P2]** L2 · `body-L1/shot01`–`shot11` — the smiling sun sits exactly behind the friend's head: half a sun face pokes out from Bunny's cheek and Kitty's ear. **Fix:** `body.js` `spawnFriend()` `f.position.set(0.4, …)` or the level look's sun in `stage.js` — sun ≥ 2 units clear of x 0.4.
14. **[P2]** L2 · `body-L1/shot10` — Kitty's head is flattened ~25 % wider with both ears splayed horizontally (finale hop/ear reaction); looks deformed. **Fix:** `body.js` `onFinale()`/`react()` ear branch (`wiggle(s, head, …)`, `hop(s, f, 0.6, 0.6)`) — cap head squash ~8 %, keep ear bones out of the squash.
15. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `body-L1/shot09`, `body-L2/shot07`, `body-L2/shot09` — shower toys rest badly: a duck wedged behind Kitty's leg, ducks on their side/upside-down as hollow yellow cups (one half into a rock), a hibiscus upright in the pool rim. **Fix:** `body-gear.js` `makeToy('duck')` `rot: [0, 1, 0]`, `makeSet(1)` rim collider (r 1.3–1.7); `friendCol` full leg width; close the underside in `prop_duckie.py`. ↔ AUDIT B3 (shower removed except feet/hands)
16. **[P2]** L1 · `body-L0/shot06` — toy-shower beach balls land on the towel right in front of the feet: one over the left flipper, two overlapping, a third half behind the ring. **Fix:** `body.js` `toyShower()` feet branch target `foot.x + 0.7` → `foot.x + 2.0`; widen `friendCol` (`cw`) over feet/towel.
17. **[P2]** L3 · `body-L2/shot08` — the ball batted for "hands" rests in front of Bear's left foot exactly when "Feet!" is asked. **Fix:** `body.js` `toyShower()` hands branch impulse `{ x: 4, y: 6, z: 0.5 }` → x ≥ 7, or remove the ball before the next cue.
18. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `body-L1/shot04`, `body-L1/shot05`, `body-L2/shot09` — a beach ball rolls up to the camera and sits huge at the bottom edge/corner, cut by the frame. **Fix:** `body.js` `build()` — front play-pen wall at z ≈ 3.5 (`physics.box`).
19. **[P2]** L2 · `body-L1/shot01`–`shot04` — Bunny has no outline along the soles and a stray ink squiggle between the feet; with no contact shadow the white feet melt into the sand. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/bunny.py` (sole outline); `blobShadow` under the friend in `body.js` `spawnFriend()`.
20. **[P3]** L1 · `body-L0/shot08`, `body-L0/shot09` — shower toys are aimed at Pip and the umbrella pole: ducks cross Pip's hat, one overlaps the pole, then two ducks and a starfish sit at its foot. **Fix:** as AUDIT B3; else `body.js` `toyShower()` `to.x = 0.4 + side * (2.4 + rnd * 1.6)` — clamp left to ≥ −1.8. ↔ AUDIT B3
21. **[P3]** L3 · `body-L2/shot04`–`shot06` — leftover toy spades and flowers lie flat and tiny on the sand round the pool (five thin sticks), reading as litter. **Fix:** as AUDIT B3; else `body.js` `startRound()` friend swap — clear toys with a little exit. ↔ AUDIT B3
22. **[P3]** L1–L3 · `body-L0/shot01`, `body-L1/shot01`, `body-L2/shot01` — engine: the hint hand is the OS `👆` emoji (photo-style yellow hand), a different style from the white glove cursor, and covers the friend's mouth/face/eye. **Fix:** `app/src/engine/fx.js` `ghostPath()` `el.textContent = '👆'` — use the app's glove sprite, offset below the target.
23. **[P3]** L1–L2 · `body-L0/shot01`, `body-L1/shot01` — caption "That's the tummy. Find nose!" / "Find eyes!" is ungrammatical. **Fix:** as AUDIT B1 (`body.js` `touch()` `redirect(...)`). ↔ AUDIT B1
24. **[P3]** L2 · `body-L1/shot05` — caption "Hello Kitty!" reads as the brand name. **Fix:** `body.js` `NAME.cat` / greeting → "Hi, Kitty!" or "Here's Kitty!"; conflicts with AUDIT B5: it reuses the voiced "Hello Kitty!" greeting.

26. **[P3]** L1 · `body-L0/shot10` — engine: the sticker badge covers the bear's whole face at the finale; the sticker art is a disembodied yellow `👃` emoji. Reference only (engine sticker item).
