### sink — Sink or float lab
Captures: `sink-L0`, `sink-L1`, `sink-L2` plus `-short` folders (17:58–18:08); `sink.js` (15:16) and `sink-items.js` are older than the shots, so current. `prop_bucket.glb` (the two tubs) was rebuilt 18:10:41, after the capture — tub items are re-verify.

1. **[P1]** L3 · `sink-L2/shot05`, `sink-L2/shot06`, `sink-L2-short/shot09` — the "tiny coin" is a 5–6 px speck: invisible on the crate, on the tank floor under "You were right! It sinks!" and in the sink tub. **Fix:** `sink-items.js` `ITEMS.coin.size 0.2` → ≥ 0.5 and stand it facing the camera.
2. **[P1]** L2 · `sink-L1/shot13`, `sink-L1/shot14`, `sink-L1-short/shot14` — the key misses the sink tub and lies on the sand wedged between the tubs for the rest of the level, including "Look what you made!" (both captures). **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/sink.js` `putAway()` — after `land`, test the item is within `TUB_R * 0.78` of the tub centre, else tween it in; `ITEMS.key` ball collider (`r: 0.3`) for the toss.
3. **[P1]** L3 · `sink-L2/shot11`, `sink-L2/shot12` — the big apple does not go into the float tub: it balances on the log on the rim, towering over the tub and covering half the float sign. **Fix:** `sink.js` `putAway()` — shrink-to-fit only checks `wide > TUB_R * 1.4`; account for tub contents: lay the log flat (`ITEMS.log` ≤ `TUB_R * 1.2`), `bigapple` ≤ 1.1.
4. **[P1]** L3 · `sink-L2/shot11`, `sink-L2/shot12` — the tiny marble is off the crate (stuck in mid-air against the front-right post), then lies on the sand in front of the tubs at the finale photo; sink tub empty. **Fix:** `sink.js` `putAway()` settle check as #2; `placeOnCrate()` z + 0.05 puts small balls at the lip — centre them.
5. **[P1]** L2–L3 · `sink-L1-short/shot04`, `sink-L2-short/shot04` — on the way back to the crate the apple/log passes through the tank's top-right corner (rim end and corner post drawn across it). **Fix:** as AUDIT S6 (return trip deleted); else `sink.js` `liftOut()` second leg — arc `sin * 0.8` → ≥ 1.6, or clear x > `TP.x + IX + rad` before descending. ↔ AUDIT S6
6. **[P2]** L1–L2 · `sink-L0/shot11`, `sink-L0/shot12`, `sink-L1/shot13` — things in the tubs are almost invisible (duck a yellow sliver, rock + stone + shell one grey sliver, gem a purple tip, marble unseen) and both tubs are dry; the sink tub looks empty at the finale. **Fix:** `sink.js` `TUB_H = 0.85`, `physics.container(p, { floor: 0.14 })` → floor ~0.45, or water disc at 0.55 (float tub) and sand bed (sink tub). (re-verify: capture pre-dates `prop_bucket.glb` rebuild)
7. **[P2]** L1–L2 · `sink-L0-short/shot03`, `sink-L0-short/shot07`, `sink-L1/shot04` — after the test the thing is lifted to `TANK.TOP + 1.0`, behind the caption or off the top (rock hidden behind "It sinks!", tank already empty; L2 key/leaf/marble sit on the sun's face). **Fix:** as AUDIT S6 (item stays in the water); else `sink.js` `liftOut()` → `TANK.TOP + 0.35` and `result()` waits for `say` instead of `wait(1.6)`. ↔ AUDIT S6
8. **[P2]** L3 · `sink-L2/shot03`–`shot12` — the log sticks out of the float tub diagonally like a spoon in a cup and is visibly about half its crate size. **Fix:** as #3 — lay it flat inside the tub; avoid the visible rescale (`putAway()` `s1`). ↔ AUDIT S12
9. **[P2]** L2 · `sink-L1/shot11`, `sink-L1/shot13`, `sink-L1/shot14` — the block ends standing on one corner on the float tub's rim, more than half above and outside the tub. **Fix:** `sink.js` `putAway()` `target.y = 0.6 + Math.min(n, 3) * 0.25`, x/z spread 0.3 — settle check as #2.
10. **[P2]** L1 · `sink-L0/shot09`, `sink-L0/shot11`, `sink-L0-short/shot12` — the beach ball fills half the float tub, presses on the wall and buries the duck and sponge. **Fix:** `sink-items.js` `ITEMS.ball` `size: 1.2, r: 0.53` → `size: 0.85, r: 0.375`.
11. **[P2]** L3 · `sink-L2-short/shot08` — "The ball rolls!" but the ball has already left the frame. **Fix:** `sink.js` `pick()` roll branch — stop wall ~1.5 units past the ramp foot or follow with the camera; raise `setLinearDamping(0.55)`.
12. **[P2]** L3 · `sink-L2/shot03`, `sink-L2-short/shot07`, `sink-L2-short/shot08` — the ramp stands in front of crate and tub: high end covers the crate front and the bottoms of both choices, low end overlaps the float tub, Crab squeezed between tank and ramp. **Fix:** `sink.js` `RAMP_AT (2.4, 0, 3.0)` → x ≈ 1.2, z ≈ 3.6; `CAMS.test` d 8.2 → 9.2.
13. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `sink-L0/shot07`, `sink-L1/shot04`, `sink-L2/shot01` — when Crab watches the tank it turns side-on and tips up: looks rolled onto its side, one claw at its mouth, legs bunched. **Fix:** `sink.js` `crabLook()` (`h.rotation.x = 0.25` on a whole-shell "head") + auto look-at in `engine/creature.js` — `crab.userData.noLook = true`; eye-stalk tilt or `crab.rotation.y` ≤ 0.3.
14. **[P2]** L1–L2 · `sink-L0/shot04`, `sink-L0/shot11`, `sink-L1/shot09` — Crab parks directly in front of a tub, covering its star band and base; in `sink-L0/shot11` the caption says "In the sink tub!" while Crab sits at the float tub. **Fix:** `sink.js` `putAway()` `walkCrab(tub.x - 0.15, 0, tub.z + TUB_R + 0.9)` → beside the tub (x ± (TUB_R + 0.7), z + 0.5), always the tub just used.
15. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `sink-L0/shot01`, `sink-L1/shot01`, `sink-L2/shot02` — framing cuts characters: in the tank view Pip is cut by the left edge (L3 `shot02`: only his wing in frame), float sign and blue tub cut on the right; in the tubs view Crab is cut at the left/bottom. **Fix:** `sink.js` `CAMS.tank = cam(-2.0, 1.55, 0.3, 8.7)` → d ≈ 9.6, or `pip.position.set(-6.15, …)` → x −5.5; `CRAB_HOME` x −0.45 → 0.2.
16. **[P2]** L1 · `sink-L0/shot01` — the duck floats with its back to the camera: a yellow bean with one dot, no beak or face. **Fix:** `sink.js` `update()` buoyancy for `d.up` items — gentle yaw spring toward camera-facing (ry ≈ 0.6); `dropIn()` `spin` ±0.4 on rotation.z only.
17. **[P2]** L1 · `sink-L0/shot05`, `sink-L0-short/shot10` — the beach ball is pale blue and white, the same as water, glass and sky: reads as a bubble; ragged white panel edge, stray ink dashes on its wet line. **Fix:** `sink-items.js` `ITEMS.ball.tint 0x7fd6ff` → red/yellow; `prop_m_beachball` panels in `tools/blender/creatures/prop_meadow_toys.py`.
18. **[P2]** L2 · `sink-L1/shot03`, `sink-L1-short/shot06`, `sink-L1-short/shot07` — on the crate the key stands on the edge of its ring with the shaft horizontal in mid-air. **Fix:** `sink-items.js` `ITEMS.key` — add `rx: -Math.PI / 2` so `placeOnCrate()` rests it on its side.
19. **[P2]** L2 · `sink-L1/shot05`, `sink-L1-short/shot11`, `sink-L1-short/shot12` — the leaf lies edge-on on the crate (green pickle/caterpillar with a brown stub); dropped in, it dives vertically and stands on end under water. **Fix:** `sink-items.js` `ITEMS.leaf` — tilt to camera (`rx ≈ -0.9`); keep flat on the surface (`up` spring from first contact).
20. **[P2]** L3 · `sink-L2/shot08` — the big apple (size 1.65) is as tall as the water-to-rim gap: floating, its top and stalk go through the top frame and overlap the yellow bar. **Fix:** `sink-items.js` `ITEMS.bigapple.size 1.65` → 1.3.
21. **[P2]** L2 · `sink-L1/shot01`–`shot14`, `sink-L1-short/shot09` — the caption bubble sits on the sun in every view: eyes/smile poke out under it, rays show through the fading caption. **Fix:** `sink.js` `CAMS.tank`/`CAMS.tubs` look x, or the level-2 sun position (`stage.js`) — sun ≥ 250 px from the caption.
22. **[P2]** L2 · `sink-L1/shot02`, `sink-L1/shot03`–`shot14` — backdrop props collide with the lab: a lantern seen through the glass as if inside the tank, a sailboat mast behind the rim like a flag, a second lantern post touching the sink sign's pole. **Fix:** `sink.js` `build()` — hide those backdrop props (`calmBackdrop`) or shift `TP`/sign positions.
23. **[P2]** L3 · `sink-L2/shot01`, `sink-L2/shot05`, `sink-L2-short/shot02` — glowing backdrop jellyfish sit behind the tank at water level and read as floating inside it (pink one at the waterline centre); lantern shows through the glass top-left (`sink-L2/shot02`). **Fix:** `sink.js` `build()` — hide backdrop jellyfish/lantern within the tank's screen span.
24. **[P2]** L3 · `sink-L2/shot04`–`shot12` — the campfire sits directly behind the sink sign's pole; flame and sign merge. **Fix:** move the sink sign (`sign.position x + 0.15`) or hide the fire in this lesson.
25. **[P2]** L1 · `sink-L0-short/shot14` — the big result arrow `⬇` floats up behind the caption bubble and is cut by the top edge. **Fix:** as AUDIT S11 (arrows removed); else `sink.js` `result()` `fx.float(top, …, { size: 13 })` anchored beside the tank (x + 2.6, y 2.2). ↔ AUDIT S11
26. **[P2]** L1 · `sink-L0/shot10`, `sink-L0/shot03` — the splash is a clump of outlined droplets hanging above the surface as a bow/butterfly shape, not touching the water. **Fix:** `sink.js` `splash()` — start radius 0.2 → 0.5, hide the `prop_m_drop` ink outline, drop `size 0.9–1.4` → 0.5–0.8.
27. **[P2]** L1 · `sink-L0/shot01`–`shot10` — the tank's sandy floor has ragged brush-stroke edges with patches of the blue plinth showing through. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_sinklab.py` `tank()` layer `sand` — one clean rounded slab covering the whole inner floor.
28. **[P3]** L1 · `sink-L0/shot03`, `sink-L0/shot07`, `sink-L0-short/shot13` — "rock" is a featureless grey ellipsoid and "stone" the same shape with a green dot: two of six things look alike. **Fix:** `sink.js` `PLANS[0]` — swap `stone` for a distinct sinker (key/coin-sized shell exists); `prop_rock` facets/speckles.
29. **[P3]** L1 · `sink-L0-short/shot01`, `sink-L0-short/shot09`, `sink-L0-short/shot13` — each new thing appears hanging 2 units above the crate and drifts down from nowhere. **Fix:** `sink.js` `startRound()` (`it.position.y = y1 + 2.2`) — `popIn` on the crate, or Crab carries it on.
30. **[P3]** L3 · `sink-L2-short/shot14` — the picked ball hangs motionless in mid-air above the crate before the bounce drop. **Fix:** `sink.js` `pick()` `wait(0.3)` — let Crab hold it up, or drop without the pause.
31. **[P3]** L2–L3 · `sink-L1/shot01`, `sink-L2/shot01` — the hint halo (~250 px) plus the `👆` emoji hand cover the apple/log completely. **Fix:** `sink.js` `showHint()` `addHalo(o, 0.9)` → 0.6; engine hand as body #22.
32. **[P3]** L1 · `sink-L0/shot01`, `sink-L0/shot05` — two water lines: the white foam lip on the front glass sits lower than the tinted wet line on floating things, so they look cut at the wrong height. **Fix:** `sink-items.js` `makeTank()` `lip.position.z = IZ - 0.005` / camera pitch — lip at the surface the object meets, or drop the lip.
33. **[P3]** L2 · `sink-L1/shot09`, `sink-L1/shot10` — the block's white star has ragged edges with dark flecks. **Fix:** `prop_m_block` star as a separate clean `layer()` in `tools/blender/creatures/prop_meadow_toys.py`.
34. **[P3]** L3 · `sink-L2/shot01`–`shot12` — the moon is cut by the top edge and sits under the `hold` button in both camera stations. **Fix:** level-3 look moon position (`stage.js`) or camera pitch.

## Farm world

Audit cross-references: `AUDIT-farm.md` has IDs only for X1–X3; other rows are cited as `AUDIT §<section no.> "<Beat cell>"` (§1 moo, §2 share, §3 band, §4 pots, §5 story).

### moo — Who says moo?
Captures: `moo-L0`, `moo-L1`, `moo-L2` (shots 01–10 each; 11–16 are fade/Home). All shots post-date the last edit of `moo.js` / `moo-farm.js`; nothing stale. Engine items recorded by group A, referenced only: clouds across the sun (raw: "moo L1 shots 06–10, moo L2 shots 07–10"), sticker badge over the scene (raw: "moo L1/L2 shot10").

1. **[P1]** L1, L3 · `moo-L0/shot04`, `moo-L2/shot07` — pig's "muddy" result is three dark-brown outlined ovals floating in front of the body at fixed offsets (left one past the hip silhouette; in L3 one bridges the gap between arm and belly). **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/moo-farm.js` `SIGNATURE.pig` — delete the three `geo.sphere(0.17…)` blobs (`[[0.32,0.95,0.72],[-0.38,0.6,0.7],[0.12,0.4,0.78]]`); add a hidden `mud` paint layer (3 soft splat decals on belly/cheek) to `tools/blender/creatures/pig.py` and toggle it here.
2. **[P1]** L2 · `moo-L1/shot03` — hen's trough runs into the sheep's left hay bale (trough x −2.7…−1.1 at z −1.0; bale centre x −0.95, z −1.15, r ≈ 0.5: 0.3-unit intersection). **Fix:** `moo-farm.js` `makeSpot` — spot props reach ±1.6–2.0 but slots are 3.7 apart (`XS[3]` in `moo.js`): trough to `(0.9, 0, -1.5)`, hay bales to z −1.7, every side prop within |x| ≤ 1.3.
3. **[P1]** L2–L3 · `moo-L2/shot01`, `moo-L2/shot04`, `moo-L1/shot08` — the `trough` spot's pail lands inside neighbours' props: embedded in the horse's big pumpkin (centres 0.58 apart, radii 0.5 + 0.3); swallowed by the pig's right `hedge` bush (only the handle shows); inside the sheep's right bale footprint in the 4-row (pail x −2.41, z −0.37; bale x −2.53, z −0.89, r 0.51); against Pip's big bale, handle crossing the strap (`moo-L1/shot03`). **Fix:** `moo-farm.js` `makeSpot` `kind === 'trough'` — drop the pail (`pail.position.set(-1.6, 0, -0.75)`) or place it at `(1.2, 0, -0.2)`; 4-up rows (`XS[4]`, 2.75 apart): scale side offsets 0.7 not 0.85 (`moo.js` `sp.scale.setScalar`) and use only spots with nothing beside the animal.
4. **[P2]** L1–L3 · all shots — framing: animals ~200 px of 772 (26 %; FEEL-BRIEF asks 40–60 %), bottom 170 px empty grass + flat orange path band; L3 4-up animals 30 % with 22 % empty at the bottom. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/moo.js` `build()` — `frame(0, 3.6, 11.8, 0, 2.2, 0)` → about `frame(-0.6, 3.0, 9.4, -0.6, 1.9, 0)` for 2–3 animals (Pip's bale at x −6.3 still in frame); glide back to the current view only for L3 4-animal rounds.
5. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `moo-L0/shot05`, `moo-L0/shot07`, `moo-L1/shot01` — `pond` spot is two flat un-outlined ellipses plus a flat green disc lily pad (also flat `mud` and straw discs); hen, horse, cow, pig stand on the water with dry feet — the "flat disc used as a puddle" `docs/VISUAL-QA.md` forbids. **Fix:** `moo-farm.js` `makeSpot` `kind === 'pond'` — replace the `disc()` stack with a sculpted `prop_farmpond` in `tools/blender/creatures/prop_farmkit.py` (raised grassy rim, inset water, modelled lily pad); sink feet ~0.06 with a ripple ring per leg.
6. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `moo-L0/shot01`, `moo-L1/shot01`, `moo-L2/shot09` — `voice()` sets the face to `'scared'` for 0.6 s on every call: the tapped animal (cow, pig, duck) looks frightened; the sheep looks alarmed in the finale chorus. **Fix:** as AUDIT §1 "Common, wrong tap" (`setFace(f, 'joy')`); raw alternative: add an open-mouth `sing` face in `engine/actors.js`. ↔ AUDIT §1 "Common, wrong tap" (scared row)
7. **[P2]** L3 · `moo-L2/shot05` — round change: new spots pop in while old animals/spots are still popping out (`popOut` first inflates to 112 %); for ~0.2 s the horse stands inside the new barn wall, the cow in the hay cart, tree + trough + apple + pumpkins pile onto sheep and pig. **Fix:** `moo.js` `startRound` — after starting the `popOut`s add `if (this.animals.length) await this.wait(0.24);` before creating new spots/animals (as `share.js` does). ↔ AUDIT §1 "Common, `afterSuccess` / `startRound`" (audit replaces the pop-out with tray walk + `fadeAway(…, 0.5)`; the wait must cover that exit)
8. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `moo-L1/shot01`, `moo-L1/shot06`, `moo-L2/shot03` — `barn` spot: `prop_barnwall` is a flat red gable slab, kennel-sized (no taller than the cow); in the left slot its eave lands on Pip's hay bale and feet, and it hides the silo and both background trees. **Fix:** `moo.js` `startRound` spot shuffle — never assign `barn` to slot 0; rebuild `barnwall()` in `prop_farmkit.py` as an open double door with plank lines, 3.4 wide × 3.6 tall.
9. **[P2]** L3 · `moo-L2/shot03`, `moo-L2/shot06`, `moo-L2/shot08` — `cart` spot: hay cart centred behind the animal and almost hidden — hay tufts beside the hen's head like horns, wheel between the cow's arm and body, shafts poking into the neighbouring tree trunk / barn wall / past the horse's arm. **Fix:** `moo-farm.js` `makeSpot` `kind === 'cart'` — `prop('prop_haycart', 0, 0, -1.65)` → `(1.35, 0, -1.9, undefined, 0.5)` (offset like the trough, shafts to the back).
10. **[P2]** L1, L3 · `moo-L0/shot01`, `moo-L2/shot06`, `moo-L2/shot08` — `tree` spot: tree shorter than the animal (canopy level with the cow's ear, trunk as thick as her arm; ear and left arm overlap canopy and trunk); in L3 it lands directly behind the duck's head and as a green blob glued to the cow's barn wall. **Fix:** `moo-farm.js` `makeSpot` `kind === 'tree'` — `makeTree(0.62)` at `(-1.75, 0, -2.6)` → `makeTree(1.15)` at `(-2.5, 0, -3.6)`; `moo.js` `startRound` — filter `tree` out of `this.pool` when `kinds.length === 4`.
11. **[P2]** L2 · `moo-L1/shot06`, `moo-L1/shot02`, `moo-L1/shot04` — `pen` spot narrower than its occupant: hen's wings cover both side rails and the right post, duck's wing lies across the right rail, pig's hooves sit on the rails. **Fix:** `prop_farmkit.py` `penfence()` — posts x ±1.4 → ±1.85 (back rail to match), or scale the prop ×1.3 in `makeSpot`.
12. **[P2]** L3 · `moo-L2/shot01`, `moo-L2/shot04`, `moo-L2/shot07` — `hedge` spot: hedge block is invisible against the identical garden-hedge backdrop and its two bushes crowd neighbours (over the hen's tail, over the trough end). **Fix:** `moo-farm.js` `SPOT_POOLS[2]` — remove `'hedge'`, or make it a flower arch behind the animal only.
13. **[P2]** L3 · `moo-L2/shot01`, `moo-L2/shot08` — `pumpkins` spot: horse's and pig's hooves rest on the pumpkin stems (pumpkins inside the arm span); in slot 0 the left pumpkin overlaps Pip's bale. **Fix:** `moo-farm.js` `makeSpot` pumpkins `[[-1.5,-0.8],[1.55,-0.95],[1.45,0.55]]` → `[[-1.2,-1.3],[1.25,-1.4],[1.1,0.75]]`.
14. **[P2]** L1 · `moo-L0/shot07` — when the non-target horse turns to the winner, its far eye hangs half outside the head silhouette. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/horse.py` eyes — inset/decal instead of protruding domes; until then `moo.js` `tapAnimal` turn `* 0.6` → `* 0.3`.
15. **[P3]** L1–L3 · `moo-L0/shot03`, `moo-L1/shot08`, `moo-L2/shot06` — signature `puddle()` lays a second flat, hard-edged, un-outlined disc on the existing spot (blue over the flower spot, dirt yard and pumpkin patch disc; brown under the tree spot and on the flower spot). **Fix:** `moo-farm.js` `puddle()` — use the sculpted puddle prop of #5 at scale 0.7; skip when the spot is already `pond`/`mud`.
16. **[P3]** L1 · `moo-L0/shot06` — `hay` spot: both bales sit at the cow's hoof tips; each hoof overlaps a bale face (looks like gripping). **Fix:** `moo-farm.js` `makeSpot` `kind === 'hay'` — bale x `1.5 / -1.55` → `1.95 / -2.0` (or z −1.1 → −1.7).
17. **[P3]** L1, L3 · `moo-L0/shot03`, `moo-L2/shot08` — rained treats fall in front of the face: bread roll over the duck's cheek/eye, clover on the cow's horn and mouth. **Fix:** `moo.js` `giveTreats` — start x `to.x ± 0.2` → `to.x ± 0.9` with `|x| ≥ 0.6`, z `to.z + 0.3`. Conflicts with AUDIT §1 "Common, right tap": audit deletes `giveTreats` (item moot if that lands).
18. **[P3]** L1 · `moo-L0/shot08` — finale chorus: `♪` glyph drawn on the horse's forehead. **Fix:** `moo.js` `head()` / `voice()` — float the note at `head(a) + (0.7, 0.9, 0)`. ↔ AUDIT X3 / §1 "Ending" (audit deletes the automatic `onFinale` chorus; offset still needed for `voice()` in `onAdmirePoke`)
19. **[P3]** L2 · `moo-L1/shot06` — "Cluck!" word and its `♪` land on Pip's wing and the silo, with a second `♪` by the caption and the shrinking bubble still visible: four overlapping call effects around Pip. **Fix:** `moo.js` `tapAnimal` — skip the extra `fx.float('♪')` in `voice()` when `this.solved`; float `SHOUT[k]` at `head(a) + (0.9, 0.4, 0)`. ↔ AUDIT X2
20. **[P3]** L1–L3 · `moo-L0/shot08`, `moo-L1/shot09`, `moo-L2/shot09` — engine: finale caption "Look what you made!" in a game where the child made nothing. **Fix:** `app/src/engine/lesson.js` `admire()` — `this.admireText ?? 'Look what you made!'`; `moo.js` sets `admireText = 'Hello, farm friends!'`. ↔ AUDIT X1 (audit adds a keepsake tray; the override may be unnecessary once it lands)
21. **[P3]** L1–L3 · `moo-L0/shot05`, `moo-L1/shot08`, `moo-L2/shot08` — sheep: thin stray ink lines cross the wool at neck and chest. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/sheep.py` wool layer — raise the outline crease threshold / merge the tufts so no interior edges are inked.
22. **[P3]** L1 · `moo-L0/shot01` — sound bubble jammed between sun and silo (edge touches the sun's rays, covers the silo roof). **Fix:** `moo.js` `BUBBLE_AT` `(-4.0, 5.0, -3.4)` → `(-3.6, 4.4, -3.4)`.
23. **[P3]** L2 · `moo-L1/shot08` — `prop_trough`: lower-right corner ragged/melted, plank line a wobbly dashed scribble. **Fix:** `prop_farmkit.py` `trough()` — clean bevel; two crisp `layer()` plank grooves.
24. **[P3]** L2 · `moo-L1/shot02`, `moo-L1/shot06` — hen and duck feet outlined with thin black scribbles around the toes; hairline across the top of the hen's bib. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/hen.py` / duck model — toes ≥ 0.05 thick so the hull outline closes; remove the bib seam edge from inking.
25. **[P3]** L3 · `moo-L2/shot03` — egg's `✨` sparkles float over the hen's bib, not the egg. **Fix:** `moo-farm.js` `SIGNATURE.hen` — `fx.float(egg.position + (0, 0.5, 0))` → `+ (0.5, 0.15, 0.3)`.
26. **[P3]** L3 · all shots — garden lanterns on the back hedge are code primitives (yellow cylinder + red cone), reading as tiny houses. **Fix:** `moo-farm.js` `makeBackdrop` level 2 — replace `geo.cyl` + `geo.cone` with a small Blender lantern (`prop_farmkit.py`).
27. **[P3]** L3 · `moo-L2/shot08` — pig: flat shading facets across cheek and body. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/pig.py` — one more smooth pass / smooth normals.

### share — Share the snacks
Captures: `share-L0` (01–07), `share-L0-short` (01–16), `share-L1` (01–09), `share-L1-short` (01–16), `share-L2` (01–11), `share-L2-short` (01–16). All shots post-date the last edit of `share.js` / `share-props.js`; nothing stale. Engine item recorded by group A, referenced only: bunny outline breaking up on white fur (every bunny round).

1. **[P1]** L2 · all shots — Pip is embedded in the barn wall: head, a wing, belly patch and one foot poke through the red planks with plank lines drawn across them; on a hop his hat sits on the top beam (Pip z −3.0; wall z −2.65 ± 0.15 spanning his x). **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/share.js` `build()` — for `this.level === 1` `this.pip.position.set(-6.6, 0, -1.7)`, or in `app/src/lessons/share-props.js` `makeSet` level 1 start the wall right of x −5.6.
2. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `share-L1-short/shot16`, `share-L2/shot03`, `share-L0-short/shot05` — with three friends the waiting snack hovers over the left plate/bowl: covers half of it and the snack already in it (carrot L2; fish, apple, cookie L3; overlaps the left rim at L1). **Fix:** `share.js` `SPAWN_Y` — `carrot: 1.95` → `1.3`, `corn: 1.9` → `1.3`, `SNACKS.*.held` `0.3` → `1.2`; for n = 3 place the bin at `spawnAt.x = -1.75`, z `5.6`. (Raw also asks `CRATE` z 4.9 → 3.9 in #6 — reconcile.)
3. **[P1]** L2–L3 · `share-L1/shot08`, `share-L2/shot05`, `share-L2-short/shot11` — carrots on one plate pass through each other: end-to-end along x only 0.5 apart, greens cut through the next body (L2: two tufts, one carrot; L3: three tufts, one chain, overflowing the leaf plate both sides and covering the bunny's legs). **Fix:** `share-props.js` `SNACKS.carrot` — `lay: [0, 0.3, 1.45]`, `gap: 0.5` → `lay: [0, 1.35, 1.45]`, `gap: 0.42`, `size: 0.6` (side by side, tips toward the child); same for `corn`.
4. **[P1]** L2 · `share-L1-short/shot02` — when the bowl pulses gold (×1.18) its back rim cuts into the pig (belly shows through a gap in the rim). **Fix:** `share.js` `drop()` — `p.scale.setScalar(1 + Math.sin(q * Math.PI) * 0.18)` → scale y only, or 0.06; plus plate z 1.35 (#10).
5. **[P1]** L3 · all shots — party rug's back-right edge broken by jagged green/grey slivers (z-fighting with the meadow ground patches; rug y 0.012/0.016); dark rim flickers along that edge. **Fix:** `share-props.js` `makeSet` level 2 — rug y `0.012` / `0.016` → `0.05` / `0.055`, or `polygonOffset` on both rug materials.
6. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `share-L0/shot02`, `share-L1/shot01`, `share-L2/shot08` — the bin the child drags from is cut by the bottom of the frame (base and picture label sliced off; in the L3 four-friend round the basket is at the bottom-left corner) and sits half on the blanket, half on the bare path. **Fix:** `share.js` `CRATE` `(0, 0, 4.9)` → `(0, 0, 3.9)` and `SPAWN` z to match.
7. **[P2]** L1–L3 · all shots — framing: friends ~190 px (25 % of frame) at the very back; ~40 % of the picture is empty blanket between plates and bin. **Fix:** `share.js` `build()` — with the bin moved (#6), `frame(0, 5.2, 11.8, 0, 1.3, 1.2)` → about `frame(0, 4.4, 9.8, 0, 1.3, 0.9)`.
8. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `share-L0-short/shot06`, `share-L1/shot08`, `share-L2-short/shot11` — the count numeral floating up after each snack is drawn over the friend (grey "1" across the hen's eye, on the pig's snout, "2" on the bunny's nose, "3" on the bunny's legs); half-faded it is unreadable. **Fix:** `share.js` `drop()` — `fx.float(u.plate.position + (0, 1.0, 0.4), …)` → `+ (0, 0.45, 1.3)`.
9. **[P2]** L1 · `share-L0/shot01`, `share-L0-short/shot02` — a carrot does not fit its plate: body spans the inner well, leaf clump hangs over the rim onto the blanket. **Fix:** `share-props.js` `SNACKS.carrot` — `size: 0.8` → `0.6` (plate inner radius `rr` 0.8; carrot ≈ 1.3 long at scale 1).
10. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `share-L1/shot06`, `share-L0-short/shot01`, `share-L1/shot05` — plates hide the friends' feet and lower legs (plate back rim z 0, friends z −0.6); the 0.32-high L2 bowls hide them from the waist down (hen appears to sit in the bowl). **Fix:** `share.js` `startRound` — plate z `1.0` → `1.35`; `style === 'bowl'` → 1.5.
11. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `share-L0-short/shot08`, `share-L1/shot03`, `share-L2/shot02` — snacks shrink sharply on landing: fish ~100 px while waiting → ~30 px speck on the plate, unreadable. **Fix:** `share-props.js` `SNACKS.fish` — `size: 0.7` → `1.0`, `lay: [-1.3, 0, 0]` → `[-0.9, 0, 0]` (side to camera).
12. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `share-L0/shot04`, `share-L1/shot03`, `share-L2/shot02` — fish in the pail read as blue striped capsules (no visible tail; crossed blue/white/orange rectangle markings). **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_fish.py` — rounder body, big tail fin and side fin as shapes, one curved belly colour area; `share-props.js` `makeBin` — stand them tail-up or lay them on their sides.
13. **[P2]** L2 · all shots — "inside the barn" set is a 2.3-high red slab with dark exposed end caps, the outdoor farm visible above it, on a flat yellow slab floor with zig-zag straw strokes; half the frame is plain yellow. **Fix:** `share-props.js` `makeSet` level 1 — wall height 2.3 → 4.6, x −10…10, with a hay-loft beam; Blender straw-strewn plank floor instead of `geo.box` + 70 box straws.
14. **[P2]** L2 · `share-L1/shot06`, `share-L1/shot02`, `share-L1-short/shot12` — the two lamps are code primitives (box hook, yellow cylinder, black cone) hanging exactly behind the outer friends' heads in 3-friend rounds: on the hen's comb like a hat, between the left bunny's ears. **Fix:** `share-props.js` `makeSet` level 1 — Blender lantern prop hung from the trim at y 2.9, set x −0.75 and 2.75.
15. **[P2]** L3 · all shots — rug is a flat two-tone lilac ellipse whose front is sliced in a dead-straight line by the path strip (y ≈ 583 px, full width); the bin stands on the bare path. **Fix:** `share.js` `build()` — do not apply the `z * 1.45 + 1.19` stretch to the level-2 rug (front edge z ≤ 4.3), move the bin onto it (#6); `share-props.js` — fringe/scalloped border prop.
16. **[P2]** L3 · all shots — left bunting pole stands in front of Pip, cutting across his hat brim and right wing. **Fix:** `share-props.js` `makeSet` level 2 — poles set x `-4.6 / 7.6` → `-7.2 / 9.2`, or `share.js` `build()` Pip to `(-7.6, 0, -2.2)` for level 2.
17. **[P2]** L3 · `share-L2/shot08`, `share-L2-short/shot16` — four-friend party: left bunny (x −4.5) stands in front of Pip, covering his wing and half his body, pole between them. **Fix:** `share.js` `XS[4]` `[-4.5, -1.5, 1.5, 4.5]` → `[-3.6, -1.2, 1.2, 3.6]`, plates scaled 0.82.
18. **[P2]** L3 · `share-L2/shot08`, `share-L2-short/shot16` — four leaf plates in a row touch: each stem runs into the next leaf's tip (one joined chain). **Fix:** `share.js` `startRound` — for `style === 'leaf'` `plate.rotation.y = Math.PI / 2` (stems toward the child) or scale 0.82 when n = 4.
19. **[P2]** L3 · `share-L2/shot09`, `share-L2/shot04`, `share-L2-short/shot01` — slot rings on the leaf plates are broken: one bisected by the midrib, the other a "c" arc buried in the curved leaf; reads as "OC". **Fix:** `share-props.js` `makePlate` — for the Blender leaf raise slots to `floorY + 0.04`; `depthTest: false`, `renderOrder: 2` on `slotMat`.
20. **[P2]** L3 · `share-L2/shot07`, `share-L2/shot11`, `share-L2-short/shot15` — "gold glow" under a full plate is a flat beige ellipse on the lilac rug; reads as a stain. **Fix:** `share-props.js` `makePlate` `glowMat` — `0xfff27a` normal blend → `0xfff9c4` with `T.AdditiveBlending` and a radial-gradient alpha texture instead of hard-edged `geo.circle(1.45)`.
21. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `share-L1-short/shot11`, `share-L1/shot05`, `share-L2-short/shot15` — cheer pose: hens' wings swing up across the chest and over beak/wattle; pigs' arms swing ~120° up and vanish behind the head (two armless pigs). **Fix:** `share.js` `update()` — in `a.rotation.z = u.limbR0[i] + (i ? 1 : -1) * (u.reach * 1.5 + flap)` clamp `u.reach * 1.5` to ≤ 0.7 rad for `hen`, ≤ 1.1 for the others.
22. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `share-L1/shot04`, `share-L2-short/shot04` — bin swap: old bin still shrinking inside/beside the new one (blue fish pail inside the red corn crate; corn crate beside the fish pail); in L3 a pre-filled fish lies on a plate whose cat has not appeared yet. **Fix:** `share.js` `setBin()` — make it async, `await popOut(old)` before adding the new bin, await it in `startRound`; call `placeCarrot(b, true)` only after that friend's `popIn`.
23. **[P2]** L1 · all shots — a purple tulip grows through the picnic blanket at the left (flower at set x −4.2, z 2.9; blanket stretched by `build()` to z −1.35…4.75). **Fix:** `share-props.js` `makeSet` level 0 — flower `[-4.2, 2.9, 0xa77bff]` → `[-7.4, 2.9, 0xa77bff]`, or remove it.
24. **[P2]** L1 · `share-L0-short/shot11` — hop landing squash on the hen is extreme: ~65 % height, head sunk into the body. **Fix:** `share.js` `drop()` / `payoff()` — for `u.kind === 'hen'` `hop(this.s, b, 0.25, 0.5)` and `b.userData.noSquash = true`, or cap the landing squash in `engine/actors.js` `hop` at 12 %.
25. **[P2]** L1–L3 · all bunny shots — bunnies' bows are two squashed code spheres on the head; on a head turn the bow sits mid-forehead. **Fix:** `share.js` `makeFriend` — replace the `geo.sphere` bow with a Blender bow prop (two loops + knot) parented at `(0.42, 0.62, 0.1)` at the ear base.
26. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `share-L0/shot02`, `share-L1/shot01`, `share-L2/shot06` — `prop_wicker` basket: weave paint is a ragged zig-zag of patches, straw lining has jagged black ink marks; the cookies in it have a visibly polygonal outline. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_wicker.py` — 3 clean horizontal weave bands, crisp rim, smooth lining; `prop_cookie.py` — 24+ segments.
27. **[P3]** L1–L3 · `share-L0-short/shot02`, `share-L1-short/shot02`, `share-L2-short/shot11` — the gold `=` between plates lands on the picket fence/grass/wall and reads as a small crate or orange brick. **Fix:** as AUDIT §2 "Common, last snack" (delete the `=` floats); raw alternative if kept: `share.js` `drop()` float at `y + 0.35, z + 1.0`, `size: 9` → `11`. ↔ AUDIT §2 "Common, last snack" / X2
28. **[P3]** L1–L3 · `share-L0/shot07`, `share-L1/shot09`, `share-L2/shot11` — engine: finale "Look what you made!" over empty plates/bowls/leaves (everything was eaten). **Fix:** `engine/lesson.js` `admire()` text override (moo #20); `share.js` — re-fill every plate before the admire step or `admireText = 'Everyone is full!'`. ↔ AUDIT X1 / §2 "Common, round change" (audit adds a rug tray of fed friends)
29. **[P3]** L1–L3 · `share-L0/shot06`, `share-L1-short/shot01`, `share-L2/shot10` — the snack's arc to the plate passes in front of the friend (apple over the pig's chin/chest, carrot across the bunny's chest, corn on the hen's wing/chest, fish at the cat's paw). **Fix:** `share.js` `placeCarrot` — arc height `1.0` → `0.6`; ease z first (reach the plate's z by p = 0.5).
30. **[P3]** L1 · `share-L0/shot01` — carrot greens: stray dark ink squiggles inside the leaf clump. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_carrot.py` — greens as 3 clean leaf shapes, no interior outline.
31. **[P3]** L1–L2 · `share-L0/shot04`, `share-L1-short/shot06` — engine: on a hop the cat's ears flop completely flat and the eyes squint; one of two identical cats looks like a different animal. **Fix:** `app/src/engine/creature.js` ear bounce — clamp ear swing for `cat` to ±0.25 rad.
32. **[P3]** L1 · all shots — blanket is a perfectly flat plane whose checks are stretched to 0.42 × 0.61 rectangles by the z ×1.45 stretch in `build()`. **Fix:** `share-props.js` `makeSet` level 0 — build the plane at final size (14 × 6.1) with `map.repeat.set(3.3, 1.45)`; add a soft scalloped edge.
33. **[P3]** L2 · `share-L1/shot04`, `share-L1-short/shot01`, `share-L1-short/shot06` — "how many" slot rings (`0x3a2838` at 55 %) barely visible on the dark-brown bowl floor. **Fix:** `share-props.js` `makePlate` — for `style === 'bowl'` cream slot colour (`0xfff6e6`, opacity 0.8).
34. **[P3]** L3 · `share-L2-short/shot15` — the three `♥` floats rise into the bunting and sit half-transparent on the flags. **Fix:** as AUDIT §2 "Common, last snack" (delete the hearts); raw alternative if kept: `share.js` `payoff()` hearts at `x.position + (0, 2.6, 0.6)` instead of `(0, 3.4, 0)`. ↔ AUDIT §2 "Common, last snack" / X2

### band — Barn band
Captures: `band-L0` (15), `band-L1` (7) + `band-L1-short` (16), `band-L2` (8) + `band-L2-short` (16). `band.js` / `band-sound.js` were saved at 18:25, during the `band-L1` main capture and after `band-L0` and `band-L1-short`; every item was re-checked against current code (seat/stage/prop constants) and cross-level items re-seen in `band-L2*` (post-edit). Engine items recorded by group A, referenced only: clouds across the sun (`band-L0/shot07`–`shot10`), photo card and sticker badge over the scene (`band-L0/shot14`, `shot15`), sticker badge over the board (`band-L1-short/shot16`).

1. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `band-L0/shot11`, `band-L1/shot02`, `band-L2/shot04` — the seat-0 friend is hidden: L1 behind the third hay bale (left half cut off every frame; 3-dot board post in front of the hen's face); L2–L3 behind the quiet instrument (small cowbell covers the cow, stand bar across the sheep's mouth, post down the horse's face). The code comment "no friend stands behind it" is untrue on screen. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/band.js` `SEATS[0]` `[5.0, -3.4]` → about `[5.9, -2.4]` (raw L2: `[5.9, -2.2]`), clear of the post at `x = 1.4 + w/2 - 0.25` (up to 5.15 for 4 dots), or `POS.s` x 3.4 → 2.9; `app/src/lessons/band-props.js` `makeStage()` level 0 bale `[4.1, -2.75, 0.85, 0.15]` → e.g. `[3.2, -3.6]` or delete.
2. **[P1]** L1, L3 · `band-L0/shot10`, `band-L2-short/shot08` — round change: old and new things occupy the same spot at once — two beat boards superimposed (double frame, two posts, old "1" dot in the new 3-dot row), Pip's old pumpkin drum inside his new bucket drum / bell stand, pumpkins inside the cowbell stands, caption still "What a band!". **Fix:** `band.js` `setInstruments()` and `startRound()` — `await popOut(...)` of the old drums / `pipInst` / `this.dots` before adding and `popIn`-ing the new ones (currently `popOut(…0.18)` and `popIn` start in the same frame).
3. **[P1]** L1, L3 · `band-L0/shot03`, `band-L0/shot09`, `band-L2/shot04` — pumpkin drum (main tap target for 2 of 6 L1 rounds; also the quiet instrument at L3) is a squashed orange blob with thick black ink strokes starting and stopping at random along the creases, drum skin a small floating disc; reads as a placeholder beside the red drum. **Fix:** `band-props.js` `pumpkin()` — stop the non-uniform `pk.scale.set(r/0.55, h/0.8, r/0.55)`; build a Blender `prop_pumpkindrum` (clean lobes, creases as paint not outline, skin lashed on with cord).
4. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `band-L0/shot03`, `band-L1/shot02`, `band-L2/shot01` — the beat board stands on one post under its right end; nothing under the left corner (in `band-L1/shot02` the big cowbell's stand looks like the missing post). `tools/blender/creatures/prop_farmkit.py` `beatboard()` defines two posts (`x = ±(W/2 - 0.25)`). **Fix:** inspect `prop_beatboard` in the viewer, restore the left post (lost in the bake or culled), re-export; keep both posts when `makeDots()` stretches it with `size: [w/6, 1, 1]`.
5. **[P1]** L2–L3 · `band-L1/shot01`, `band-L1/shot04`, `band-L2/shot05` — the wooden spoons float in mid-air above the pots, held by nobody (big spoon bowl as large as the cow's head, ~1 unit over the rim; small pot's spoon in front of the horse's legs; Pip's beside him). **Fix:** `band-props.js` `pot()` `spoon.position.set(r + len*0.55, h + len*0.75, 0.3)`, `rest = -1.35` — rest the spoon on the rim (pivot at rim height, handle leaning out) and tap from there; put Pip's spoon in his wing.
6. **[P1]** L2 · `band-L1/shot01`–`shot07`, `band-L1-short/shot01`–`shot16` — the seat-1 friend is cut by the right curtain (pig's right arm and ear, sheep's flank, half the chick behind the red folds); a scenery hay-bale end pokes out beside the chick. **Fix:** `band.js` `SEATS[1]` `[7.2, -2.2]` → `[6.2, -1.6]` for level 1, or `band-props.js` `makeStage()` level 1 curtain `1.4 + s * (7.8 - i*0.45)` → `8.8`.
7. **[P1]** L2–L3 · `band-L1/shot05`, `band-L1-short/shot10`, `band-L2/shot04` — bits on the quiet instrument stack in a tower: three apples on the small churn (lid 0.3 wide) cover the sheep's face all round; eggs on the small pumpkin drum balance egg on egg, a third in front of the horse. **Fix:** `band-props.js` `surface()` churn `[u.h - 0.05, u.r * 1.0]` and `band.js` `placeBits()` `n = d.userData.kind === 'B' ? 4 : 3` — quiet churn/pumpkin gets 1 bit (or 2 at 0.6 scale); widen the catcher to the lid radius without the knob box.
8. **[P1]** L3 · `band-L2/shot01`, `band-L2-short/shot01`–`shot03` — buttons on the small churn stand upright on their edges, stacked, covering the pig's snout (flat discs with ball colliders). **Fix:** `band.js` `placeBits()` `physics.add(b, { shape: 'ball' … })` — default hull for `buttons`; count as #7.
9. **[P1]** L3 · `band-L2-short/shot15` — at "Your turn!" the instruments hop up through the peas; on both buckets only green flecks stick out of the drum head. **Fix:** `band.js` `prompt()` `hop(this.s, d, 0.25, 0.5)` — squash on the spot instead, or `jumpBits(d, false)` in the same frame.
10. **[P1]** L3 · `band-L2/shot01`–`shot08`, `band-L2-short/shot01`–`shot16` — the "evening concert" is two bare black sticks and loose coloured dots: bulbs float with no wire, several on the windmill and one on the sun's face; sky is the daytime rain/rainbow sky; the left stick appears to grow out of the back of Pip's drum. **Fix:** `band-props.js` `makeStage()` level 2 — Blender `prop_fairylights` (two turned posts, sagging cable, bulbs in sockets), posts at x −5.2 / 9.2; ask the engine for a dusk sky for this level.
11. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `band-L0/shot01`–`shot15`, `band-L1/shot01`, `band-L2/shot01`–`shot08` — the seat-2 friend stands directly behind the big instrument: only head and one hand above the drum (pig, cow); chick shows only eyes over the big pot rim; horse cut by the bell stand post (`band-L1/shot06`). **Fix:** `band.js` `SEATS[2]` `[-2.6, -3.6]` (`[-2.6, -1.9]` is still behind the drum in screen space) — put it on a hay-bale riser (y ≈ 0.9) or move to `x ≈ -2.9, z ≈ -4.2` raised.
12. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `band-L0/shot09`, `band-L1/shot02`, `band-L2/shot06` — when struck the whole instrument changes colour (pumpkins lemon yellow, red bucket pink, small gold bell white, steel pot cream, buckets orange); reads as a different object, not a flash. **Fix:** `band-props.js` `strike()` / `pumpkin()` / `bucket()` — only the drum-head material in `mats`; cap emissive at ~0.35.
13. **[P2]** L1, L3 · `band-L0/shot05`, `band-L0/shot11`, `band-L2/shot07` — bucket drum's yellow handle lies flat on the floor as a ring round the base (reads as a plate/puddle, passes through the bucket wall); the head is the same flat colour as the body with a stray black arc. **Fix:** `band-props.js` `bucket()` `pm.userData.bones.handle.rotation.x = 1.85` — hang the bail down the front wall (≈ 3.0) or hide the handle layer; add a cream head disc like `makeDrum`'s.
14. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `band-L0/shot07`, `band-L1/shot02`, `band-L2/shot01`–`shot08` — Pip never touches his instrument: no sticks, small drum in front of his tummy, a beat is only a raised wing; at L2 his bell stand is pushed against his wing (post touches it, foot on the stage corner). **Fix:** `band.js` `pipHit()` — two drumsticks (Blender prop parented to the wing bones); swing the wing down onto `pipInst`'s head (`w.rotation.z` towards the drum, not `0.3 + 1.5` upward).
15. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `band-L0/shot01`–`shot15`, `band-L1/shot01`–`shot07`, `band-L2/shot01`–`shot08` — framing: big drum ~190 px of 772 (25 %; instruments ~22 % at L2), bottom 27 % empty planks + flat orange band. **Fix:** `band.js` `build()` `frame(0.4, 3.6, 13, 0.4, 2.3, 0)` → about `frame(0.8, 3.0, 10.5, 0.8, 2.0, 0)` (board top still inside).
16. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `band-L1/shot02`, `band-L1/shot06`, `band-L2/shot03` — the big cowbell's stand arm lies exactly on the beat board's bottom frame and its post lines up with the board edge (one tangled structure); the red tree sits right behind both. **Fix:** `band-props.js` `cowbell()` `H = 3.3 * s` → 2.7, or `band.js` `DOTS_AT` y 4.1 → 4.6.
17. **[P2]** L2 · `band-L1/shot01`–`shot07` — the theatre is code primitives: curtain folds are plain cylinders, pelmet is squashed spheres on a yellow box whose ends show as black slabs past the curtains. **Fix:** `band-props.js` `makeStage()` level 1 — Blender `prop_theatre` (draped curtains with tie-backs, scalloped valance, turned wooden proscenium).
18. **[P2]** L2 · `band-L1/shot01`–`shot07` — the theatre has no back: barn, silo, trees, windmill show through; red tree directly behind the board, windmill blades above the pelmet, sun cut in half by it. **Fix:** `band-props.js` `makeStage()` level 1 — backdrop cloth (plane at z −2.2, 14 × 5.6) or `calmBackdrop(…, { hide })` the trees/windmill.
19. **[P2]** L2 · `band-L1/shot06`, `band-L1/shot07`, `band-L1-short/shot13` — the chick fan is less than half the size of the other fans, tucked in the corner by the curtain; reads as a stray object. **Fix:** `band.js` `FAN_SIZE.chick` 0.95 → 1.4.
20. **[P2]** L3 · `band-L2/shot01`–`shot08` — the round stage is a flat purple ellipse with a yellow line; the two "spotlight pools" are flat lilac ovals reading as puddles, not centred under the instruments. **Fix:** `band-props.js` `makeStage()` level 2 — Blender round stage with a stepped edge; soft radial-gradient pools centred on `POS.B` / `POS.s`.
21. **[P2]** L3 · `band-L2/shot03`, `band-L2-short/shot06`, `band-L2-short/shot07` — the seat-1 friend is jammed between the right black pole and the hay-bale stack; the sheep's hand overlaps the pole. **Fix:** `band.js` `SEATS[1]` → `[6.4, -1.8]` for level 2; pole to x 9.2.
22. **[P2]** L3 · `band-L2/shot01`, `band-L2/shot02` — engine: Pip's blue hat sits so low the brim cuts off the top of his eyes. **Fix:** engine hat fit for the blue hat (raise ~0.08 or tilt back); report to engine owner.
23. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `band-L1-short/shot14`, `band-L2-short/shot03`, `band-L2-short/shot13` — "notes" payoff scatters eight faint notes over the beat board and tree, some on the board frame; at L3 over the "1" on the beat dot and over the cow. **Fix:** as AUDIT §3 "Common, last beat" (delete `payoff()`); raw alternative if kept: `band.js` `payoff('notes')` 3 notes per drum, start at `d.userData.h + 0.4`, keep below y 3. ↔ AUDIT §3 "Common, last beat" / X2
24. **[P3]** L1 · `band-L0/shot02`, `band-L0/shot07`, `band-L0/shot09` — butterflies/birds fly through the play area at instrument and board height (bird on the board frame, butterfly under the board); `calmBackdrop` does not remove them. **Fix:** `band.js` `build()` `calmBackdrop(this.meadow, { hide })` — hide the ambient flyers.
25. **[P3]** L1 · `band-L0/shot05`–`shot15` — horse and pig fans hold a stiff arms-straight-out pose all level; the horse's hand touches the hay stack. **Fix:** `band.js` `makeFan()` — relaxed rest pose (`u.arms` rotation.z ±0.9) for horse/pig.
26. **[P3]** L1 · `band-L0/shot05`, `band-L0/shot07`, `band-L0/shot12` — the breathing loud dot grows until it touches the panel's top and bottom edge. **Fix:** `band.js` `update()` `1 + … * 0.15` → 0.08 for loud dots (r 0.74).
27. **[P3]** L1 · `band-L0/shot01` — Pip's small drum straddles the left stage edge: half on the planks (top at y 0.05, base sunk into them), half over grass. **Fix:** `band.js` `PIP_INST` x −4.2 → −3.7 and y 0.05, or extend `planks(g, 12, …)` 1 unit left.
28. **[P3]** L1 · `band-L0/shot06` — two apples on the bucket intersect (model larger than its physics ball). **Fix:** `band-props.js` `BITS.apples.r` 0.2 → match `makeApple(0.62)` (~0.26).
29. **[P3]** L2 · `band-L1-short/shot03`, `band-L1-short/shot07` — at the round change last round's bits pop away in front of the cow's face while new ones fall past it. **Fix:** covered by #1 (seat) and #2 (sequence the swap).
30. **[P3]** L3 · `band-L2/shot07`, `band-L2/shot08`, `band-L2-short/shot14` — the 4-dot board (8 units) reaches the left pole and hides all fairy lights and the windmill hub. **Fix:** `band-props.js` `makeDots()` `gap = 1.85` → 1.55 for 4 beats.

### pots — Peekaboo pots
Captures: `pots-L0` (8) + `pots-L0-short` (16), `pots-L1` (10) + `pots-L1-short` (16), `pots-L2` (11) + `pots-L2-short` (16). `pots.js` (14:51) and `pots-props.js` pre-date all captures; nothing stale. Engine items recorded by group A, referenced only: clouds across the sun (`pots-L0/shot03`, `shot04`, `pots-L0-short/shot07`–`shot10`, L2), sticker badge (`pots-L0/shot08`, `pots-L2/shot11`), confetti + sticker badge at the finale (`pots-L1/shot10`).

1. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `pots-L0-short/shot13`, `pots-L1/shot07`, `pots-L2/shot05` (flights; otherwise between consecutive shots) — every found friend ZIPs to Pip: 6–11 units in 0.9 s, peak 0.58–0.66 units/frame (35–40 units/s); up to 9.96 u at L2, 9.61 u at L3. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/pots.js` `jumpToPip()` `s.anim(0.9, …, Ease.inOut)` — 2–3 hops at ≤ 3.5 units/s (`dur = Math.max(1.0, dist / 3.5)`, one arc per ~2.5 units), or Pip hops over to the friend.
2. **[P1]** L1–L2 · `pots-L0-short/shot07`, `pots-L0-short/shot14`, `pots-L1/shot04` — the bucket cover's yellow handle is a horizontal ring stuck round the rim: lifted, it stays like a hat brim and cuts through the bucket wall; on the ground it reads as a yellow ring/plate. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/pots-props.js` `bucket()` `pm.userData.bones.handle.rotation.x = 1.85` — hang the bail against the front wall (≈ 3.0) or hide the handle layer; `tools/blender/creatures/prop_bucket.py` `LEAN`.
3. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `pots-L0-short/shot10`, `pots-L2/shot08`, `pots-L1-short/shot12` — the hidden friend is squashed to a flat pancake (face stretched) in the open while the cover is still a metre above it (chick under basket L1, under pumpkin L3; L2: proven from the same `tapPot()` code, kitten caught mid-pop under the lifted box). **Fix:** `pots.js` `tapPot()` `c.scale.set(base, base * 0.3, base)` + the 0.2 s wait, and `hideUnder()` `k = p.position.y / top` (`top` from `Box3.setFromObject` of a skinned creature is too tall) — crouch (lower `position.y` / uniform scale 0.8) only once the cover rim is within 0.2 of the head; per-kind height table.
4. **[P1]** L2 · `pots-L1/shot01`–`shot10`, `pots-L1-short/shot01`–`shot16` — Pip stands inside the end of the brown pen fence: picket tops come through his tummy, rails cross his legs in every frame. **Fix:** `pots-props.js` `makeSet()` level 1 `makeFence(15, …)` at `(0.3, 0, -2.2)` ends at x −7.2 = Pip's x (`pots.js` `this.pip.position.set(-7.2, 0, -2.6)`) — `makeFence(12)` centred at x 1.6, or Pip at z −1.0 in front of it.
5. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `pots-L1-short/shot09`, `pots-L2/shot02`, `pots-L0/shot06` — "jumps into Pip's arms": L2–L3 the friend covers Pip's face (chick over eyes and beak; hedgehog leaves one eye; bunny and kitten cover the beak); L1–L2 it ends hovering 0.3 above the ground in front of Pip's wing, overlapping wing and cheek, and after the 0.8 s wrap Pip's wings are back at his sides or pointing the other way. **Fix:** `pots.js` `jumpToPip()` `to = pip.position + (0.4, 0.3, 1.25)` — land on the ground beside Pip (`(1.3, 0, 0.6)`); Pip turns and keeps one wing round the friend until `afterSuccess()`.
6. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `pots-L2/shot07`, `pots-L2-short/shot01`, `pots-L0/shot02` — the friend's hello spot (`FRONT_Z = 3.4`) is wrong for every set: L3 behind the front veg row at z 3.9 (cabbage over the hedgehog's mouth and half the chick, carrot tops over kitten/bunny/chick feet and tummy); L1 in the sprout row (leaf pokes out of the bunny's side and the chick's underside); L2 right slot (x 4.3) off the straw floor on the path, bottom-right corner (`pots-L1-short/shot01`). **Fix:** `pots.js` `FRONT_Z` 3.4 → 2.7 (raw L3: 2.6); or `pots-props.js` `makeSet()` L1 sprout row `z = 3.5` → 4.3, L3 veg row → z 4.7 with gaps at `XS` x (−3.1, 0.6, 4.3); L2 floor `scale.set(7.4, 3.0, 1)` → `(7.8, 3.4, 1)`.
7. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `pots-L0/shot01`–`shot07`, `pots-L1/shot01`–`shot08`, `pots-L2/shot01`–`shot10` — framing: a cover is ~160 px of 772 (21 %), a friend ~17 %; top 40 % sky, bottom 12 % empty band; Pip pressed against the left edge. **Fix:** `pots.js` `build()` `setCamera((0, 3.6, 12.4), (0, 2.1, 0))` → about `(0.3, 3.3, 10.2)` looking at `(0.3, 1.9, 0.7)`; Pip x −7.2 → −5.9.
8. **[P2]** L2 · `pots-L1/shot01`–`shot10`, `pots-L1-short/shot01`–`shot16` — two striped triangular z-fighting artefacts at the front edge of the straw floor, bottom centre, in every frame. **Fix:** `pots-props.js` `makeSet()` level 1 `floor.position.set(0.3, 0.02, 0.7)` — raise to 0.05 / `polygonOffset`; keep the straw sticks (`st`) off the rim.
9. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `pots-L1-short/shot14`, `pots-L1-short/shot13`, `pots-L2/shot03` — weather is not calmed: at L2 a falling leaf lies across Pip's face like a second beak, one covers the sun's cheek, leaves fall in front of the covers and red/orange litter on the straw looks tappable; at L3 rain streaks fall in front of covers and friends (down the right barrel, across the baskets `pots-L2/shot05`). **Fix:** `pots.js` `build()` — call `calmBackdrop(this.meadow)` (`view-tools.js`) as band does.
10. **[P2]** L3 · `pots-L2/shot01`–`shot11` — the scarecrow is cut in half by the right frame edge; its post stands in front of/into the scenery hay bales. **Fix:** `pots-props.js` `makeSet()` level 2 `sc.position.set(8.4, 0, -2.6)` → `(6.6, 0, -3.4)`.
11. **[P2]** L3 · `pots-L2/shot01`, `pots-L2/shot03` — engine: Pip's blue hat brim cuts off the top of his eyes. **Fix:** engine hat fit (same as band #22).
12. **[P2]** L3 · `pots-L2/shot08`, `pots-L2-short/shot03`, `pots-L2-short/shot04` — the "hollow pumpkin" cover is solid: lifted, it shows a closed zig-zag underside; thick black ink on every crease. **Fix:** `pots-props.js` `pumpkin()` (`makePumpkin(2.25)`) — dedicated hollow half-pumpkin cover with a cut rim, creases as paint.
13. **[P2]** L1 · `pots-L0/shot01`–`shot08` — the garden bed is code primitives: flat brown box, sprouts of two squashed spheres, a row of identical grey pebbles. **Fix:** `pots-props.js` `makeSet()` level 0 — Blender bed with a raised timber edge, real seedlings, varied stones.
14. **[P2]** L2 · `pots-L1/shot01`–`shot10` — the pen is primitives: flat yellow ellipse with 60 identical stick strokes and a plain picket strip. **Fix:** `pots-props.js` `makeSet()` level 1 — Blender straw bedding with clumps, post-and-rail pen with a gate.
15. **[P2]** L1 · `pots-L0-short/shot09` — engine: seen from behind, the chick has an orange patch with a ragged, dashed black outline on its back. **Fix:** `engine/farm.js` `makeChick` (tail-feather paint/outline) — clean the paint edge or drop the outline on that layer.
16. **[P3]** L1–L2 · `pots-L0-short/shot03`, `pots-L1-short/shot03` — stale caption: "A snail! Where's Chick?" / "A sock! Where's Kitten?" still up while the friend is already revealed under the lifted cover. **Fix:** `pots.js` `tapPot()` — `this.say('Peekaboo!')` (or clear the caption) at the tap, not after `await up`. ↔ AUDIT §4 "Common, wrong tap" + "Common, right tap" (audit splits the combined line and voices "Peekaboo!" via `success()` after a 0.4 s beat; caption must still change at the tap)
17. **[P3]** L1–L3 · `pots-L0/shot01`, `pots-L1/shot02`, `pots-L2/shot04` — a lifted cover takes its blob shadow up with it: nothing on the ground under a 2.4-high floating pot; ball/friends have no ground shadow either. **Fix:** `pots.js` `lift()` — separate ground shadow per slot that shrinks as the cover rises.
18. **[P3]** L1 · `pots-L0-short/shot16` — at round end covers and friend just shrink to dots (tiny piglet by Pip's foot, three button-sized buckets). **Fix:** as AUDIT §4 "Common, `afterSuccess`" (friend walks to the fence tray, covers `fadeAway(q, 0.5)`); raw alternative: `popOut` with the friend waving first. ↔ AUDIT §4 "Common, `afterSuccess`" / X1
19. **[P3]** L1 · `pots-L0/shot02`, `pots-L0/shot04` — the upside-down basket's handles stick out at ground level like two brown ears pushed into the soil. **Fix:** `pots-props.js` `basket()` — hide the wicker handles when turned over.
20. **[P3]** L2 · `pots-L1/shot02` — the box's "this way up" arrow reads as a red keyhole / exclamation mark. **Fix:** `prop_box` sticker — a proper two-arrow "up" label.
21. **[P3]** L2–L3 · `pots-L1-short/shot05`, `pots-L1-short/shot14`, `pots-L2-short/shot09` — lifted covers overlap scenery: the left cover (x −3.1) hides the silo and stops a few pixels from Pip's hat; at L3 the right cover overlaps the scarecrow's arm and the windmill. **Fix:** `pots.js` `XS[3]` `[-3.1, 0.6, 4.3]` → `[-2.7, 0.9, 4.5]` after moving Pip (#7); L3 covered by #10.
22. **[P3]** L3 · `pots-L2-short/shot03` — the scanning hint hand stops on the frog's face. **Fix:** `pots.js` `playDemo()` / `demoL3()` — `fx.clearHands()` on any tap.

Teleports: L1 6 events, all ZIP (`jumpToPip()`, `pots.js` line 298; 2.62–9.61 u), P1 #1. L2 9 events: 6 ZIPs (`jumpToPip()`, 1.88–9.96 u), P1 #1; 3 SNAPs of 3.70 u (`tapPot()` line 269 `c.position.set(p.position.x, 0, p.position.z)` re-seats the hidden, `visible = false` friend under its swapped cover) — intentional. L3 12 events: 6 ZIPs (`jumpToPip()`, 2.54–9.61 u), P1 #1; 6 SNAPs of 3.70 u (`tapPot()` line 269, ~1.7 s before each ZIP) — intentional.

### story — What happens next?
Captures: `story-L0`, `story-L1`, `story-L2` (16 shots each) + `story-L0-short`, `story-L1-short`, `story-L2-short` (16 each). Captures post-date the code (`story.js` 15:01); nothing stale. `story-L1` reaches its finale; `story-L2` stops in round 6, so the L3 finale was reviewed from the full-length `qalong/story-L2` (11 s interval, in-game shots 01–13, Home from 14). Engine items recorded by group A (clouds over the sun, sticker badge, photo-flash frame) not re-listed.

1. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `story-L0/shot11`, `story-L1/shot15`, `qalong/story-L2/shot13` — finale says "Look what you made!" for 4+ s over empty crate stands: every card has flown away, Pip is off-screen (x = −6.7), no character or creation on screen; the saved gallery photo on Home is an empty farm (`story-L0/shot13`, `qalong/story-L2/shot14` "You made this!"). **Fix:** as AUDIT §5 "Ending" (`makeTray` shelf receiving each story's ending card, `creationView()` / `creationObjects()`), plus bring Pip into frame for the admire. ↔ AUDIT §5 "Ending" / X1
2. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `story-L0/shot03`, `story-L1/shot07`, `story-L2/shot06` — butterfly ending card: on a stand the butterfly is edge-on, a dark vertical bar with a sliver of pink/yellow hanging from the twig (reads only lying on the grass seen from above, `story-L0/shot08`); this is the story's payoff picture. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/story-tales.js` `butterfly.steps.fly.build` — `prop('prop_m_butterfly', { … r: [-1.35, 0, -0.18] })`: re-pose so the open wings face the PICK and STORY cameras (9–13° above horizontal), check both cameras and the choice row; s 1.0 → ~1.3; move it off the twig (at x 0.3 it overlaps the leaves).
3. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `story-L0/shot07`, `story-L1/shot02`, `story-L2/shot03` — "Bunny eats the apple" card: the bitten apple floats in front of Bunny's chin/chest while both paws are spread to the sides; nothing holds it and it covers the mouth. **Fix:** `story-tales.js` `apple.steps.eat.build` — arm bones `st.bun.userData.arms[0/1].rotation.z` inward ~0.9 so both paws meet on the apple; apple `at: [0.44, 0.36, 0.3]` → y ≈ 0.27; tilt the head down; keep that pose in `act` instead of resetting `h.rotation.x = 0`.
4. **[P1]** L1–L3 · between `story-L0/shot09` and `shot10`; after `story-L1/shot03`, `shot04`, `shot11`; after `qalong/story-L2/shot05`, `shot09` — teleport: the character inside a choice card jumps while the card flies to its stand (chick 0.36 u in one frame; pig 1.55 u, pig 1.85 u, chick 1.56 u, 4–5 frames each; pig 1.11 u, bunny 1.14 u, 3 frames each). **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/story.js` `flyTo(c, slot, dur = 0.85)` — `Ease.inOut` (cubic) peaks at 3× mean speed (6–7 unit diagonal in 0.85 s ≈ 24 units/s): `dur = 0.6 + dist * 0.1` (about 1.2–1.3 s for long diagonals) or a softer ease.
5. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `story-L0/shot01`, `story-L1-short/shot07`, `story-L2/shot16` — the flying card arcs out of the top of the frame: top edge cut by the screen edge, passes behind the caption bubble / under the Home button (`story-L2/shot07`); at L3 a flying card balloons to ~45 % of screen height and covers stands 3–4. **Fix:** `story.js` `flyTo` — `const h = 1.8 + Math.abs(a.x - b.x) * 0.1` → about `0.7 + …*0.05`.
6. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `story-L0-short/shot02`, `story-L1/shot01`, `story-L2/shot01` — stale caption: "Hmm, not that one!" stays up while the right card flies in, lands and the finished story plays in close-up; "Not yet! What comes first?" still up after the first card is placed and the "?" is on stand 2. **Fix:** `story.js` `place()` — clear or replace the caption at the start (`this.say('', …)` or the tale's own line); `complete()` must not start with an old caption up. ↔ AUDIT §5 "Common, finished story" + L2/L3 card lines (audit says each card's line as it lands, which replaces the stale text)
7. **[P2]** L2 · `story-L1/shot02`, `story-L1/shot05`, `story-L1-short/shot11` — engine: the autumn look's falling leaves drift in front of and into the story cards (red leaf beside the apple tree reads as a second apple, yellow leaf on the muddy pig's head, orange leaf on the rain puddle). **Fix:** `app/src/engine/scenery.js` `addFalling(… z: [-14, 2] …)` — keep leaves behind the stands for this game (z ≤ −2.5; stands z −0.8, choice row z 3.1), e.g. a per-lesson `fallZ` option set from `story.js` `build()`.
8. **[P2]** L3 · `story-L2/shot07`, `story-L2/shot09`, `story-L2-short/shot06` — engine: the rainy look's long white streaks fall in front of cards and stands (across the muddy-pig choice card, across card 2). **Fix:** `app/src/engine/scenery.js` `addRain()` — keep rain behind z −2.5 for this game (same option as #7) or thin/shorten the streaks.
9. **[P2]** L2 · `story-L1/shot02`, `story-L1/shot05`, `story-L1/shot14` — under the autumn light the cards' sky panels turn a dingy grey-green (bright blue at L1); pictures look dirty. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/story-props.js` `makeCard()` — unlit material for the frame's `back` layer (as the `shine` layers already skip ink).
10. **[P2]** L3 · `story-L2/shot03`, `story-L2/shot06`, `story-L2/shot09` — four-card close-up: cards are only ~27 % of screen height, in the lower-middle; top 38 % is sky/rainbow. **Fix:** `story.js` `build()` `storyCam` — for n = 4 `look.y` 2.55 → 2.0 (`pos.y` likewise), `w / 1.05` → `w / 1.15`. ↔ AUDIT §5 "L3 (all)" (audit limits 4-card stories to rounds 5–6 or drops them)
11. **[P2]** L1–L2 · `story-L0/shot04`, `story-L0-short/shot01`, `story-L1/shot03` — PICK view with no choice cards (opening, between rounds): stands fill only the middle band, lower ~40 % is bare grass and path, no caption. **Fix:** `story.js` `CAMS.pick` — frame tighter when no choices are out (`pos` z 10.3 → ~8.6, look y 1.55 → 1.9); glide to the current PICK framing only when `deal()` runs.
12. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `story-L0/shot10`, `story-L1/shot11`, `qalong/story-L2/shot03` — the cracked egg reads as an egg with one brown bullseye eye (brown ring with dark centre above a zigzag); neither "crack" nor "beak". **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_story.py` `prop_st_eggcrack` — clear jagged hole with a visible orange beak and a second crack line; drop the ring.
13. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `story-L0/shot01`, `story-L1/shot13`, `story-L2-short/shot05` — rainbow card: the little sun is half hidden behind the frame's top-left corner (in the choice row only its lower rays show, an orange crown-shaped scrap). **Fix:** `story-tales.js` `rain.steps.rainbow.build` — `sky('prop_word_sun', { s: 0.24, at: [-0.66, 0.93, -0.2] })` → about `at: [-0.5, 0.74, -0.2]`.
14. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `story-L0/shot09`, `story-L1/shot13`, `story-L2/shot13` — rain card: the cloud's top pokes through/over the frame's top inner edge (white above the ink line). **Fix:** `story-tales.js` `rain.steps.rain.build` — cloud `at: [0, 1.1, 0.0]`, `s: 0.42` → y ≈ 0.95; drops' top in `act` 0.8 → 0.68.
15. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `story-L0/shot05`, `story-L1/shot05`, `story-L2/shot08` — pig-and-mud card: Piggy's left hand overlaps the frame's left inner wall (L3: hand and ear against wall/top). **Fix:** `story-tales.js` `pig.steps.mud.build` — `critter('pig', { s: 0.46, at: [-0.44, 0, 0.06] })` → x ≈ −0.3 (or s 0.42); mud to x 0.55.
16. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `story-L0/shot07`, `story-L1/shot02`, `story-L2/shot03` — bunny cards: on the eat card Bunny's right paw and ear tip touch the frame's right wall; on the apple-core card the paw disappears into the wall and the ear touches it. **Fix:** `story-tales.js` `apple.steps.eat.build` / `core.build` — bunny `at: [0.5, 0, -0.02]` → x ≈ 0.36; tree to x −0.5.
17. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `story-L0/shot10`, `story-L1/shot12`, `qalong/story-L2/shot04` — hatched-chick card: the chick's right wing overlaps the frame's right wall, the nest's left rim runs into the left wall, nest and chick touch. **Fix:** `story-tales.js` `chick.steps.chick.build` — chick `s: 0.82, at: [0.44, …]` → `s: 0.72, at: [0.36, …]`; `nest(d, -0.47, 0.7)` → `nest(d, -0.42, 0.62)`.
18. **[P2]** L3 · `story-L2/shot11`, `story-L2/shot12` — bud card: the top of the pink bud is hidden behind the frame's top band (the one detail that tells "bud" from "flower"). **Fix:** `story-tales.js` `flower.steps.bud.build` — stem `s: 0.92` → 0.78; bud `at: [0, SOIL_TOP + 0.84, 0.08]` → `SOIL_TOP + 0.68`.
19. **[P2]** L3 · `story-L2/shot09`, `story-L2-short/shot12`, `story-L2-short/shot13` — "dry" card: the tub's right rim is cut by the frame's right wall. **Fix:** `story-tales.js` `pig.steps.dry.build` — `prop('prop_metaltub', { s: 0.38, at: [0.52, 0, -0.02] })` → x 0.4; pig x −0.4 → −0.45 with s 0.42.
20. **[P2]** L3 · `qalong/story-L2/shot12` — the butterfly card's sparkle is a ring of pale yellow dots drawn straight over the butterfly, hiding what little is readable. **Fix:** `story-tales.js` `butterfly.steps.fly.act` `sparkleAt(st.bf)` → `sparkleAt(st.bf, 0.35)` (above/beside), once the butterfly is re-posed (#2).
21. **[P3]** L1 · `story-L0-short/shot14` — caterpillar card: the leaf's stem tip runs into the left frame wall. **Fix:** `story-tales.js` `butterfly.steps.cat.build` / `eggs.build` — `prop_st_leafbed` `s: 1.15` → 1.02.
22. **[P3]** L1 · `story-L0/shot05`, `story-L0/shot09` — every card shows a hairline vertical ink line down the left edge of the sky panel (the back panel's side edge). **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_story.py` `frame()` — back panel half-width `FW / 2 - 0.12` → flush with the side walls (`FW / 2 - FT + 0.02`), or hide the `back` layer's outline.

24. **[P3]** L1 · `story-L0/shot05` — the muddy pig is one flat brown from ears to feet (reads as a different, brown pig). **Fix:** `story-tales.js` `pig.steps.muddy.build` `tintSkin(st.pig, MUD)` — mud patches (a `cloth_mud` layer or decals on belly, snout, legs) over pink skin.
25. **[P3]** L1 · `story-L0/shot09` — sun card: the two flowers are tiny and pushed into the bottom corners, the left one against the wall. **Fix:** `story-tales.js` `rain.steps.sun.build` — flowers `s: 0.5/0.45` → 0.75, x ±0.7 → ±0.55.
26. **[P3]** L1 · `story-L0-short/shot12` — on exit the first card flies up across the Home button. **Fix:** as AUDIT §5 "Ending" (ending card to the tray, others slide into a stack; no fly-off); raw alternative: `story.js` `clearRound()` lift straight up only after shrinking, or drift toward centre. ↔ AUDIT §5 "Ending"

28. **[P3]** L2 · `story-L1/shot01`, `story-L1/shot06`, `story-L1/shot13` — the golden "?" marker floats over the wheat/fence clutter, gold outline on gold-orange scenery; hard to pick out. **Fix:** `story.js` `moveQ()` — sit it on the stand top (`to` y + 0.05 only) and/or give `prop_st_qmark` a white backing disc.

Bunny ate the apple!" is still up after all cards have gone (caption over empty stands). **Fix:** `story.js` `afterSuccess()` — hide the caption before `clearRound()`. ↔ AUDIT §5 "Ending" (audit keeps the ending card on a tray, so the stands are no longer empty)
31. **[P3]** L2 · `story-L1/shot02`, `story-L1/shot05` — engine: the tops of the red and yellow autumn trees show as flat blobs between and above the cards in the close-up. **Fix:** engine `calmBackdrop` (group A's item), or move the two trees out of x −2…4 for this game.
32. **[P3]** L3 · `story-L2/shot09` — the "dry" ending card is just a pig beside an empty tub; nothing says dry or clean (no towel, no sparkle at rest). **Fix:** `story-tales.js` `pig.steps.dry.build` — add a towel prop or a few resting sparkle decals.
33. **[P3]** L3 · `story-L2/shot14`, `story-L2/shot15` — "cloud" card: the sun is almost fully hidden (only a few rays show) and the lower half of the card is empty. **Fix:** `story-tales.js` `rain.steps.cloud.build` — sun `at: [-0.4, 0.62, -0.24]` → x −0.55, y 0.75; cloud y 1.0 → 0.85.

## Home world

### dress — Dress Monkey
Captures: `dress-L0` (in-game shots 01–09), `dress-L1` (01–10), `dress-L2` (01–11); the rest is Home (not reviewed). Captures post-date the code (nothing stale). Engine items already recorded by group A (clouds over the sun, sticker badge over the scene, photo-flash frame) are not re-listed.

1. **[P1]** L1–L2 · `dress-L0/shot01`, `dress-L1/shot01` — the garment to drag is unreadable on its white rail disc: stripy T-shirt = squashed red-and-white pillow (reads as lips), no neck hole or sleeves; party dress = squashed pink clam (bodice slab on a flattened dotted skirt). **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/dress.js` `makeProp()` / rail code calling `fitProp` — garments on a little hanger, top tilted forward ~20°, or dedicated display models; `tools/blender/creatures/monkey.py` `tshirt()` — longer sleeves (`sleeve(s, 0.48, …)`).
2. **[P1]** L1 · `dress-L0/shot02`, `dress-L0/shot04`, `dress-L0/shot05` — T-shirt worn: stripes have torn, ragged ends at both sides and smear into vertical fragments under the right arm; brown arm fur shows through at the shoulder; no readable sleeve. **Fix:** `monkey.py` `tshirt()` — `stripes_y(0.12, …)` painted by a smooth height field (clean bands on sleeves and side seams); thicken `torso(0.05)` at the armpits; extend sleeves over the upper arm.
3. **[P1]** L1 · `dress-L0/shot04`, `dress-L0/shot05` — a brown patch of body pokes through the front of the orange shorts between the legs. **Fix:** `monkey.py` `shorts()` — crotch blob `c.blob((0, 0.37, 0.03), (0.17, 0.08, 0.3))` → about `(0.24, 0.12, 0.36)`, or inset the body there while shorts are worn.
4. **[P1]** L3 · `dress-L2/shot06`, `dress-L2/shot07` — scarf over the jumper: the jumper pokes through the scarf's hanging end (torn vertical gap, ragged white bottom); the neck wrap is half buried in the collar. **Fix:** `monkey.py` `scarf()` — `pts = [(0.17, 1.12, 0.39), (0.21, 0.94, 0.47), (0.24, 0.76, 0.49)]` z +0.09, wrap ring radii `0.35, 0.33` → 0.4, 0.38 (must clear `jumper()`), or offset the scarf layer in `dress.js` when a top is worn.
5. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `dress-L0/shot06`–`shot09`, `dress-L1/shot07`, `dress-L2/shot08`–`shot11` — finale "Look what you made!" shows the dressed monkey from behind at the left edge; the face is a small reflection partly hidden by the monkey; Pip stands large centre foreground; the gallery photo is the same view. **Fix:** `dress.js` `mirrorDance()` — monkey three-quarter to camera (`b` further right, `face` ~45° toward the child), Pip `0.6` → ~4.5; `creationView()` as AUDIT D2. ↔ AUDIT D2
6. **[P2]** L1 · `dress-L0/shot03`, `dress-L0/shot04` — "Shorts on!": the T-shirt hem covers the shorts; only a ~25 px orange sliver shows, so the second action has almost no visible result. **Fix:** `monkey.py` `tshirt()` `cut_below(0.425, ['top'])` → hem ≈ 0.5, or longer shorts legs (`cut_below(0.25, …)` → 0.18).
7. **[P2]** L1–L2 · `dress-L0/shot01`, `dress-L0/shot02`, `dress-L1/shot01` — thought bubble is a flat emoji-style 2D monkey with a dashed ring and a tiny emoji that does not match the garment (blue polo for the red-striped T-shirt, T-shirt for the party dress, shorts for the tutu: icons come from the slot); clashes with the sculpted 3D look. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/dress-room.js` `bubbleTex()` / `dress.js` line ~374 — `plain` bubble with the real 3D garment (`setBubbleGarment(spec.name)`) at all levels, or per-garment `SLOTS.*.icon` in `dress-clothes.js`. conflicts with AUDIT D1: D1 keeps the L2 bubble icon.
8. **[P2]** L3 · `dress-L2/shot01`, `dress-L2/shot05` — the bubble here is a 3D garment with a snowflake emoji overlapping it, while L1–L2 use the flat emoji monkey: two bubble designs in one game. **Fix:** one design (item 7); move the weather icon clear of the garment (`bubbleTex` icon at 196, 52).
9. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `dress-L0/shot02`, `dress-L1/shot02`, `dress-L2/shot05` — after a garment is taken a large blank white disc with a pink rim hangs on the rail (reads as a placeholder; at L3 it pops larger than its rest size) and the next garment arrives riding over the top of the rail. **Fix:** `dress.js` — pop the old card out before the next `makeCard()` deal; bring the new card in already carrying its garment.
10. **[P2]** L1–L2 · `dress-L0/shot06`, `dress-L1/shot07` — a green "Go!" badge pops up over the window during the mirror finale, when there is nothing to do. **Fix:** `dress.js` — `this.goPending = false` before `mirrorDance()` (round ≥ `totalRounds`), or `engine/lesson.js` `checkGo()` skip when the level is complete.
11. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `dress-L0/shot07`, `dress-L1/shot08`, `dress-L2/shot09` — from behind, the tail comes straight through the T-shirt hem and shorts / the dress and tutu / the jumper hem (no opening; cloth and tail intersect). **Fix:** `monkey.py` — cut a tail notch in the garment backs (`cut` around the `TAIL` root in `shorts()`/`tshirt()` etc.) or raise the tail root above the waistband.
12. **[P2]** L2 · `dress-L1/shot02`, `dress-L1/shot04`, `dress-L1/shot06` — party dress paint: the yellow waist bow is a ragged splat with a dotted yellow trail, stray ink scribbles at the waist seam, tutu ruffle is a tangle of heavy outline squiggles. **Fix:** `monkey.py` `dress()` / `tutu()` — bow as a separate `layer()` with its own outline; ruffle as 8–10 clean scallops.
13. **[P2]** L2 · `dress-L1/shot04`, `dress-L1/shot05` — the dress has puff-sleeve caps at the shoulders but the arms come out of the bodice below them (empty sleeves). **Fix:** `monkey.py` `dress()` — sleeve caps on the arm roots (same anchor as `sleeve()` in `tshirt()`).
14. **[P2]** L2 · `dress-L1/shot05`, `dress-L1/shot06` — heart glasses: each temple arm is a straight pink stick ending on the cheek instead of at the ear; in the party-hat jump the lower eye outline shows below the right lens. **Fix:** `monkey.py` `hearts()` — arm capsules `(s*0.34 …) → (s*0.56, …, 0.34) → (s*0.64, …, 0.0)` follow the head surface to the ear; lens paint `(0.12, 0.1, 0.06)` → 0.15, 0.13.
15. **[P2]** L2 · `dress-L1/shot04`, `dress-L1/shot05`, `dress-L1/shot06` — the bow tie is half sunk into the dress collar; reads as three purple beads. **Fix:** `monkey.py` `bowtie()` — z 0.34/0.37 → 0.42/0.45, blobs ×1.4 (`(0.11, 0.085, 0.05)` → `(0.16, 0.12, 0.06)`).
16. **[P2]** L1 · `dress-L0/shot07`, `dress-L0/shot09`, `dress-L0/shot04` — sneakers: from behind each shoe has a big yellow heel oval that reads as the foot poking through; from the front the white toe caps are ragged blobs. **Fix:** `monkey.py` `sneakers()` — `c.paint('heel', …, 0xffd23f)` → small white pull-tab (or remove); clean the `toe` paint ellipse.
17. **[P2]** L1 · `dress-L0/shot07`, `dress-L0/shot09` — the sun hat's pink ribbon is a ragged, scribbly band with frayed strands at the bow. **Fix:** `monkey.py` `sunhat()` — `ring(…, 'ribbon', n=26 …)` → clean band (lathe/torus strip, crisp stripe) and a tidy two-loop bow.
18. **[P2]** L3 · `dress-L2/shot02`, `dress-L2/shot01` — dungarees: the two straps are short stubs standing straight up from the bib (like rabbit ears) instead of going over the shoulders; on the rail disc the dungarees are a splayed frog shape. **Fix:** `monkey.py` `dungarees()` — run the straps over the shoulders to the back.
19. **[P2]** L3 · `dress-L2/shot09` — the mirror reflection's face is broken: mouth is a dotted line, cheek speckled (mirrored clone uses negative scale). **Fix:** `dress.js` `mirrorDance()` `r.scale.set(-MS, MS, MS)` — flip the face decal materials' `side`/polygon offset for the clone, or mirror with a flipped-geometry copy.
20. **[P2]** L2 · `dress-L1/shot02` — mid-twirl the mouth wraps under the chin and reads as a dark beard. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/dress-moves.js` `twirl` — keep the head facing the camera during the spin (counter-rotate the head bone) or hide the open mouth (`setFace(f, 'happy')`).
21. **[P3]** L1–L3 · `dress-L0/shot01`–`shot05`, `dress-L1/shot01`–`shot06`, `dress-L2/shot01`–`shot07` — the clothes rail is code primitives (plain cylinders, sphere knobs, squashed-sphere feet) next to sculpted furniture; its left pole stands on Pip's wing edge. **Fix:** `dress-room.js` `makeRail()` — a Blender prop (turned posts, rounded feet); `RAIL.x0` 1.0 → 1.3 or Pip left.
22. **[P3]** L1, L3 · `dress-L0/shot01`, `dress-L0/shot03`, `dress-L2/shot05` — the bubble's trail dots sit on the monkey's head (L1) / ear and eye (L3). **Fix:** `dress.js` `BUBBLE_AT` — raise/shift so the trail ends above the head.
23. **[P3]** L1 · `dress-L0/shot05` — "Hat on!": during the tip move the hat sits skewed with the brim across the forehead; the daisy shows only as a white blob at the brim's far end. **Fix:** `monkey.py` `sunhat()` — daisy on the camera side (front-left); reduce the tilt in `dress-moves.js` `tip`.
24. **[P3]** L3 · `dress-L2/shot04`, `dress-L2/shot07` — wellies: the left boot has no dots and a dark band, the right has two dots; a brown patch of body shows between the boots under the jumper. **Fix:** `monkey.py` wellies — symmetric paint; dungarees crotch as item 3.
25. **[P3]** L2 · `dress-L1/shot06` — the mirror's pink bow lines up exactly with the monkey's head and reads as a bow on the monkey. **Fix:** `dress.js` monkey x or `dress-room.js` `MIRROR.x` — offset by ~0.6.
26. **[P3]** L1 · `dress-L0/shot06` — the closed wardrobe doors carry dark scribbly strokes along their edges (sketch lines, not shading). **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures` wardrobe prop — clean the door paint.
