### myday — My day
Captures: `myday-L0/-L1/-L2` and their `-short` folders (18:53–19:04) pre-date the `myday.js` edits of 19:07 and 19:58; `qalong/myday-L0` and `qalong/myday-L2` (20:19–20:25) post-date them; no L2 `qalong` capture. P1s with no re-verify mark were not visible at 20:20 (towel wrap, cup at the mouth, level intro, all of L2) and still need re-verifying. Not captured anywhere: hair brushing in action, the L1 finale, hops onto/off the stool, into/out of the bath and bed. Line numbers are from the 19:07 file (from l.110 on, about 5 lower now); code items were re-checked against the current file and are unchanged. Geometry (`prop_myday.py` `sink()`, `myday-house.js`): basin rim top y 1.84, bowl floor y 1.31–1.33, bowl outer radius 1.28 (x) × 0.79 (z), spout tip (-4.27, 1.96, -1.30), basin back edge z -1.79, Bunny on the stool at (-4.3, 1.25, -2.6), scale 1.45.

1. **[P1]** L1, L3 · `myday-L0/shot01`, `myday-L0-short/shot02`, `myday-L2/shot01` — belly clipping into the sink: the tummy patch is cut straight across by the basin's back rim, no gap, stool or feet visible; Bunny reads as waist-deep in the basin (belly front z ≈ -1.8 touches rim back edge z -1.79; `bend 0.5` tips the torso into the bowl). **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/myday-house.js` `SPOTS.sink` / stool — stool to z -2.95, `STOOL_TOP` 1.25 → 1.55 (tummy above the 1.84 rim with a strip of wall visible), or a 3/4 station view; `bend` ≤ 0.3. (re-verified 20:20: still present — `qalong/myday-L0/shot03`, `shot11`, `qalong/myday-L2/shot07`)
2. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `myday-L0/shot03`, `myday-L0-short/shot06`, `myday-L1-short/shot10` — hands don't go into the sink: at wet/rinse the paws hang at rim height, pads to camera; the spout runs between/in front of them; the water is a 0.1-unit blue sliver (two outlined blue balls). **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/myday.js` `PAWS.in` (l.73), `waterOnPaws()` (l.776) — `hands()` at about (-4.27, 1.55, -1.30), pads down; stream `max(0.1, spout.y - hands.y - 0.02)` → floor 0.35, ending on the paws. (re-verified 20:20: still present — `qalong/myday-L0/shot02`, `shot10`, `qalong/myday-L2/shot08`; L2 not re-verified)
3. **[P1]** L1–L2 · `myday-L0/shot03`, `myday-L0/shot08`, `myday-L1/shot05` — washing pose reads as a floating head with two white tubes: body hidden behind the head (`bend: 0.5`), arms stretched 20% (`reach: 1.2` → `a.scale.set(1, P.reach, 1)`, l.1351) into straight sausages; stray black ink tick beside the left arm. **Fix:** `myday.js` `PAWS.in` — `bend` 0.5 → 0.3, `reach` 1.0, tilt the head (`headX` 0.25); sink camera pitch in `fitView` 0.16 → 0.3. (re-verified 20:20: still present — `qalong/myday-L0/shot02`, `shot10`; L2 not re-verified)
4. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `myday-L0-short/shot07`, `myday-L0-short/shot08`, `myday-L2/shot03` — terrible towel (in use): dragged, the hand towel is a rigid flat slab held upright before Bunny's chin; `prop_md_towelwrap` then appears as a box standing in the basin, cutting the rim line, hiding paws and chest, sparkle over the eye (L2 `myday-L1/shot07`, L3 `myday-L2-short/shot07`: box held over the mouth). **Fix:** `myday.js` `dry()` (l.824–838) — raise the paws above the rim first (`paws('out')`), wrap at `hands()` with y ≥ 2.3, scale ≤ 0.55, sparkle at `hands() + (0.9, 0.4, 0.8)`; `tools/blender/creatures/prop_myday.py` `towelwrap()` — soft rounded bundle with two paw tips. (re-verify: not visible in the 20:20 capture)
5. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `myday-L0/shot04`, `myday-L0-short/shot09`, `myday-L2/shot01` — terrible towel (hanging): a plain rectangle slab dead centre under the basin, cut in half by the bottom frame edge, hiding the pedestal; no drape, fold or thickness. **Fix:** `prop_myday.py` `towel()` / `towel_panel()` — real drape (two unequal hanging lengths, fold volume, curved hem, soft S-folds); `myday.js` `viewFor()` — include the whole towel (y down to 0.0) or hang it on a ring beside the basin (`sink()` anchor `towel`). (re-verified 20:20: still present — `qalong/myday-L0/shot05`, `shot11`, `qalong/myday-L2/shot01`; L2 not re-verified)
6. **[P1]** L1–L3 (code); seen L2 · `myday-L1-short/shot09`, `myday-L1/shot04` — stuck, then jumps behind the sink (from code, not seen in shots; the squeeze is visible): the walkway `PATH_NODES.sinkRa` → `sinkR` (-2.55, 0, -2.75) (`myday-house.js` l.27) passes 0.97 from the soap dish and ~1.05 from the cabinet, Bunny's half-width is ~1.2 (`bunnyBox`, l.229): ledge and soap cut across the tummy, paw over the drawer; it then stops, turns 0.4 s (l.456), squashes 0.18 s (l.364) and hops 1.75 units sideways to the stool. **Fix:** approach the stool only from the open left, or move cabinet/bin so the gap is ≥ 2.6; hop straight out of the walk (no stop-and-turn).
7. **[P1]** L1–L3 · from code, not seen in shots — permanent freeze on a repeated tap of the tissue: `atchoo()` awaits `this.s.tween(o.scale, …, 0.35)` (l.917); `pick()` (l.632) calls `this.s.stop(o.scale)` on any touch, not gated on `busy`; `stop()` in `app/src/engine/tween.js` (l.51) drops the item without resolving. `busy` stays true, `phase` stays `'acting'`, every later tap is ignored. **Fix:** engine `tween.js` `stop()` — resolve dropped items; `myday.js` `pick()` — return unless `phase === 'step' && !busy`, or disable drag targets in `run()`.
8. **[P1]** L1, L3 · from code, not seen in shots — full 360° spin hopping onto the stool after the bath round (L1 round 4 → 5, L3 round 2 → 3): `walkPath()` accumulates `yaw` unwrapped (l.449–451), ends at -2π (l.454–456), then `hopTo(SPOTS.sink, …, face = 0)` lerps `rotation.y` -2π → 0 in 0.75 s (l.368). **Fix:** normalise `b.rotation.y` to (-π, π] at the end of `walkPath()`; shortest-arc turn in `hopTo()`.
9. **[P1]** L1–L3 · from code, not seen in shots — into/out of the bath is one 4.4-unit leap in 0.9 s (4.9 units/s vs 2.1 walk speed): `goTo()` l.423–424, l.410 lerp between `mat` (-6.6, 0, 0.2) and `SPOTS.bath` (-10.4, 0.05, -1.9), across the bubble bottle, sponge and big towel. **Fix:** add a path node beside the tub (about (-8.6, 0, -0.3)), walk there, then hop ≤ 1.6 units over the rim.
10. **[P1]** L1–L3 · from code, not seen in shots — `walkPath()` hard-sets `b.position.y = 0` (l.435); with `this.spot` null (every walk, l.412, and level start) `goTo()` walks a straight line to the nearest node (l.404–405). A call while Bunny is on the stool (y 1.25), in the bath or in bed drops it to the floor in one frame and drags it through furniture. **Fix:** track real location separately from `spot`; always leave stool/bath/bed with their hop; never start `walkPath` off a node.
11. **[P1]** L1–L3 · from code, not seen in shots — `lieDown()` (l.471–476: `position.copy(LIE.pos)`, `rotation.set(0, 0, LIE.rz)`) is called from `startRound()` (l.511) when a bed routine starts with `spot === null`: a teleport into bed. Not reachable with today's three plans; any plan starting `wake`/`night` after a walk, or a round started mid-walk, hits it. **Fix:** `lieDown()` only in `setupLevel()`; otherwise `await this.goTo('inBed')`.
12. **[P1]** L1–L3 · from code, not seen in shots — `cheer()` writes `b.position.y` (l.1154) / `b.rotation.y` (l.1158) for 1.0–1.1 s, then hard-assigns the start values; safe only because `success()` waits 1.6 s (`engine/lesson.js` l.368–369). The promise is stored in `this.payoff`; the engine waits on `this.payoffP`. If the wait shortens, Bunny walks at stool height / mid-twirl and snaps to `y0`/`r0`. **Fix:** `this.payoffP = this.cheer(…)`; animate back with the same tween, no absolute resets.
13. **[P1]** L2–L3 · from code, not seen in shots — getting into/out of bed (`climbIntoBed()` l.458–470, `getOutOfBed()` l.477–489) is a 1 s straight lerp `bedFront` (7.3, 0, 0.45) ↔ `LIE.pos` (8.45, 1.95, -2.15) while `rotation.z` swings 90° about the feet: the 4.7-tall body sweeps through the front rail and footboard; `rotation.z = 0` hard-set at l.486, `update()` (l.1358) takes over when `bedAnim` clears. **Fix:** hop onto the mattress upright (0.5 s), lie down in place (0.6 s); reverse to get up.
14. **[P1]** L1, L3 · `myday-L0/shot12`, `myday-L2/shot07`, `myday-L2-short/shot16` — drying after the bath: the big towel is a rigid slab stuck to Bunny's front; Bunny is cut by the bottom (and right) edge, stands in front of the sink and stool, toothbrush looks like it grows from the ear; the "All clean!" twirl shows a blank cropped back. **Fix:** `myday-house.js` `SPOTS.mat` (-6.6, 0, 0.2) → on the bath mat (about (-8.3, 0, -0.3)); `myday.js` `viewFor()` for `at: 'body'` — frame `bunnyBox('mat')` whole with feet; towel model as item 5. (re-verified 20:20: still present — `qalong/myday-L0/shot08` (also halo over the face), `qalong/myday-L2/shot05`)
15. **[P1]** L2–L3 · `myday-L1/shot01`, `myday-L1-short/shot01`, `myday-L2/shot14` — Bunny in bed is the upright model rotated 90° (`LIE.rz`): lies on its side on top of the bedding, face sideways, duvet covers only a front strip, head hangs in front of the pillow and overlaps the headboard post. **Fix:** `myday.js` `LIE` (l.39), `DUVET.over` (l.42) — real lying pose (on the back, head on the pillow at (BED.x - 1.85, 1.45), `LIE.pos.y` 1.95 → ~1.6); duvet to the chin, wrapped over the body. (re-verified 20:20: still present — `qalong/myday-L2/shot10`, `shot11`; L2 not re-verified)
16. **[P1]** L2–L3 · `myday-L1/shot01`, `myday-L1/shot02`, `myday-L2/shot13` — duvet model: yellow stars are extruded pegs/cubes on a blue slab, saw-tooth lower edge with thick ink, stretched per state (`over` y ×3.6, `floor` 0.5/2.0/0.55, `folded` 0.32/2.4/1.0); folded (L3, `myday-L2/shot14`) it is a blue block with pegs overlapping the footboard post. **Fix:** `prop_myday.py` `duvet()` — stars as flat paint/decal, clean rounded quilt edge, separate baked made / pulled-over / crumpled / folded shapes instead of scaling in `myday.js` `DUVET`; `DUVET.folded` x 0.3 further from the footboard. (re-verify: L2 not in the 20:20 capture)
17. **[P1]** L3 · `myday-L2/shot14`, `myday-L2/shot15`, `myday-L2/shot16` — "cuddle Teddy": Teddy stands upright in a T-pose on top of the lying Bunny (later on the duvet), held by nothing. **Fix:** `myday.js` `cuddle()` (l.1104) — lay Teddy along Bunny's chest (`rot` z = `LIE.rz`, tucked under the arm, half under the duvet) and close the arm over it. (re-verified 20:20: still present — `qalong/myday-L2/shot10`, `shot11`)
18. **[P1]** L3 · `myday-L2/shot12`, `myday-L2-short/shot01` — engine: "Amazing! Bless you!": both ears collapsed (left a round stub with broken ink dashes, right folded flat sideways), stray ink ticks on the crown. **Fix (probable cause):** `app/src/engine/creature.js` `stepSprings()` (hop-gait kick -5, l.330) driven by `cheer()` 0.3 bounce (l.1154), `afterSuccess()` 0.6 s straighten (l.596) and the ear override in `update()` (l.1360–1364) — `MAX_SWING` 0.9 → 0.5 for Bunny, sine bounce, override only when `spot === 'inBed'`. (re-verified 20:20: still present, milder — `qalong/myday-L2/shot08`, `shot05`)
19. **[P1]** L1, L3 · `myday-L2/shot04`, `qalong/myday-L0/shot06` — praise ("Super! Clean paws!", "Well done! Bless you!") is shown over the bath view while Bunny is a cropped white mass at the right edge, with the sink's cup, paste and brush covering its mouth and half its face. **Fix:** `myday.js` `startRound()` (l.510) — no glide to the next station until the praise has finished and Bunny has left the stool; `SPOTS.mat` as item 14. ### snack — Snack chef
Captures: `snack-L0/-L1/-L2` and their `-short` folders (19:04–19:16) pre-date the saves of `snack.js` (19:17:35) and `snack-kitchen.js` (19:17:35, 19:25:07); `qalong/snack-L2` (20:26, after the 19:33 saves) covers L3 only, including the finale and the open fridge. L1/L2-only items (bunny, frog, plate2, plain-plate foods) could not be re-verified and need a re-capture. `snack-kitchen.js` did not parse between 19:17 and 19:25 (line 161), so any capture from that window is a dead game. Layout since the first captures: soap left of the tap (x −6.55), friend right of the basin (`SINK_X = SINK.x + 0.75`), friends exit left. Never captured: the towel drying hands (L3), a fruit in the basin under the tap, the wrong-answer food hop.

1. **[P1]** L3 · `qalong/snack-L2/shot02`, `qalong/snack-L2/shot09` — hands still do not go into the sink at the new stand position: bear's right paw hangs over the worktop right of the basin, left paw pressed against the spout nozzle; pig's trotters and foam hover above the back rim right of the water, stream untouched. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/snack.js` `SINK_X = SINK.x + 0.75`, `solveReach()` goals `sp.x + 0.04` / `sp.x + 0.44` at `TOP + 0.32`, `SINK.z - 0.08` → goals `SINK.x ± 0.16`, `y = TOP + 0.08`, `z = SINK.z`, stand at `SINK.x + 0.35`; reject hands outside the basin (`|x − SINK.x| < 0.5`, `|z − SINK.z| < 0.35`).
2. **[P1]** L1 · `snack-L0/shot05`, `snack-L0-short/shot10`, `snack-L0-short/shot12` — Bunny at the sink: both paws stop at the basin's back rim and hang above it, never inside; the stream falls in front of the left paw; the body leans until the face is at worktop height (spout end behind the cheek, soap bottle over the mouth corner). **Fix:** `snack.js` `solveReach()` — use the returned `err`: when `err > 0.12` step closer (`u.standZ` up to `COUNTER_BACK - rest.front - 0.02`); goal into the basin (`goal.y = TOP + 0.10`, `z = SINK.z`); lengthen the forearm in `tools/blender/creatures/bunny.py` (≈ +35 %) or a per-kind `FS.bunny` sink offset; head rule as item 3. (not re-verifiable in the 20:26 capture: no bunny at L3; see item 1 for bear and pig)
3. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `snack-L1/shot05`, `snack-L0/shot09`, `snack-L2/shot12` — friend's head lowered onto the basin: frog (L2, worst) — head lies over the basin, spout vanishes into the cheek, stream from under the face, body over the back-left rim and tap handle, soap over the mouth, hands hidden; pig (L1, L3) — gooseneck goes up into the cheek, soap neck buried in the other cheek. **Fix:** `snack.js` `solveReach()` (l.244–270) — reject poses whose head box passes `SINK.z - 0.25` or touches `K.tap`/`K.soap`; pig/frog/bunny lean ≤ 0.1, reach from the arms (`ax` to −2.8) or a Blender "wash" pose; raw also proposed `SINK_X` `+0.5` → `+0.25` and soap to x −3.0 (layout has since changed). (pig appears fixed in 20:26 capture — `qalong/snack-L2/shot09`: upright, spout and soap clear; frog and bunny not in that capture — re-capture L1/L2)
4. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `snack-L0/shot01`, `snack-L0/shot05`, `snack-L0/shot08` (all sink shots) — the towel (`prop_k_towel`) is a flat orange rectangle on the cabinet front, bottom-left; only its top ~100 px is in frame; no folds, drape or thickness; rail brackets are two ragged white lumps; reads as a yellow sticker. At L3 it is a tap target ("Dry with the towel!") mostly below the frame. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_kitchen.py` `towel()` — draped cloth (S-fold, rounded hem, 2–3 folds, terry edge, loop), rail as its own prop/bone; `app/src/lessons/snack-kitchen.js` l.163 — hang it fully visible (hook/ring on the tiled wall right of the tap, y ≈ 2.2); `snack.js` `viewFor('sink')` frames it whole. (re-verified 20:26: still present — `qalong/snack-L2/shot01`–`02`, `04`–`06`, `08`–`09`; L1/L2 not re-captured)
5. **[P1]** L3 · `snack-L2/shot13` — the towel's chrome rail lifts off the cabinet with the towel: rail and both brackets float up and overlap the worktop's front edge. **Fix:** `prop_kitchen.py` `towel()` — layer `'rail'` has no bone of its own: make it a separate static prop (or its own bone left at rest) so only the cloth moves in `snack.js` `doDry()`/`towelPath()`. (not re-verifiable: no 20:26 frame shows the towel moving; `prop_k_towel.glb` unchanged since Nov 17, so still expected)
6. **[P1]** L3 · from code, not seen in shots — towel drying (`snack.js` `doDry()`, was l.775–789, now l.785–799): the towel is moved by its origin (top fold) to `handsMid + (0, 0.05, 0.42)`; the 0.85-long cloth hangs from ≈ `TOP + 0.37` to `TOP − 0.48`, through the basin floor (`BASIN_FLOOR` 0.75 = `TOP − 0.40`), in front of the paws, rail in mid-air; paws never touch cloth. **Fix:** hold the towel by its middle (model offset −0.45 in y, laid over the paws with a fold), keep it above `TOP + 0.05`, animate a drape/rub; needs item 4's model. (re-verified 20:26: still present in current code; towel in use again not captured)
7. **[P1]** L2–L3 · `snack-L1/shot04`, `snack-L1-short/shot06`, `qalong/snack-L2/shot06` — engine: cat's ears collapse — both folded flat onto the head with torn black ink lines, after stopping at the plate, after eating, and at the sink after washing. **Fix:** `app/src/engine/creature.js` spring chains (`SPRING_RE` `ear…`, `MAX_SWING` ≈ 50°) — per-kind clamp ≤ 15° for `cat` ears (or `userData.noSpring` in `snack.js` `makeFriend()`); reset spring velocity after `walk()`/`turnFront()` and the eat lean (`P.lean` 0.2). (re-verified 20:26: still present)
8. **[P1]** L3 · `snack-L2/shot04`, `snack-L2/shot09`, `qalong/snack-L2/shot07` — four-part plate (`prop_k_plate4`): four jagged flat shards (pink, green, yellow, blue) instead of readable strawberry/carrot/bread/bottle prints; white divider cross with scribbly doubled ink and torn ends. **Fix:** `prop_kitchen.py` `plate4()`/`dividers()` — prints as item 9; dividers as one rounded cross mesh (not two crossing `tube`s); `extras['outline']` 0.02 → 0.012 on the divider layer. (re-verified 20:26: still present — also `qalong/snack-L2/shot03`, `shot10`–`shot12`)
9. **[P1]** L2 · `snack-L1/shot03`, `snack-L1/shot11`, `snack-L1/shot07` — fruit|veg plate (`prop_k_plate2`): huge flat white oval, two pastel half-discs each holding a jagged flat polygon (pink pentagon/arrow, green wedge) meant as strawberry and carrot; unreadable; no rim shading or blue edge. **Fix:** `prop_kitchen.py` `plate()`/`plate2()` — replace `paint_fn` of `strawberry_poly`/`carrot_poly` (72-segment lathe) with a `sticker()`/`decal()` layer with its own outline, upright to camera; tint the rim (as `prop_chinaplate` 0x7fd0ff); `k` 1.25 → 1.05. 10. **[P1]** L3 · `snack-L2/shot05`, `snack-L2/shot10`, `qalong/snack-L2/shot12` — fridge view (and the finale's wide view): camera pulls far back; the plate is cut off by the left frame edge, the friend half out of frame, fridge small, lower-right quarter bare floor. **Fix:** `snack.js` `viewFor()` l.118 `x0 = PLATE.x - 1.5` → `PLATE.x - R - 0.9` (plate radius 1.44, plate 0.8–1.8 nearer than fit plane z −0.6); better: keep the plate view and hop fridge foods onto the board when the door opens. (re-verified 20:26: still present — `qalong/snack-L2/shot03`, `shot07`, `shot11`; `viewFor()` unchanged) ↔ AUDIT S2 (finale frame only: S2's `creationView()` replaces it)
11. **[P1]** L1–L3 · from code, not seen in shots — friend snap-turn: `snack.js` `walk()` (was l.224–236, now l.223–235) with `toStation()` l.43 turns by `r0 + dy * Math.min(1, p * 4)` (done in the first 25 % of a segment); the last segment `laneZ → standZ` is 0.05–0.5 long (0.02–0.2 s at `WALK` 2.3): a 90° snap in 1–3 frames; `turnFront(0.5)` has nothing left. **Fix:** time-based turn (≥ 0.35 s), or for `d < 0.9` keep the heading and let `turnFront` turn. (re-verified 20:26: still present in current code)
12. **[P1]** L1–L3 · from code, not seen in shots — `snack.js` `pipWalk()` (was l.163–173, now l.164–173): (a) `yaw - r0` not wrapped to ±π (long-way spin); (b) turn-in during the first 25 %, turn back to −0.45 in the last 15 % (≈ 115° in 0.09 s when `dur` = 0.6); (c) `this.s.stop(p.position)` does nothing (`s.anim` items have no `target`, `engine/tween.js` l.37–41), so overlapping `goStation()` calls fight and Pip jumps. **Fix:** wrap the angle, both turns ≥ 0.3 s, cancel the previous walk with a token (as `camTok`). (re-verified 20:26: still present in current code)
13. **[P1]** L1–L3 · from code, not seen in shots — `snack.js` `build()` l.74–75 `this.pip.position.copy(this.pipSpot(v)); this.pip.rotation.y = -0.45;` places Pip by direct assignment at level start; if the engine intro shows Pip elsewhere first he jumps. **Fix:** place Pip before the first rendered frame only (assert), or `pipWalk` him from the engine's start spot. ### cook — Cook a soup
Captures: `cook-L0`, `cook-L1` and their `-short` folders (teleport log present after the L2 re-capture); `cook-L2` (19:26–19:28) pre-dates `cook.js` (19:33), so L3-only items are marked re-verify; `cook-L2-short` is 16 failed-boot frames (ignored).

1. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `cook-L0/shot01`, `cook-L0-short/shot03`, `cook-L0-short/shot10` — the knife is held by nobody: it hovers and chops in mid-air while Bear stands with both arms tucked behind the counter; the blade is well above the veg while pieces split. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/cook.js` `makeFriend()` (arms at `rest ± carry`) + knife state (`this.knife`, `hoverY: 0.9`) — pose Bear's right arm onto the handle while `knife.state !== 'rest'` (or a paw mitt on the handle); drop the blade to the piece top on each chop.
2. **[P1]** L3 · `cook-L2/shot08`, `cook-L2/shot07` — the spoon is held by nobody: the ladle hangs in front of Bear's face covering nose and mouth, then stirs by itself with its handle across his ear. **Fix:** `cook.js` `ladle()` / `placeSpoon()` — path the ladle on the camera side of the pot (z +0.5); pose Bear's arm on the spoon. (re-verify: capture pre-dates code change)
3. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `cook-L0/shot07`, `cook-L0-short/shot07`, `cook-L2/shot03` — in the pot views the pot's right handle covers Bear's left eye and half his nose. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/cook-kitchen.js` `FRIEND_AT = (-1.55, 0, …)` — pot at x -3.15, handle reaches ≈ -2.1: stand Bear at x ≈ -0.6 for pot views (walk him there), or turn the pot 90°.
4. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `cook-L0/shot01`, `cook-L0-short/shot03`, `cook-L2/shot01` — in the board view the caption bubble covers Bear's right eye/cheek; ears and head top are cut by the frame top. **Fix:** `cook.js` `VIEWS.board = V(BOARD.x, TOP + 0.35, BOARD.z - 0.05, 4.9, 0.9)` — e.g. d 4.9 → 5.6, el 0.9 → 0.78, look y +0.3.
5. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `cook-L0-short/shot05`, `cook-L0-short/shot06`, `cook-L2/shot06` — the carried chopping board floats with nobody holding it and crosses Bear's face (forehead and left eye); at L3 a wall of tomato slices covers the window and half the scene. **Fix:** `cook.js` `CARRY_Y = POT_RIM + 0.62` and the carry path — carry lower and further forward (z +0.7), or have Bear hold the board's handle.
6. **[P1]** L3 · `cook-L2/shot04`, `cook-L2/shot01`, `cook-L2/shot08` — the recipe card covers the right-hand tomatoes in the veg bowl when the caption asks "Now two tomatoes!"; elsewhere it covers the bowl's contents (`shot01`, `shot02`, `shot05`) or the knife handle (`shot08`–`shot10`). **Fix:** `cook.js` `makeCard()` HUD — `hud.scale 0.3` → 0.24, pinned top-right clear of `VBOWL`; never over a drag source. (re-verify: capture pre-dates code change)
7. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `cook-L0/shot02`, `cook-L0-short/shot03`, `cook-L2/shot02` — chopped pieces interpenetrate in a heap, cut faces carry black scratchy hatch lines, pieces hang off the board's back/left edge, knife lies inside the pile; L3 worst: two tomatoes become ~10 full-diameter rounds with a pinwheel seed pattern, half a tomato left uncut. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/cook-slice.js` `inkMat()` / `makePiece()` — exclude cap triangles from the ink shell or add `polygonOffset`; `cook.js` after each cut — separate overlapping pieces, clamp to `BW`/`BD`; `app/src/lessons/cook-veg.js` tomato cap `bands` — seed chambers.
8. **[P1]** L2 · `cook-L1-short/shot10` — a broccoli slab stands upright straight through the knife blade. **Fix:** `cook.js` chop step — push pieces apart along the cut normal, never animate them through `S.knife`; lay the knife above the pile.
9. **[P1]** L1–L2 · `cook-L0-short/shot13`, `cook-L1/shot06` — a carrot chunk lies on the hob under the pot among the flames (missed or fell through); at L2 a floret tumbles outside the pot's left rim over the handle. **Fix:** `cook.js` tip/pour-in landing — clamp every landing point inside `POT_IN`; remove strays below `POT_RIM`.
10. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `cook-L0-short/shot01`, `cook-L1-short/shot15`, `cook-L2/shot04` — opening view: Bear is half outside the left frame edge with the Home button on his head/ear; Pip is a cropped yellow blob in the bottom-right corner. **Fix:** `cook.js` `VIEWS.bowl = V(1.75, …, 6.4, 0.68)` — look x 1.75 → 1.0, d → 7.4; `this.pip.position.set(5.6, 0, 2.0)` — fully in or out of frame.
11. **[P2]** L3 · `cook-L2/shot09`, `cook-L2/shot10` — the served soup is a flat orange disc with three red dots (code primitives), a stray ink squiggle at the bowl's left rim; the bowl stays on the board. **Fix:** `cook.js` `serve()` — `geo.circle` + `geo.sphere` bits → a domed soup surface with real mini pieces from `makePiece`; `prop_bowl` ink. 18. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `cook-L0-short/shot01`, `cook-L0-short/shot02`, `cook-L0/shot04` — the resting knife's handle end disappears under the veg bowl's rim. **Fix:** `cook-kitchen.js` `KNIFE_REST = (0.3, …, -0.62)` — x 0.3 → -0.2 (bowl at `VBOWL.x = 3.0`, `VBOWL_K = 1.45`).

## Building site world

### bricks — Brick letters
Captures: `bricks-L0`, `bricks-L1`, `bricks-L2` plus `bricks-L0-short`, `bricks-L1-short`, `bricks-L2-short` (all under `qa/`; crops in `crops/F1/` `b0-*`, `b0s-*`, `b1-*`, `b1s-*`, `b2-*`). Captures post-date the game code (shots 19:29–19:39, `bricks*.js` 19:07).

1. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `bricks-L0/shot07`, `bricks-L1/shot10`, `bricks-L2/shot11` — finale "Look what you made!" plays over an empty letter board: last letter already knocked off, all bricks (L2 14, L3 20) back on the stand; admire pause, photo and sticker show only the word picture. The game defines no `creationView()`, `creationObjects()` or `onFinale`. **Fix:** as AUDIT B1; plus `app/src/lessons/bricks.js` `afterSuccess()`/`tumble()` — no tumble after the last round (`if (this.round >= this.totalRounds - 1) return;`). conflicts with AUDIT B1: audit frames a mini-letter sign strip; raw frames the board (`creationView()` pos `(BX, 3.9, 11.5)`, look `(BX, 3.5, BZ)`; `creationObjects()` → `this.placedBricks`). ↔ AUDIT B1, X2
2. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `bricks-L0/shot04`, `bricks-L1/shot08`, `bricks-L2-short/shot11` — finished letter is pulled apart as its picture lands (D flying off during the duck's pop-out; Q breaking up while "Q is for Queen's crown!" is on screen); letter + picture are still together ~0.1 s (`success()` 1.6 s, `afterSuccess()` 1.0 s, picture show ends 2.5 s). **Fix:** as AUDIT B1 (`wait(1.0)` → `2.5`); plus `bricks.js` `complete()` — set `this.payoffP` to the `showPicture()` + `showWord()` promise before `this.success(...)`. ↔ AUDIT B1
3. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `bricks-L1/shot02`, `bricks-L2/shot10`, `bricks-L2-short/shot14` — count number is drawn on the bricks just placed: green "5" over I's red/yellow bricks, orange "12" on M, huge green "16" hides three bricks of T's stem, teal "18" covers R's leg as the letter finishes. **Fix:** `bricks.js` `click()` `countNum(top, n, col, last ? 12 : 9)` — one fixed spot beside the board (e.g. `(BX + 3.9, 5.6, faceZ + 0.5)`), size 8.
4. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `bricks-L0-short/shot01`, `bricks-L1/shot07`, `bricks-L2/shot04` — engine: green "Go!" badge sits on the board's top-left corner over the hazard stripe and first slots; on L2/L3 it pops up after 4–6 bricks are in and hides the top of the letter (top yellow brick in `bricks-L1/shot07`). **Fix:** `app/style.css` `#go-badge { top: 13vmin }` (engine `ui.js` `showGo`) — for this game place it left of the board (e.g. `left: 30%`), or raise `BOARD`/lower camera so 13–25vmin is clear.
5. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `bricks-L0/shot01`, `bricks-L1/shot01`, `bricks-L2/shot01` (every shot) — Pip stands directly in front of Bear: head tuft touches Bear's cheek, Bear's left arm and half his body hidden, his hard hat sits on the crane mast; on L3 the floodlight pole also lines up behind the hat and the light cone cuts across his face. **Fix:** `bricks.js` `BEAR` `(-7.1, 0, -2.1)` and `this.pip.position.set(-6.5, 0, 1.2)` — separate by ≥ 1.8 in x (e.g. Bear `(-5.4, 0, -2.4)`, Pip `(-7.4, 0, 1.6)`).
6. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `bricks-L0/shot03`, `bricks-L1/shot05`, `bricks-L2/shot07` — word picture pops out exactly in front of the scenery digger (L3 also the floodlight pole): cab behind the apple's leaf, between the cat's ears, behind the duck and grape stalk; fish rides on it; kite tail over the cab; digger shows through the moon; pole comes out of the rabbit's ear. **Fix:** `bricks.js` `PIC` `(7.55, 0, 0.3)` + `app/src/engine/scenery.js` (digger "at the back right", ~l.1949) — hide/move the digger for this game in `build()`, or put a plain sand heap behind `PIC`.
7. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `bricks-L0-short/shot03`, `bricks-L1/shot06`, `bricks-L2/shot03` — bricks morph in flight: a 2-stud brick leaves the stand and stretches to a 7-stud (B's spine), 4-stud (D, E) or 3-stud bar in mid-air, and shrinks on the way back. **Fix:** `bricks.js` `fly()`/`tumble()` `setBrickLength(...)` lerp — pre-size stand bricks to the next letter's slot lengths in `throwIn()`/`tumble()` so no brick changes length in view.
8. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `bricks-L0-short/shot14`, `bricks-L1-short/shot05`, `bricks-L2-short/shot14` — glow behind a finished letter shows a hard horizontal edge across the board about a third down (camera-facing `Sprite`, upper half tilts behind the board face). **Fix:** `bricks.js` `build()` `this.halo = glow(...)` — `PlaneGeometry` mesh parallel to the board at `faceZ + 0.01` (or `halo.material.depthTest = false; halo.renderOrder = 1`).
9. **[P2]** L2 · `bricks-L1/shot05`, `bricks-L1-short/shot11` — K: purple arm brick lies on the teal leg brick's corner and the pink brick (coplanar overlap). **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/bricks-letters.js` `LETTERS.K` third stroke start `[m[0] + c * 0.5, m[1] - c * 0.6]` → `c * 1.15` clear of the arm's axis (e.g. `[m[0] + c * 0.9, m[1] - c * 1.25]`).
10. **[P2]** L3 · `bricks-L2/shot05` — Q: tail brick is tucked under the ring's purple brick (coplanar overlap). **Fix:** `bricks-letters.js` `LETTERS.Q` `p0 = ... (rx + c * 0.6)` → `(rx + c * 1.15)`.
11. **[P2]** L1 · `bricks-L0-short/shot12` — "F is for Fish": blimp shape with ragged blue stripes, flat painted orange rectangle for a fin, stray ink tick under the belly, hovering in mid-air. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_fish.py` — sculpted side fin and tail, clean stripe bands; `bricks-words.js` `WORDS.F` — water puddle/bowl, or drop `float: 1.1`.
12. **[P2]** L2 · `bricks-L1-short/shot14` — "L is for Leaf": plain green teardrop on a stick hovering 0.9 above ground (reads as a lollipop tree), stray ink tick in the middle, scratchy marks at the tip. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_word_leaf.py` — real leaf silhouette with centre vein and curl; `bricks-words.js` `WORDS.L` — no dangling stalk while floating.
13. **[P2]** L1 · `bricks-L0/shot02`, `bricks-L0-short/shot04` — beach ball's panel seams are ragged, hairy ink strokes (worst between red and yellow panels). **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_word_ball.py` — panels as clean paint areas (or a separate `layer()` per panel).
14. **[P3]** L1, L3 · `bricks-L0-short/shot14`, `bricks-L0/shot06`, `bricks-L2-short/shot03` — count numbers that rise above the board: faint grey "7" lies across the hazard header and caption (L1); dark purple/green "13", "11" unreadable on the night sky (L3). **Fix:** as #3 (fixed spot beside the board / on the board frame, white outline).
15. **[P3]** L1–L3 · `bricks-L0/shot05`, `bricks-L1/shot07`, `bricks-L2/shot09` — stand bricks sit crooked (pink and teal tilted ~2–3°, one corner off the studs) balancing on their narrow edge; on L2/L3 the back row also hides the board's bottom rail (L3: and feet). **Fix:** `bricks.js` `buildStand()` `rz: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.08` → `0`.
16. **[P3]** L3 · `bricks-L2/shot02`, `bricks-L2-short/shot15` — tumble puts 9–18 bricks in the air at once in an overlapping cluster in front of the board. **Fix:** `bricks.js` `tumble()` stagger `Math.min(0.1, 1.2 / list.length)` → fixed `0.12`, hop in letter order.
17. **[P3]** L1 · `bricks-L0/shot01`, `bricks-L0-short/shot14` — bricks overlap at joints: A's crossbar ends tucked under both legs, yellow leg overlaps blue; G's red bar touches/overlaps the teal ring brick. **Fix:** `bricks-letters.js` `LETTERS.A` `inset = c / Math.sin(th) + 0.02` → `+ 0.08`; `LETTERS.G` bar start `W - 2 * c` → `W - 2 * c - 0.08`; `SEAM` 0.05 → 0.08.
18. **[P3]** L2–L3 · `bricks-L1/shot02`, `bricks-L1-short/shot05`, `bricks-L2/shot05` — on short ~1.0-long bricks two studs are squeezed in so their ink rings touch/merge (I's bars, Q's ring bricks). **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/bricks-pieces.js` `studsFor` `Math.round(L / PITCH)` → `Math.max(1, Math.floor(L / 0.56))`.
19. **[P3]** L2 · `bricks-L1/shot10` — nest is jammed against the right end of the stand; red and purple bricks overlap its twigs. **Fix:** `bricks.js` `PIC.x` 7.55 → 8.0, or `STAND.x1` 5.45 → 5.0.
20. **[P3]** L1 · `bricks-L0/shot04`, `bricks-L0-short/shot08` — "D is for Duck": the duck is a yellow bird nearly identical to Pip on the other side of the board. **Fix:** `bricks-words.js` `WORDS.D` — white duck / mallard variant.
21. **[P3]** L1 · `bricks-L0-short/shot10` — egg: open black ink arc around the highlight, speckles are jagged triangles. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_word_egg.py` — highlight as a sticker without outline, round speckles.
22. **[P3]** L3 · `bricks-L2/shot05` — crown band has ragged pale streaks. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_word_crown.py` — clean band highlight or none.
23. **[P3]** L1 · `bricks-L0/shot01` — apple leaf is a flat green slab with one stripe. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_word_apple.py` — curved leaf with a centre crease.

Teleports: L1 0 events; L2 0 events (no creature pictures); L3 0 events. Code audit: Pip and Bear placed once in `build()`, then only `hop()` in place. Intentional pop-ins: picture creatures (cat, duck, pig, rabbit) scale 0.01 → 1 on a 0.6 s arc from behind the board in `showPicture()`, leave with `popOut`.

### blocks — Block builder
Captures: `blocks-L0`, `blocks-L1`, `blocks-L2` plus `blocks-L0-short`, `blocks-L1-short`, `blocks-L2-short` (all under `qa/`; crops in `crops/F1/` `k0-*`, `k0s-*`, `k1-*`, `k1s-*`, `k2-*`, `k2s-*`). Captures post-date the game code (shots 19:41–19:51, `blocks.js` 15:13).

1. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `blocks-L0/shot10`, `blocks-L1-short/shot16`, `blocks-L2-short/shot09` — at the start of every round the new loose blocks show for ~3 s piled inside each other at the world origin (mat's front edge), half sunk and z-fighting, then vanish and re-appear from the toy box (also `blocks-L2-short/shot14` at "Which one stacks?"). **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/blocks.js` `newBlock()` — add `b.visible = false;` (blocks are created in `setupBuild()`/`setupStack()` ~3 s before `fromBox()` hides and places them).
2. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `blocks-L0/shot12`, `blocks-L1/shot14`, `blocks-L2/shot11` — the last build is toppled and swept into the toy box like every round, so finale admire/photo/sticker happen over an empty mat (L3: empty tray too). **Fix:** `blocks.js` `complete()` (`this.crashP = ... this.topple()`) and `afterSuccess()` (`this.sweep()`) — skip both on the last round; add `creationView()` → `this.buildCam(top, 0)`, `creationObjects()` → `this.placedB`. conflicts with AUDIT K1: audit replaces `crashP` with a child-driven knockdown and frames a keepsake shelf of minis, not the standing build. ↔ AUDIT K1, X2
3. **[P1]** L1 · `blocks-L0-short/shot12`, `blocks-L0/shot02` — on topple the red cone flies into Bear and sits wedged between his arm and body; the green cone ends against his paw. Bear has no collider and the impulse throws blocks back-left at him. **Fix:** `blocks.js` `build()` — add `physics.box(new T.Vector3(BEARP.x, 1.1, BEARP.z), new T.Vector3(1.6, 2.2, 1.4))` (and one for Pip); `topple()` impulse `z: -(0.8 + up * 0.9)` → `-(0.2 + up * 0.3)`.
4. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `blocks-L0/shot01`, `blocks-L1/shot12`, `blocks-L2/shot10` — Pip stands directly behind the toy box: only the head shows, cut at the beak by the rim; blocks hop out across Pip's face, spawn on it (block on the head like a hat, orange block over the eyes, arch on the head, ball on the face) and swept blocks fly through it. **Fix:** `blocks.js` `PIPP` `(6.6, 0, -3.75)` — clear of the box (e.g. `(3.6, 0, -4.3)`, between Bear and the hut).
5. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `blocks-L0/shot09`, `blocks-L1/shot02`, `blocks-L2-short/shot16` — caption bubble hides the payoff: rocket nose lifts behind it; big house's flag stick and tall tower's cone/flag run into it; "The cube stacks!" hides the placed cube's top edge and the flag is cut by the screen top; the ball is held behind "Oops! The ball rolls off!". **Fix:** `blocks.js` `buildCam()` `(H + 2.4) / 0.6 - 1.6` → `(H + 3.6) / 0.6 - 1.6` (room for the 0.7 lift / 0.95 flag), or lower `look.y`; stack round `buildCam(top + 1.2, ...)`.
6. **[P2]** L1 · `blocks-L0-short/shot11` — "copy it" is not copied: finished tower is yellow–blue–red, the model beside it blue–yellow–red; any cube is accepted in any cube slot. **Fix:** `blocks.js` `drop()` — choose the slot by colour too (`mine.find((s) => this.isOpen(s) && s.col === b.userData.col)`), or recolour the stand's mini (`this.minis[slot.i]`) to the block placed.
7. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `blocks-L0/shot01`, `blocks-L1/shot07`, `blocks-L2/shot01` — framing: the build fills ~28–30% of screen height, the 11-unit tray runs off both sides holding 1–3 blocks, the bottom ~17–20% is bare ground. **Fix:** `blocks.js` `buildCam()` minimum `D` 9.2 → ~7.6 for builds ≤ 3 tall; `blocks-pieces.js` `TRAY.len` 11.0 → sized to the level's widest row (L1: ~7).
8. **[P2]** L1–L2 · `blocks-L0/shot03`, `blocks-L1/shot10` — engine: "Go!" badge covers the top of the roof/house ghost (target outline). **Fix:** `app/style.css` `#go-badge` position (engine `ui.js` `showGo`) — keep it off the build column (e.g. over the tray).
9. **[P2]** L3 · `blocks-L2/shot03`, `blocks-L2-short/shot07` — "Which one rolls?": the ramp reads as a flat yellow sheet with two white lines (no side, thickness or slope), hides Bear's whole body, and the cube on it overlaps his cheek. **Fix:** `blocks.js` `RYAW` 0.2 → ~0.6; roll-round camera in `startRound()` `this.buildCam(2.6, 6.5)` → lower, side-on (e.g. pos `(MATP.x + 5, 3.0, 7)`, look `(MATP.x, 1.0, -1.2)`); `tools/blender/creatures/prop_blocks.py` `ramp()` — contrasting side faces, support posts.
10. **[P2]** L2–L3 · `blocks-L1/shot11`, `blocks-L2/shot02`, `blocks-L2-short/shot05` — the build stands in front of Bear and hides half his face: finished house while he cheers (L2), left tower of "a bridge with towers" (slot x = −1.5) with its cone (L3). **Fix:** `blocks.js` `BEARP` (see #21; e.g. `(-2.9, 0, -3.2)`), or shift `hometree` slots +0.4 in x.
11. **[P2]** L3 · `blocks-L2-short/shot10` — with six blocks the row is as long as the tray, so end blocks clip the end rims (teal cube's corner in the left rim, purple roof through the right rim). **Fix:** `blocks.js` `traySpots()` — shrink `GAP` so `tot <= TRAY.len - 0.6` (or scale `KT` down for 6 blocks).
12. **[P2]** L2 · `blocks-L1/shot02` — in the "xylophone run" a block hops before the block resting on it starts, so it rises into the one above (yellow cube pushed into the grey-blue block, no longer level with the orange cube). **Fix:** `blocks.js` `complete()` — hop from the top down, or add each block's offset to all blocks above it (`moveFixed` on the stack above).
13. **[P2]** L2 · `blocks-L1-short/shot04` — when a block lands its mini twin on the stand hops 0.25 (62% of a mini's height) through the mini above: grey-blue mini pushed through the red roof (z-fighting stripes). **Fix:** `blocks.js` `place()` mini hop `Math.sin(q * Math.PI) * 0.25` → `0.07`, or lift every mini above it too.
14. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `blocks-L0/shot05`, `blocks-L1/shot04`, `blocks-L2/shot01` — cone block has a pinched knob at its tip (reads as a bottle teat) and a broken outline along its base. **Fix:** `prop_blocks.py` `cone()` — profile jumps from r 0.06 at `H - tip` to a second cap at `H - tip + 0.06`; end the slope at r 0.10 and close with one hemisphere of r 0.10.
15. **[P2]** L2 · `blocks-L1/shot02` — ball block shows straight-edged shading patches (facets) across its face. **Fix:** `prop_blocks.py` `ball()` — smooth normals (the block builder hardens normals per face).
16. **[P3]** L1 · `blocks-L0/shot04`, `blocks-L0-short/shot05` — each round the camera cuts to a close-up of the empty stand for ~1 s ("Now a house!" over a bare table) before the model pops in. **Fix:** `blocks.js` `startRound()` — call `showMini` before/while the camera glides (move `setupBuild()` ahead of `await this.glide(CAMS.model, ...)`).
17. **[P3]** L2 · `blocks-L1-short/shot03` — after a wrong drop the caption says "Find the roof shape!" while the hint hand and a large halo point at the orange cube; the halo ring covers the two neighbouring blocks. **Fix:** `blocks.js` `drop()` — for a block whose slot is not open yet use `redirect('Bottom first!')`; `showHint()` `addHalo(pr.b, 0.9)` → `0.6`. conflicts with AUDIT K4: audit replaces "Bottom first!" with "First the <shape>!" (L2–L3) and K5 rewrites "Find the X shape!". ↔ AUDIT K4, K5
18. **[P3]** L3 · `blocks-L2/shot07`, `blocks-L2-short/shot15` — hint halo is a very large double ring (white ring + orange glow) covering the mat front, the tower's base and the tray. **Fix:** `blocks.js` `makeErrorless()` `this.addHalo(t, 0.9)` → `0.55`.
19. **[P3]** L2 · `blocks-L1-short/shot05` — as the big house topples the flag hangs in mid-air beside the falling roof for a moment. **Fix:** `blocks.js` `topple()` — pop the flag out before the impulse (move the `this.flag` block above `ksfx.crash`).
20. **[P3]** L3 · `blocks-L2/shot04`, `blocks-L2/shot07`, `blocks-L2-short/shot14` — a glowing fence lamp sits exactly behind the model on the stand and reads as an orange ball on top of the mini tower. **Fix:** `blocks.js` `STANDP`/`CAMS.model` — shift the stand 0.5 in x, or hide that lamp post.
21. **[P3]** L1 · `blocks-L0/shot04`, `blocks-L0/shot06`, `blocks-L0/shot08` — in the model close-up Bear stands half behind the stand; the table rim hides his hip and one leg. **Fix:** `blocks.js` `BEARP` `(-2.3, 0, -3.95)` → `(-1.7, 0, -3.95)` (one `BEARP` must also satisfy #10).
22. **[P3]** L2–L3 · `blocks-L1/shot07`, `blocks-L1-short/shot16`, `blocks-L2-short/shot08` — tray: in the L2 look its floor is the same colour as the sand, so it reads as an empty green frame on the ground; ragged dashes / jagged teal "teeth" show under the front rim (rim and floor edge fighting). **Fix:** `prop_blocks.py` `tray()` — clearly lighter wood floor (or `blocks-pieces.js` `makeTray` tint); close the gap under the rim boxes (`F`/`Hr` overlap).
23. **[P3]** L3 · `blocks-L2-short/shot05` — reward flag is a code primitive (plain cylinder stick, sphere knob, flat double-sided triangle) on a sculpted set, and red on a red cone. **Fix:** `blocks-pieces.js` `makeFlag()` — Blender prop with a waving cloth; colour different from the top block.
24. **[P3]** L1–L2 · `blocks-L0/shot01`, `blocks-L0-short/shot04`, `blocks-L1/shot06` — cylinder block shows vertical crease/facet lines on its round side (clear on the blue rocket cylinders). **Fix:** `prop_blocks.py` `cyl()` — smooth normals on the side wall, hard edges only at the rims.

Teleports: L1 0 events; L2 0 events; L3 0 events. Code audit: Pip and Bear positioned once in `build()`, then only `hop()` in place. The monitor tracks creatures only; the blocks' own snap origin → toy box is P1 #1.

### balance — Fill & balance
Captures: `balance-L0`, `balance-L1`, `balance-L2` (crops `b0_*`, `n1_*`, `b2_*`). `balance-L1` was re-captured at 3 s interval: 11 in-game frames, full level covered.

1. **[P1]** L1–L3 · `balance-L1/shot03`, `balance-L2/shot01`, `balance-L0/shot02` — Pip stands right behind the plank's right end, so the plank is always drawn across him: raised end over his left eye (half the eye hidden under the red tip) or resting on his brow / head tuft; level plank's red tip cuts across his belly and wing. **Fix:** `app/src/lessons/balance.js` `PIP = (7.35, 0, -2.3)` — move clear of the plank's swept arc on screen (e.g. `(8.5, 0, 0.4)`, `rotation.y = -0.9`), or shift `CAMS.main.look.x` 2.2 → 2.7; re-check both tilt extremes.
2. **[P1]** L2–L3 · `balance-L2/shot02`, `balance-L2/shot06`, `balance-L1/shot02` — pouring into the raised pail puts the scoop behind the caption bubble and out of the top of the frame; the jug is cut by the top edge beside the Home button (L2: scoop handle cut by the frame top). **Fix:** `balance.js` `autoPour()`/`pourStep()` — `pail.mouth().add(V(-1.0, 0.9, 0))` → y +0.55; `CAMS.main` → pos `(2.2, 4.5, 10.0)`, look y 1.15 → 1.5.
3. **[P1]** L1 · `balance-L0/shot04`, `balance-L0/shot06` (through `shot13`) — the first sand castle is put down directly in front of the see-saw's pivot: hides the red pivot brick, its flag sits on the plank's level bubble for the rest of the level. **Fix:** `balance.js` `SPOTS` — `SPOTS[0] = (2.45, 0, 2.0)` is 0.25 from `SS.x = 2.2`; keep x in [1.0, 3.4] free, e.g. `[(-1.05,0,2.3), (-2.9,0,2.4), (5.6,0,2.6), (0.4,0,3.0)]`.
4. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `balance-L0/shot01`, `balance-L1/shot05`, `balance-L2/shot05` — what sits on the right seat overlaps Pip: weight Chick's wing over Pip's cheek (two near-identical yellow chicks read as one two-headed blob); on L2/L3 the right pail's handle and rim cross his wing and belly. **Fix:** as #1 (`PIP` move); and/or `balance.js` `build()` `makeChick(0.8)` — a different-coloured weight (duckling/another animal).
5. **[P2]** L3 · `balance-L2/shot03`, `balance-L2/shot07`, `balance-L2/shot11` — the "which holds more" pails stand so close (`FRONT` z 1.75) that the big one overlaps the plank's front edge, the seated right pail's base and Pip's feet. **Fix:** `balance.js` `FRONT` — z 1.75 → 2.7, x `(0.2, 4.2)`; scale camera accordingly.
6. **[P2]** L3 · `balance-L2/shot07`, `balance-L2/shot11` — a candidate pail has the same colour as a pail on the see-saw (teal + teal; orange + orange): four pails, two alike, so "which one" is unclear. **Fix:** `balance.js` `PLANS[2]` `cands` — colours not on the plank (e.g. `[PURPLE, …], [YELLOW, …]` and `[RED, …], [PINK, …]`).
7. **[P2]** L1 · `balance-L0/shot11`, `balance-L0/shot13` — the second castle (`SPOTS[1] = (5.35, 0, 2.0)`) stands in front of the plank's right end, Chick's feet and Pip; its flag crosses Pip's belly. **Fix:** as #3 (`SPOTS`).
8. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `balance-L0/shot01`, `balance-L1/shot02`, `balance-L2/shot01` — parked jug is ~60% outside the left frame edge (a cropped blue blob); sand heap and scoop are cut by the same edge; the water tub is never in frame (L1). **Fix:** `balance.js` `TUB = (-4.3, 0, 1.2)` / `HEAP = (-5.2, 0, -2.4)` — bring to x ≥ -3.0, or pull `CAMS.main` back (pos z 9.0 → 10.2).
9. **[P2]** L1–L2 · `balance-L0/shot06`, `balance-L1/shot03`, `balance-L1/shot05` — the pourer set down beside the pail overlaps things: jug lands between plank and castle (spout in the battlement, body over the plank's front edge); beside a lowered pail the scoop's cup presses into the rim and the jug covers the plank's right tip and the pail's base. **Fix:** `balance.js` `READY_Z = 1.0` → ~1.4–1.5; `bringPourer()` `x = … - side * 1.55` → `side * 2.1` (the pail swings outward and down when its pan drops); test against every `SPOTS` entry.
10. **[P2]** L2 · `balance-L1/shot04` — the jug hangs at the raised right pan, overlapping the pail's left third, handle and the plank edge; reads as standing on the tilted plank. **Fix:** `balance.js` `goHome()`/`autoPour()` arc control point `c` — keep the path in front of the plank (z ≥ `SS.z + 1.6`) until clear of the pail; never hold beside a raised pail.
11. **[P2]** L1–L2 · `balance-L0/shot10`, `balance-L1/shot07` — the tipped-out pail flies as a huge upside-down bucket (≈35% of frame height) in front of Chick, covering his feet and the plank end (L1); hangs in front of the pivot and level bubble (L2). **Fix:** `balance.js` tip-out anim (`g.position.lerpVectors(...)` + `Math.sin(q*PI)*1.4` arcs near lines 534–608) — route the arc over the empty left/centre foreground, not across the right seat.
12. **[P2]** L2 · `balance-L1/shot02` — during "Fill this bucket half way!" the thick near-opaque halo ring sits over the whole pail and washes out the half mark and fill level. **Fix:** `balance.js` target-pail halo — thin ring at 1.25× the pail radius, opacity ≤ 0.4, off while pouring.
13. **[P2]** L3 · `balance-L2/shot07` — engine: green "Go!" button overlaps the bottom border of the caption bubble and covers the hut (same defect in cook). **Fix:** engine Go! badge (`app/src/engine/lesson.js` ~line 204–209, styled in `app/style.css`) — 12 px gap below the caption.
14. **[P2]** L1–L3 · `balance-L0/shot01`, `balance-L1/shot01`, `balance-L2/shot03` — pail model: ragged white blotch on the floor (every empty pail), rim ring looks doubled / off-register against the white band, glass wall so faint that the dashed half-mark, star and handle float in air; at 1.25× (L3) also scratchy white zig-zag streaks inside the rim. **Fix:** `tools/blender/creatures/prop_balance.py` `pail()` — `floor` layer `target_faces=300` → ~900, clean bevelled disc, rim `ring` concentric with the white band; `app/src/lessons/balance-props.js` `glassMat()` — raise wall opacity / tinted fresnel edge.
15. **[P2]** L2 · `balance-L1/shot07` (through `shot11`) — the half-height castle is the full castle squashed (`makeCastle(sx, sy)` scales the whole model): battlements flattened to bumps, flag pole halved, stray ink on top; reads as a bundt cake. **Fix:** `balance-props.js` `makeCastle()` — scale only the body in y, keep battlements and flag unscaled (or build a `prop_bal_castle_half`).
16. **[P2]** L1 · `balance-L0/shot06`, `balance-L0/shot11` (`shot04`–`shot13`) — castle model: stray black ink strokes between the battlements, broken jagged outline down the left silhouette, ragged-edged door paint. **Fix:** `prop_balance.py` `castle()` — `target_faces=2200` → ~3500, door as a separate `layer()` decal, battlement boxes welded with a fillet so the ink shell does not self-intersect.
17. **[P2]** L2 · `balance-L1/shot01` — water tub model: the water surface is an angular flat polygon with hard corners that does not follow the round tub, covered in scribbly white marks; the tub is cut by the left frame edge. **Fix:** `prop_balance.py` `tub()` `water` layer — round disc matching the inner wall with two or three clean ripple decals; `balance.js` `TUB` x -4.3 → -3.4.
18. **[P3]** L1 · `balance-L0/shot03` — the tip-out halo is a thick near-opaque double ring ~300 px wide covering the pivot and the plank's centre. **Fix:** `balance.js` halo call for the `empty` task — radius ×0.6, opacity ≤ 0.5.
19. **[P3]** L3 · `balance-L2/shot12`, `balance-L2/shot13` — finale caption "Look what you made!" but L3 makes nothing (no castles; just two pails). **Fix:** as AUDIT F4 (L3 tip-outs then leave castles, plus `creationView()`); otherwise `balance.js` level-3 end line — "It balances!" / engine finale text per lesson. ↔ AUDIT F4, X2

Teleports: L1 0 events; L2 0 events; L3 0 events. Code audit (`balance.js`): `pip.position.copy(PIP)` only in `build()`; Chick is parented to the seat; no mid-game direct position assignment. Unnumbered engine notes from the raw logs (no severity or fix given): sticker badge covers the pail in `balance-L0/shot13` and the level bubble in `balance-L2/shot14`; confetti + sticker in `balance-L1/shot11` are tidy.

# Part 3 — Models to rebuild

Two lists. **A** is every prop or character that failed in the games themselves, by world, including props built from code primitives that have no model file. **B** is the catalogue review of all 392 model files. A model can appear in both; A says how it fails in play, B how the file looks on its own.

### A. Failed in game

| `prop_m_beachball` | `tools/blender/creatures/prop_meadow_toys.py` `beachball` | sizes, colours | Torn-edged panels, ink scratches on seams, splatter highlight like a stain | Clean lune panels meeting at a button, one rounded shine |

### B. Catalogue review of all 392 model files

Method: every catalogue image m00–m65 was opened at full size; then every image was cut into two 640×200 half-bands (three models each) and enlarged to 1600×500 (`crops/M/mNNL.jpg`, `crops/M/mNNR.jpg`); 110 of those 132 enlargements were read, covering every model listed under Rebuild, Touch up and Broken. Models judged only from the full-size image are all in Passed.

Reading notes:
- Models that render **white** in the catalogue are tint-at-runtime props (`argMode 'tint'`, or scenery layers tinted by `scenery.js`). They were judged on shape, outline and stray ink only, never on colour. Where the white makes a model unreadable in the catalogue (shop, stall, palm, window), that is said in the row.
- Relative size was ignored (everything is normalised to one box).
- Three recurring paint defects account for most Touch-up rows and should be fixed at the pipeline level as well as per model: (1) **sawtooth / triangle-shard paint** where a thin stripe or texture crosses coarse triangles (doors, chimney, cone, trough, ramps, umbrella stand); (2) **scribbled ink loops** where an embossed dot, star or highlight is outlined (shape pieces, starfish, block, cups, fruit highlights); (3) **dotted or hair-thin outlines** on thin white parts (plates, cake stand, hat box, pedestal, drape, umbrella).
- The parent's "carrots" complaint: `prop_carrot` as rendered here is now acceptable (chunky root, leafy top, face) and is in Passed. The "terrible towel" is most likely `prop_towel` or `prop_md_towel`; both are in Rebuild, and `prop_k_towel` is in Touch up.

#### Broken or empty renders

| kind | source script + function | what is wrong (as seen) | what a premium version looks like |
|---|---|---|---|
| `prop_sunflower` | `prop_sunflower.py` (module level) | Renders upside-down in the catalogue: flower head on the ground with the stem pointing up. `scenery.js` assembles the head and `stem` layer itself, so this may be a layer-origin convention rather than a bad mesh, but any direct use of the kind shows it like this. | Stem below the head in the model's own rest layout so the kind is correct wherever it is loaded. |
